Tokenisation via Convex Relaxations
Authors: Jan Tempus, Philip Whittington, Craig W. Schmidt, Dennis Komm, Tiago Pimentel
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tokenisation is an integral part of the current NLP pipeline. Current tokenisation algorithms such as BPE and Unigram are greedy algorithms -- they make locally optimal decisions without considering the resulting vocabulary as a whole. We instead formulate tokeniser construction as a linear program and solve it using convex optimisation tools, yielding a new algorithm we call ConvexTok. We find ConvexTok consistently improves intrinsic tokenisation metrics and the bits-per-byte (BpB) achieved by language models; it also improves downstream task performance, but less consistently. Furthermore, ConvexTok allows the user to certify how far their tokeniser is from optimal, with respect to a certain objective, via a lower bound, and we empirically find it to be within 1\% of optimal at common vocabulary sizes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过凸松弛进行分词
分词是当前自然语言处理流水线的重要组成部分。当前分词算法如BPE和Unigram是贪心算法——它们仅做局部最优决策，而不考虑最终词汇表的整体效果。我们转而将分词器构建形式化为线性规划问题，并利用凸优化工具求解，从而提出一种新算法，称为ConvexTok。我们发现，ConvexTok在分词的内在指标和语言模型实现的每字位数（BpB）上持续表现更优；它也提升了下游任务性能，但效果不一致。此外，ConvexTok允许用户通过下界认证其分词器相对于特定目标的最优程度，实证表明在常见词汇规模下，其性能接近最优值的1%以内。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of greedy tokenisation methods like BPE and Unigram, which optimize locally without considering global vocabulary quality, the authors reformulate tokeniser construction as a linear program solvable via convex optimisation, introducing ConvexTok. Experiments show ConvexTok consistently outperforms existing methods on intrinsic tokenisation metrics and bits-per-byte in language models, with modest but measurable gains in downstream tasks. Additionally, ConvexTok provides a certified lower bound on optimality, empirically within 1% of the theoretical optimum for typical vocabulary sizes.
鉴于BPE和Unigram等贪心式分词方法缺乏全局优化，本文将分词器构建建模为线性规划问题，利用凸优化工具提出ConvexTok算法。实验表明，ConvexTok在分词内在指标和语言模型的bits-per-byte上持续优于现有方法，对下游任务性能提升效果不一但常为正向。此外，ConvexTok可提供关于分词器相对于特定目标的最优性下界，实验证明其在常见词汇规模下距离最优解不超过1%。
Which Way Did It Move? Diagnosing and Overcoming Directional Motion Blindness in Video-LLMs
Authors: Jongseo Lee, Hyuntak Lee, Sunghun Kim, Sooa Kim, Jihoon Chung, Jinwoo Choi
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:56+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 59 pages, including appendix. Code: https://github.com/KHU-VLL/DeltaDirect
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have made rapid progress on temporal video understanding, yet many fail at a basic perceptual primitive: signed image-plane motion direction. On simple videos of a single object moving left, right, up, or down, most Video-LLMs perform near chance, with above-chance cases largely attributable to prediction biases rather than genuine direction understanding. We call this failure directional motion blindness. We localize the failure by tracing motion direction information through the Video-LLM pipeline. Motion direction remains linearly accessible from the vision encoder, projector, and LLM hidden states, but the readout fails to bind this signal to the correct verbal answer option, revealing a direction binding gap. Although synthetic motion direction instruction tuning reduces this gap on the source domain, motion direction concept vector analysis shows that visual complexity weakens the signal magnitude and limits out-of-domain generalization. We introduce MoDirect, a dataset family for motion direction instruction tuning and evaluation, and DeltaDirect, a diagnosis-driven, projector-level objective that predicts normalized 2-D motion vectors from adjacent-frame feature deltas. On MoDirect-SynBench, instruction tuning with DeltaDirect improves motion direction accuracy from 25.9% to 85.4%. On MoDirect-RealBench, DeltaDirect improves real-world motion direction accuracy by 21.9 points over the vanilla baseline without real-world tuning data, while preserving standard video-understanding performance. Code: https://github.com/KHU-VLL/DeltaDirect
中文标题/摘要
标题：它向哪个方向移动了？诊断并克服视频大语言模型中的方向运动盲
视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）在时序视频理解方面取得了快速进展，但许多模型在基本的感知原语——带符号的图像平面运动方向上表现失败。在单个物体向左、右、上或下移动的简单视频中，大多数Video-LLMs的表现接近随机水平，高于随机水平的情况主要归因于预测偏差而非真正的方向理解。我们将这种失败称为方向运动盲。我们通过追踪运动方向信息在Video-LLM管道中的传递来定位失败原因。运动方向信息在视觉编码器、投影器和LLM隐藏状态中仍保持线性可访问性，但读出层未能将该信号与正确的口头答案选项绑定，揭示了方向绑定的缺口。尽管合成运动方向指令微调能在源域中减小该缺口，但运动方向概念向量分析表明，视觉复杂性会削弱信号幅度并限制跨域泛化能力。我们引入MoDirect，一个用于运动方向指令微调与评估的数据集家族，并提出DeltaDirect，一种诊断驱动、投影器级别的目标函数，可从相邻帧特征差值预测归一化的二维运动向量。在MoDirect-SynBench上，使用DeltaDirect进行指令微调可将运动方向准确率从25.9%提升至85.4%。在MoDirect-RealBench上，DeltaDirect在无需真实世界微调数据的情况下，将真实世界运动方向准确率提升21.9个百分点，同时保持标准视频理解性能。代码：https://github.com/KHU-VLL/DeltaDirect
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the failure of Video-LLMs to correctly perceive signed motion direction in videos, a basic perceptual task termed 'directional motion blindness'. The authors trace motion direction signals through the model pipeline and identify a 'direction binding gap' where visual motion features are preserved in early layers but fail to be correctly mapped to verbal output. To diagnose and fix this, they introduce DeltaDirect, a projector-level objective that predicts normalized 2D motion vectors from frame differences, and MoDirect, a new dataset family for motion direction instruction tuning. Experiments show DeltaDirect boosts motion direction accuracy from 25.9% to 85.4% on synthetic benchmarks and improves real-world performance by 21.9 points without requiring real-world tuning data, while maintaining general video understanding.
该论文指出视频大语言模型在判断物体运动方向（左、右、上、下）时存在严重缺陷，称为‘方向运动盲’。作者通过分析模型内部信号发现，运动方向信息在视觉编码器和投影器中仍可线性获取，但未能正确绑定至语言输出，形成‘方向绑定缺口’。为此，他们提出DeltaDirect方法，通过相邻帧特征差值预测归一化二维运动向量，并构建MoDirect数据集用于指令调优。实验表明，DeltaDirect在合成数据上将方向识别准确率从25.9%提升至85.4%，在真实世界数据上提升21.9个百分点，且未影响模型的常规视频理解能力。
Integrable Elasticity via Neural Demand Potentials
Authors: Carlos Heredia, Daniel Roncel
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:47+00:00
Comments: 44 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose the Integrable Context-Dependent Demand Network (ICDN), a demand-first neural model for multiproduct retail demand. The model learns log-demand as a smooth, context-conditioned function of log-prices, allowing elasticities to be derived exactly from the learned demand surface. On the Dominick's beer dataset, ICDN improves out-of-sample generalization over a directed log-log benchmark and yields more stable, economically plausible elasticity estimates, especially for weakly identified cross-price effects.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过神经需求势实现可积弹性
我们提出了可积上下文依赖需求网络（ICDN），这是一种以需求为导向的神经模型，用于多产品零售需求预测。该模型将对数需求学习为对数价格的平滑、上下文条件函数，从而能从所学需求曲面上精确推导出弹性。在Dominick's啤酒数据集上，ICDN在样本外泛化能力上优于定向对数-对数基准模型，并产生更稳定、经济上更合理的弹性估计，尤其对交叉价格效应的弱识别情况效果显著。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate and stable elasticity estimation in retail demand modeling, the authors introduce the Integrable Context-Dependent Demand Network (ICDN), a neural model that directly learns log-demand as a smooth function of log-prices conditioned on contextual features. This formulation enables exact derivation of elasticities from the learned demand surface, avoiding approximation errors common in traditional methods. Experiments on the Dominick's beer dataset show that ICDN outperforms a directed log-log benchmark in out-of-sample generalization and produces more stable, economically plausible elasticity estimates, particularly for cross-price effects that are otherwise weakly identified.
为解决多产品零售需求建模中弹性估计不准确和不稳定的问题，作者提出了可积上下文依赖需求网络（ICDN），该模型将需求对数建模为价格对数的平滑函数，并引入上下文条件。模型设计确保弹性可从学习到的需求曲面精确推导，避免近似误差。在Dominick's啤酒数据集上的实验表明，ICDN在样本外泛化能力上优于定向对数-对数基准模型，并能产生更稳定、符合经济现实的弹性估计，尤其在交叉价格弹性较弱的情况下表现突出。
Cambrian-P: Pose-Grounded Video Understanding
Authors: Jihan Yang, Zifan Zhao, Xichen Pan, Shusheng Yang, Junyi Zhang, Bingyi Kang, Hu Xu, Saining Xie
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:45+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://cambrian-mllm.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Camera pose matters. The position and orientation of each viewpoint define a shared spatial coordinate frame that relates observations across video frames. Yet this signal is largely absent from multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) for video understanding, which process frames as isolated 2D snapshots, instead of the persistent scene humans perceive. We revisit pose as a lightweight supervisory signal and introduce Cambrian-P, a video MLLM augmented with per-frame learnable camera tokens and a pose regression head. With a carefully designed sampling scheme, the model achieves substantial gains of 4.5-6.5% on spatial reasoning benchmarks such as VSI-Bench, generalizes across eight additional spatial and general video QA benchmarks, and, as a byproduct, achieves state of the art streaming pose estimation on ScanNet. Surprisingly, training on pseudo-annotated poses from in-the-wild video further improves general video QA benchmarks, showing pose helps beyond spatial reasoning. Together, these results position camera pose as a fundamental signal for video models that reason about the physical world.
中文标题/摘要
标题：寒武纪-P：基于姿态的视频理解
摄像机姿态至关重要。每个视角的位置和朝向定义了一个共享的空间坐标系，关联视频帧间的观测。然而，当前用于视频理解的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）大多将帧视为孤立的2D快照，而非人类感知的持续场景。我们重新审视姿态作为轻量级监督信号，并引入寒武纪-P，一种通过每帧可学习的摄像机标记和姿态回归头增强的视频MLLM。通过精心设计的采样方案，该模型在空间推理基准（如VSI-Bench）上提升4.5-6.5%，在八个额外的空间与通用视频问答基准上实现泛化，同时作为副产品，在ScanNet上达到最先进的流式姿态估计性能。令人惊讶的是，使用野外视频的伪标注姿态进行训练，进一步提升了通用视频问答基准表现，表明姿态的作用不仅限于空间推理。综合来看，这些结果将摄像机姿态定位为视频模型理解物理世界时的基本信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the absence of camera pose information in multimodal LLMs for video understanding, which treats frames as isolated 2D snapshots, the authors introduce Cambrian-P, a video MLLM enhanced with per-frame learnable camera tokens and a pose regression head. Through a carefully designed sampling scheme, the model achieves 4.5–6.5% performance gains on spatial reasoning benchmarks like VSI-Bench, generalizes across eight additional video QA benchmarks, and incidentally sets a new state-of-the-art in streaming pose estimation on ScanNet. Training with pseudo-annotated poses from real-world videos further boosts general video QA performance, demonstrating that pose signals benefit reasoning beyond spatial tasks.
鉴于当前多模态大语言模型在视频理解中缺乏相机位姿信息，导致其将视频帧视为孤立的2D图像，作者提出了Cambrian-P，该模型通过引入每帧可学习的相机标记和位姿回归头进行增强。采用精心设计的采样方案训练后，模型在VSI-Bench等空间推理基准上提升4.5-6.5%，并在八个额外的空间与通用视频问答基准上实现泛化，同时意外地在ScanNet上达到流式位姿估计的最先进水平。更令人意外的是，使用野外视频的伪标注位姿进行训练还能进一步提升通用视频问答性能，表明位姿信号对推理任务具有超越空间推理的广泛价值。
MotiMotion: Motion-Controlled Video Generation with Visual Reasoning
Authors: Lee Hsin-Ying, Hanwen Jiang, Yiqun Mei, Jing Shi, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Zhixin Shu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:36+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. Project page: https://motimotion.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Current motion-controlled image-to-video generation models rigidly follow user-provided trajectories that are often sparse, imprecise, and causally incomplete. Such reliance often yields unnatural or implausible outcomes, especially by missing secondary causal consequences. To address this, we introduce MotiMotion, a novel framework that reformulates motion control as a reasoning-then-generation problem. To encourage causally grounded and commonsense-consistent interactions, we leverage a training-free vision-language reasoner to refine image-space coordinates of primary trajectories and to hallucinate plausible secondary motions. To further improve motion naturalness, we propose a confidence-aware control scheme that modulates guidance strength, enabling the model to closely follow high-confidence plans while correcting artifacts under low-confidence inputs with its internal generative priors. To support systematic evaluation, we curate a new image-to-video benchmark, MotiBench, consisting of interaction-centric scenes where new events are triggered by motion. Both VLM-based evaluation and a human study on MotiBench demonstrate that MotiMotion produces videos with more plausible object behaviors and interaction, and is preferred over existing approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MotiMotion：基于视觉推理的运动控制视频生成
当前的运动控制图像到视频生成模型严格遵循用户提供的轨迹，这些轨迹往往稀疏、不精确且因果不完整。这种依赖常导致不自然或不可信的结果，尤其因忽略次级因果后果。为解决此问题，我们提出MotiMotion框架，将运动控制重构为推理后生成的问题。为鼓励因果基础且符合常识的交互，我们利用无训练视觉-语言推理器优化主轨迹的图像空间坐标，并生成合理的次级运动。为进一步提升运动自然性，我们提出一种置信度感知控制方案，调节引导强度，使模型在高置信度输入下紧密跟随计划，而在低置信度输入下利用内部生成先验修正伪影。为支持系统性评估，我们构建了新的图像到视频基准MotiBench，包含由运动触发新事件的交互中心场景。基于VLM的评估和MotiBench上的人类研究表明，MotiMotion生成的视频具有更合理的物体行为与交互，且优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
MotiMotion addresses the limitation of current motion-controlled video generation models that rigidly follow sparse and causally incomplete user trajectories, often resulting in unnatural outcomes. The method redefines motion control as a reasoning-then-generation task, using a training-free vision-language model to refine primary motion paths and hallucinate plausible secondary motions grounded in commonsense causality. It further enhances motion naturalness via a confidence-aware control mechanism that adjusts guidance strength based on trajectory reliability. Evaluated on a new benchmark, MotiBench, both automated VLM assessments and human studies confirm that MotiMotion generates videos with more plausible object interactions and is preferred over existing methods.
MotiMotion 解决了当前运动控制视频生成模型僵化遵循稀疏、不精确且因果不完整用户轨迹的问题，常导致因缺失次级因果效应而产生不自然的结果。该框架将运动控制重构为“推理后生成”任务，利用无训练视觉-语言模型优化主运动坐标并生成符合常识的次级运动。引入置信度感知控制机制，根据轨迹置信度动态调节引导强度，高置信度时紧密跟随规划，低置信度时利用生成先验修正伪影。在新构建的交互中心场景基准 MotiBench 上，通过视觉-语言模型评估和人工测试均表明，MotiMotion 生成的视频在物体行为与交互合理性上更优，优于现有方法。
Vector Policy Optimization: Training for Diversity Improves Test-Time Search
Authors: Ryan Bahlous-Boldi, Isha Puri, Idan Shenfeld, Akarsh Kumar, Mehul Damani, Sebastian Risi, Omar Khattab, Zhang-Wei Hong, Pulkit Agrawal
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:26+00:00
Comments: 24 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models must now generalize out of the box to novel environments and work inside inference-scaling search procedures, such as AlphaEvolve, that select rollouts with a variety of task-specific reward functions. Unfortunately, the standard paradigm of LLM post-training optimizes a pre-specified scalar reward, often leading current LLMs to produce low-entropy response distributions and thus to struggle at displaying the diversity that inference-time search will require. We propose Vector Policy Optimization (VPO), an RL algorithm that explicitly trains policies to anticipate diverse downstream reward functions and to produce diverse solutions. VPO exploits that rewards are often vector-valued in practice, like per-test-case correctness in code generation or, say, multiple different user personas or reward models. VPO is essentially a drop-in replacement for the GRPO advantage estimator, but it trains the LLM to output a set of solutions where individual solutions specialize to different trade-offs in the vector reward space. Across four tasks, VPO matches or beats the strongest scalar RL baselines on test-time search (e.g. pass@k and best@k), with the gap widening as the search budget grows. For evolutionary search, VPO models unlock problems that GRPO models cannot solve at all. As test-time search becomes more standardized, optimizing for diversity may need to become the default post-training objective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：向量策略优化：训练多样性提升测试时搜索性能
语言模型如今必须在未经训练的情况下泛化到新环境，并在推理扩展搜索过程中（如AlphaEvolve）工作，该过程通过多种任务特定奖励函数选择 rollout。不幸的是，标准的LLM后训练范式优化预设的标量奖励，常导致当前LLM生成低熵响应分布，从而难以在推理时搜索中展示所需多样性。我们提出向量策略优化（VPO），一种强化学习算法，明确训练策略以预测多样化的下游奖励函数并生成多样化的解决方案。VPO利用实践中奖励常为向量值的事实，例如代码生成中的每个测试用例正确性，或多个不同用户角色或奖励模型。VPO本质上是GRPO优势估计器的即插即用替代方案，但训练LLM输出一组解决方案，其中每个解决方案在向量奖励空间中专门针对不同权衡。在四个任务中，VPO在测试时搜索（如pass@k和best@k）上匹配或超越最强标量RL基线，且随着搜索预算增加，差距扩大。对于进化搜索，VPO模型解锁了GRPO模型根本无法解决的问题。随着测试时搜索日益标准化，优化多样性可能需要成为后训练的默认目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for language models to generalize to novel environments and support diverse reward functions during inference-time search, the paper introduces Vector Policy Optimization (VPO), an RL method that trains policies to anticipate and generate diverse solutions by leveraging vector-valued rewards. VPO replaces the scalar reward optimization in standard post-training with a vector-based approach, encouraging the model to produce a set of specialized outputs that cover different trade-offs in reward space. Experimental results across four tasks show that VPO matches or outperforms scalar RL baselines on metrics like pass@k and best@k, with performance gaps widening as search budgets increase; notably, VPO enables evolutionary search where GRPO fails entirely.
为使语言模型能适应新环境并支持推理时的多样化奖励函数搜索，本文提出向量策略优化（VPO）方法，通过利用向量奖励（如代码生成中的多测试用例正确性或用户角色多样性）训练策略，促使模型生成覆盖奖励空间不同权衡的多样化解决方案。VPO通过替换GRPO中的标量奖励估计器，鼓励模型输出多个专业化结果。在四个任务中，VPO在pass@k和best@k等指标上表现与最强标量RL基线持平或更优，且随着搜索预算增加优势扩大；尤其在进化搜索中，VPO能解决GRPO完全无法处理的问题，表明多样性优化应成为后训练的默认目标。
AwareVLN: Reasoning with Self-awareness for Vision-Language Navigation
Authors: Wenxuan Guo, Xiuwei Xu, Yichen Liu, Xiangyu Li, Hang Yin, Huangxing Chen, Wenzhao Zheng, Jianjiang Feng, Jie Zhou, Jiwen Lu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-21T17:58:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:58:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/AwareVLN/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to ground language instructions to its own movement within a visual environment. While state-of-the-art methods leverage the reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for end-to-end action prediction, they often lack an explicit and explainable understanding of the relationships between the agent, the instruction, and the scene. Conversely, explicitly building a scene map for heuristic planning is intuitively appealing but relies on additional 3D sensors and hinders large-scale vision-language pre-training. To bridge this gap, we propose AwareVLN, a novel framework that equips the navigation model with a self-aware reasoning mechanism, enabling it to understand the agent's state and task progress in a fully end-to-end and data-driven manner. Our approach features two key innovations: (1) a structural reasoning module that fosters spatial and task-oriented self-awareness, and (2) an automatic data engine with progress division for effective training. Extensive experiments on various datasets in Habitat simulator show our AwareVLN significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art vision-language navigation methods. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/AwareVLN/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AwareVLN：面向视觉-语言导航的自感知推理
视觉-语言导航（VLN）要求代理将语言指令与自身在视觉环境中的运动关联起来。虽然当前最先进的方法利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）的推理能力进行端到端动作预测，但它们往往缺乏对代理、指令与场景之间关系的显式且可解释的理解。相反，显式构建场景地图以进行启发式规划虽然直观，但依赖额外的3D传感器，且阻碍大规模视觉-语言预训练。为弥合这一差距，我们提出AwareVLN，一种新颖框架，为导航模型配备自感知推理机制，使其能够以端到端、数据驱动的方式理解代理状态和任务进展。我们的方法包含两项关键创新：（1）促进空间与任务导向自感知的结构化推理模块；（2）具备进度划分的自动数据引擎，以实现高效训练。在Habitat模拟器上的多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，AwareVLN显著优于先前最先进的视觉-语言导航方法。项目页面：https://gwxuan.github.io/AwareVLN/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for explainable and self-aware reasoning in vision-language navigation, AwareVLN introduces a framework that enables agents to understand their own state and task progress within visual environments without relying on external 3D sensors. It achieves this through a structural reasoning module that fosters spatial and task-oriented self-awareness, paired with an automatic data engine that divides training progress for improved learning. Experiments on multiple Habitat datasets demonstrate that AwareVLN significantly outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods in navigation accuracy and task completion rates, validating its effectiveness in fully end-to-end, data-driven navigation.
为提升视觉语言导航中的可解释性和自感知能力，AwareVLN提出一种框架，使导航代理无需依赖外部3D传感器或预建地图，即可理解自身状态与任务进度。该框架通过结构化推理模块增强空间与任务导向的自感知能力，并结合自动数据引擎按训练进度划分数据以优化学习。在Habitat模拟器多个数据集上的实验表明，AwareVLN在导航精度与鲁棒性上显著超越现有最先进方法。
Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration
Authors: Lily Goli, Justin Kerr, Daniele Reda, Alec Jacobson, Andrea Tagliasacchi, Angjoo Kanazawa
First: 2026-05-21T17:58:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:58:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：保持好奇心：用于3D探索的事件上下文与持久化世界
在稀疏奖励、长时域任务中，探索是学习有用行为的前提，尤其是在3D环境中。好奇心驱动的强化学习通过从代理对世界预测模型与现实之间的不匹配中提取内在奖励来解决这一问题。然而，将这种内在动机迁移到复杂、逼真的环境中仍很困难，因为代理可能陷入局部循环，并在重新访问已遗忘状态时获得新的奖励。本研究证明，这种失败源于缺乏空间持久性和事件上下文。我们表明，有效的探索需要一个持续更新的世界模型，以及一个能维护事件轨迹历史的代理，以便导航至新区域。我们通过在线3D重建作为持久化世界模型，同时将代理策略参数化为RGB观测序列模型以保持事件上下文。该设计在训练期间实现有效探索，同时在部署时仅使用RGB帧进行导航。仅在HM3D上通过好奇心训练，我们的代理优于基于强化学习的主动建图基线，并能零样本泛化至Gibson和AI生成的世界。我们的端到端策略支持高效下游任务适应，如摘苹果和图像目标导航，优于从零开始的基线。请参见视频结果：https://recuriosity.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of effective exploration in sparse-reward 3D environments, this work proposes that curiosity-driven agents fail due to insufficient spatial persistence and episodic context. The method combines an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent world model with a sequence-based policy that retains episodic trajectory history to guide exploration toward novelty. Experiments show that this design enables robust exploration during training and zero-shot generalization across diverse photorealistic worlds, outperforming active mapping baselines and achieving superior performance on downstream tasks like apple picking and image-goal navigation without task-specific fine-tuning.
为解决稀疏奖励长时任务中3D环境探索不足的问题，该研究指出好奇心驱动的智能体失败源于缺乏空间持久性和情景记忆。方法结合在线3D重建构建持久世界模型，并用基于序列的策略通过RGB图像保持轨迹历史。实验表明，该设计在训练中实现有效探索，且能零样本泛化至Gibson和AI生成环境，超越RL主动建图基线，并在苹果采摘和图像目标导航等下游任务中表现更优，无需额外微调。
GesVLA: Gesture-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model Embedded Representations
Authors: Wenxuan Guo, Ziyuan Li, Meng Zhang, Yichen Liu, Yimeng Dong, Chuxi Xu, Yunfei Wei, Ze Chen, Erjin Zhou, Jianjiang Feng
First: 2026-05-21T17:57:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:57:44+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robot manipulation by unifying perception and action. However, existing VLA systems primarily rely on textual instructions and struggle to resolve spatial ambiguity in complex scenes with multiple similar objects. To address this limitation, we introduce gesture as a parallel instruction modality and propose a Gesture-aware Vision-Language-Action model (GesVLA). Our approach encodes gesture features directly into the latent space, enabling them to participate in both high-level reasoning and low-level action generation, and adopts a dual-VLM architecture to achieve tight coupling between gesture representations and action policies. At the data level, we construct a scalable gesture data generation pipeline by rendering hand models onto real-world scene images. This reduces the sim-to-real visual gap while producing rich data with diverse motion patterns and corresponding pointing annotations. In addition, we employ a two-stage training strategy to equip the model with both gesture perception and action prediction capabilities. We evaluate our approach on multiple real-world robotic tasks, including a controlled block manipulation task for validation and more practical scenarios such as product and produce selection. Experimental results show that incorporating gesture consistently improves target grounding accuracy and human-robot interaction efficiency, especially in complex and cluttered environments. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GesVLA：基于手势感知的视觉-语言-动作模型嵌入表示
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通过统一感知与动作，在通用机器人操作中展现出强大潜力。然而，现有VLA系统主要依赖文本指令，难以解决复杂场景中多个相似物体的空间歧义问题。为解决此局限，我们引入手势作为并行指令模态，提出手势感知视觉-语言-动作模型（GesVLA）。该方法直接将手势特征编码至潜在空间，使其参与高层推理与底层动作生成，并采用双视觉语言模型架构，实现手势表征与动作策略的紧密耦合。在数据层面，我们通过将手部模型渲染至真实场景图像，构建可扩展的手势数据生成流水线，从而缩小仿真到现实的视觉差距，同时生成包含多样运动模式及对应指向标注的丰富数据。此外，我们采用两阶段训练策略，使模型兼具手势感知与动作预测能力。我们在多个真实机器人任务中评估该方法，包括用于验证的受控积木操作任务及更实用的场景如商品与农产品选择。实验结果表明，引入手势可持续提升目标定位准确率与人机交互效率，尤其在复杂杂乱环境中表现突出。项目页面：https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing Vision-Language-Action models in handling spatial ambiguity through text alone, this work introduces GesVLA, a gesture-aware model that integrates hand gestures as a parallel instruction modality. It encodes gesture features directly into the latent space to enable joint reasoning and action generation, using a dual-VLM architecture to tightly couple gesture representations with action policies. The model is trained via a two-stage strategy and supported by a scalable data pipeline that renders hand gestures onto real-world images, reducing simulation-to-reality gaps. Experiments on block manipulation and real-world selection tasks demonstrate consistent improvements in target grounding accuracy and interaction efficiency, particularly in cluttered environments.
为了解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型仅依赖文本指令难以处理复杂场景中空间歧义的问题，本文提出GesVLA模型，将手势作为并行指令模态引入。该模型直接将手势特征编码进潜在空间，使其参与高层推理与底层动作生成，并采用双视觉语言模型架构实现手势表征与动作策略的紧密耦合。训练数据通过在真实场景图像上渲染手部模型生成，有效缩小仿真到现实的视觉差距并提供多样化的手势与指向标注。在包括积木操作和真实产品选择等机器人任务中，GesVLA显著提升目标定位准确率和人机交互效率，尤其在杂乱环境中表现更优。
Sensor2Sensor: Cross-Embodiment Sensor Conversion for Autonomous Driving
Authors: Jiahao Wang, Bo Sun, Yijing Bai, Vincent Casser, Songyou Peng, Zehao Zhu, Meng-Li Shih, Xander Masotto, Shih-Yang Su, Kanaad V Parvate, Tiancheng Ge, Linn Bieske, Dragomir Anguelov, Mingxing Tan, Chiyu Max Jiang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-21T17:57:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:57:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust training and validation of Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS) require massive, diverse datasets. Proprietary data collected by Autonomous Vehicle (AV) fleets, while high-fidelity, are limited in scale, diversity of sensor configurations, as well as geographic and long-tail-behavioral coverage. In contrast, in-the-wild data from sources like dashcams offers immense scale and diversity, capturing critical long-tail scenarios and novel environments. However, this unstructured, in-the-wild video data is incompatible with ADS expecting structured, multi-modal sensor inputs for validation and training. To bridge this data gap, we propose Sensor2Sensor, a novel generative modeling paradigm that translates in-the-wild monocular dashcam videos into a high-fidelity, multi-modal sensor suite (AV logs) comprising multi-view camera images and LiDAR point clouds. A core challenge is the lack of paired training data. We address this by converting real AV logs into dashcam-style videos via 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) reconstruction and novel-view rendering. Sensor2Sensor then utilizes a diffusion architecture to perform the generative conversion. We perform comprehensive quantitative evaluations on the fidelity and realism of the generated sensor data. We demonstrate Sensor2Sensor's practical utility by converting challenging in-the-wild internet and dashcam footage into realistic, multi-modal data formats, further unlocking vast external data sources for AV development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Sensor2Sensor：面向自动驾驶的跨载体传感器转换
自动驾驶系统（ADS）的稳健训练与验证需要海量、多样化的数据集。虽然自动驾驶车队采集的专有数据具有高保真度，但在规模、传感器配置多样性以及地理和长尾行为覆盖方面存在局限。相比之下，来自行车记录仪等来源的野外数据规模庞大、多样性高，能捕捉关键的长尾场景和新环境。然而，这种非结构化的野外视频数据与ADS期望的结构化、多模态传感器输入不兼容。为弥合这一数据鸿沟，我们提出Sensor2Sensor，一种新颖的生成建模范式，可将野外单目行车记录仪视频转换为高保真、多模态传感器套件（AV日志），包含多视角相机图像和激光雷达点云。核心挑战在于缺乏配对训练数据。我们通过4D高斯点绘（4DGS）重建和新视角渲染，将真实AV日志转换为行车记录仪风格视频，从而解决该问题。Sensor2Sensor随后利用扩散架构执行生成转换。我们在生成传感器数据的保真度和真实感方面进行了全面定量评估，并通过将具有挑战性的野外互联网和行车记录仪视频转换为逼真的多模态数据格式，展示了其实际应用价值，进一步解锁了自动驾驶开发的海量外部数据源。
Summary / 总结
To address the scarcity of diverse, large-scale sensor data for autonomous driving systems, the authors propose Sensor2Sensor, a generative model that converts unstructured in-the-wild dashcam videos into high-fidelity multi-modal sensor data (including multi-view camera images and LiDAR point clouds) compatible with AV training pipelines. The method overcomes the lack of paired training data by first reconstructing real AV logs into dashcam-style videos using 4D Gaussian Splatting and novel-view rendering, then applying a diffusion-based architecture to perform the conversion. Experimental results show the generated sensor data achieves high fidelity and realism, successfully transforming challenging real-world video footage into usable multi-modal formats, thereby unlocking vast external data sources for autonomous vehicle development.
为解决自动驾驶系统训练所需大规模、多样化传感器数据稀缺的问题，Sensor2Sensor提出一种生成框架，将无结构的行车记录仪视频转化为包含多视角图像与激光雷达点云的高保真多模态传感器数据。该方法通过4D高斯点云重建与新视角渲染，将真实自动驾驶传感器日志转换为行车记录仪风格视频，再利用扩散模型完成数据转换。实验表明，生成数据保真度高、真实感强，成功使海量野外视频数据可用于自动驾驶系统的训练与验证。
Large-scale Score-based Variational Posterior Inference for Bayesian Deep Neural Networks
Authors: Minyoung Kim
First: 2026-02-05T16:51:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:55:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian (deep) neural networks (BNN) are often more attractive than the vanilla point-estimate deep learning in various aspects including uncertainty quantification, robustness to noise, resistance to overfitting, and more. The variational inference (VI) is one of the most widely adopted approximate inference methods. Whereas the ELBO-based variational free energy method is a dominant choice in the literature, in this paper we introduce a score-based alternative for BNN variational inference. Score-based VI can address the known issue of mode collapsing in ELBO-based VI. Although several score-based VI methods have been proposed in the community, most are not adequate for large-scale BNNs for various computational and technical reasons. We propose a novel scalable VI method where the learning objective combines the score matching loss and the proximal penalty term in iterations, which helps our method avoid the reparametrized sampling, and allows for noisy unbiased mini-batch scores through stochastic gradients. This in turn makes our method scalable to large-scale neural networks including Vision Transformers. On several benchmarks including visual recognition and time-series forecasting with large-scale deep networks, we empirically show the effectiveness of our approach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于贝叶斯深度神经网络的基于大规模得分的变分后验推断
贝叶斯（深度）神经网络（BNN）在不确定性量化、抗噪性、抗过拟合等方面通常比标准点估计深度学习更具吸引力。变分推断（VI）是最广泛采用的近似推断方法之一。虽然基于ELBO的变分自由能方法在文献中占主导地位，本文提出了一种基于得分的BNN变分推断替代方案。基于得分的VI可解决基于ELBO的VI中已知的模式坍缩问题。尽管社区已提出多种基于得分的VI方法，但大多数因计算和技术原因难以适用于大规模BNN。我们提出了一种新颖的可扩展VI方法，其学习目标结合了得分匹配损失和迭代中的近端惩罚项，从而避免重参数化采样，并允许通过随机梯度获得噪声无偏的小批量得分。这使得我们的方法可扩展至包括视觉变换器在内的大规模神经网络。在包括视觉识别和时间序列预测在内的多个基准测试中，我们实证展示了该方法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable Bayesian deep neural networks that avoid mode collapse inherent in ELBO-based variational inference, this paper proposes a score-based variational inference method tailored for large-scale models. The approach combines score matching loss with a proximal penalty term to enable training without reparametrized sampling and to support noisy, unbiased mini-batch gradients, thereby improving scalability for architectures like Vision Transformers. Experimental results across visual recognition and time-series forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method achieves competitive or superior performance compared to existing ELBO-based and score-based alternatives, while maintaining computational efficiency on large-scale networks.
为解决传统变分推断在大规模贝叶斯深度神经网络中易出现模式坍缩且难以扩展的问题，本文提出一种基于分数匹配的变分推断方法。该方法结合分数匹配损失与近端惩罚项，避免重参数化采样并支持噪声无偏的小批量梯度，从而适用于如视觉Transformer等大规模模型。在视觉识别与时间序列预测等多个基准测试中，实验结果表明该方法在后验质量与可扩展性方面优于基于ELBO的变分推断方法。
The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning
Authors: Vishal Rajput
First: 2026-05-21T17:53:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:53:28+00:00
Comments: 54 pages. 13 empirical task blocks. Companion software: matching-pmh (PyPI; https://github.com/vishalstark512/matching-pmh). Related arXiv note: 2604.21395 (geometric blind spot / isotropic PMH)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks. In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions. We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient. Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it. The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.
中文标题/摘要
标题：匹配原则：一种针对干扰鲁棒表示学习的损失函数几何理论
鲁棒性、领域自适应、光度与遮挡不变性、组合泛化、时间鲁棒性、对齐安全性及经典各向异性正则化通常被视为独立问题，各自拥有不同方法体系。本文主张，它们共享的结构本质上是一个统计问题：估计标签保持部署干扰的协方差，然后沿覆盖该协方差的矩阵范围正则化编码器雅可比矩阵（即匹配原则）。CORAL、对抗训练、IRM、数据增强、度量学习、雅可比惩罚及对齐式约束，都是该对象的不同估计器，而非独立的鲁棒性技巧。 在线性高斯模型中，我们证明了闭式最优性（定理A），包括匹配范围内立方根水填充；二次雅可比惩罚范围覆盖的必要性（定理G）；深度全局最小值处相同的范围二分性；以及两个伪证控制（引理C；推论E），并提供七个在标准可识别性假设下估计的条件一致性引理（D1-D7）。 我们引入轨迹偏差指数（TDI），作为一种在任务准确率或雅可比Frobenius范数不足时，无标签探测嵌入敏感性的工具。 十三个从经典机器学习到Qwen2.5-7B的预注册任务块，测试了预测匹配、等向及错误-W排序在几何与部署漂移下的表现；十二个通过，唯一例外（Office-31）是运行前已知的特征值间隙失败。在7B规模下，匹配风格-PMH提升选择性诚实性，并在标准DPO退化时保持Style TDI。 本文贡献在于命名部署干扰协方差，明确正则化器必须执行的操作，并在识别该对象后提供一个闭式可证伪理论，而非对所有排行榜的普适性。
Summary / 总结
This paper unifies diverse robustness techniques—such as domain adaptation, occlusion invariance, and compositional generalization—under a single geometric framework called the 'matching principle,' which posits that robust representation learning requires regularizing the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range matches the covariance of deployment nuisances. The authors derive closed-form theoretical guarantees in linear-Gaussian settings, proving optimality conditions and consistency lemmas under identifiability assumptions. Empirically, across 13 pre-registered tasks including large-scale models like Qwen2.5-7B, the 'matched' regularization outperforms isotropic or incorrect variants, with 12 tasks showing consistent geometric alignment and deployment stability; the exception (Office-31) was predicted due to an eigengap failure. A novel label-free metric, Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), further validates embedding robustness where standard accuracy or Jacobian norms fail.
该论文提出“匹配原则”，将鲁棒性、领域自适应、不变性等不同问题统一为估计部署干扰协方差并沿其范围正则化编码器雅可比矩阵的几何问题。理论部分在线性高斯模型中证明了闭式最优解、范围覆盖必要性及一致性引理。实验在13个预注册任务中验证，12个任务支持“匹配”正则化优于“各向同性”或“错误-W”方案，唯一失败案例（Office-31）源于特征值间隙。在7B规模下，匹配风格的PMH提升对齐安全性并保持嵌入稳定性，而标准DPO则导致退化。
Symphony for Speech-to-Text: Supporting Real-Time Medical Voice Interfaces
Authors: Arne Nix, Robert James, Lasse Borgholt, Anna B. Ekner, Lana Krumm, Julius Severin, Dan Engel, Lars Maaløe, Jakob Havtorn
First: 2026-05-15T18:39:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:52:56+00:00
Comments: Updated with a correction and improvement to Symphony's performance in spoken punctuation evaluation (R_punct, P_punct)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
After decades of use in dictation and, more recently, ambient documentation, speech is emerging as a primary modality for interacting with technology and AI in healthcare. Yet medical speech recognition remains difficult: systems must capture specialized terminology, resolve contextual ambiguity, and render measurements, abbreviations, and clinical shorthand precisely. Existing solutions are typically optimized either for general-purpose transcription or narrow dictation workflows, limiting their reliability in safety-critical settings and their usefulness for broader clinical workflows. We introduce Symphony for Speech-to-Text, a medical-grade speech recognition system for real-time streaming and batch file-based clinical use. Symphony decomposes the transcription process into specialized components for recognition, formatting, and contextual correction to optimize medical term recall while producing clinically structured text in real time and adapting across use cases. Evaluations on public benchmark and medical speech datasets show that Symphony substantially outperforms state-of-the-art systems in clinical settings while matching or exceeding them in general-domain settings, suggesting robust generalization rather than overfitting. We release a clinical benchmark dataset to support reliable validation and further progress in medical speech recognition. Symphony is available through a production-grade API for live dictation, conversational transcription, and batch audio file processing.

标题：面向医疗语音界面的实时语音转文字交响系统
经过数十年在听写中的应用，以及近年来在环境记录中的使用，语音正逐渐成为医疗领域与技术及人工智能交互的主要方式。然而，医疗语音识别仍面临挑战：系统需准确捕捉专业术语、解决语境歧义，并精确呈现测量值、缩写和临床简写。现有解决方案通常针对通用转录或狭窄听写工作流进行优化，限制了其在安全关键场景中的可靠性及在更广泛临床工作流中的实用性。我们提出Symphony语音转文字系统，专为实时流式和批量文件临床应用设计，具备医疗级语音识别能力。Symphony将转录过程分解为识别、格式化和语境校正等专用组件，以优化医学术语召回率，实时生成结构化临床文本，并适应不同应用场景。在公开基准和医疗语音数据集上的评估表明，Symphony在临床场景中显著优于当前最先进系统，而在通用领域表现匹配或超越现有系统，表明其具备稳健的泛化能力而非过拟合。我们发布临床基准数据集，以支持可靠验证及医疗语音识别领域的进一步发展。Symphony通过生产级API提供实时听写、对话转录和批量音频文件处理功能。

Finite-Particle Convergence Rates for Conservative and Non-Conservative Drifting Models
Authors: Krishnakumar Balasubramanian
First: 2026-05-21T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:49:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose and analyze a conservative drifting method for one-step generative modeling. The method replaces the original displacement-based drifting velocity by a kernel density estimator (KDE)-gradient velocity, namely the difference of the kernel-smoothed data score and the kernel-smoothed model score. This velocity is a gradient field, addressing the non-conservatism issue identified for general displacement-based drifting fields. We prove continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds for the conservative method on $\R^d$: a joint-entropy identity yields bounds for the empirical Stein drift, the smoothed Fisher discrepancy of the KDE, and the squared center velocity. The main finite-particle correction is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction term, and we give deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions under which this term is controlled. We keep the quadrature constants explicit and track their possible bandwidth dependence: the root residual-velocity rate $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ holds under an additional $h$-uniform quadrature regularity condition, while a more general growth condition yields the optimized root rate $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$, where $0\le β<2$. We also analyze the non-conservative drifting method with Laplace kernel, corresponding to the original displacement-based velocity proposed in~\cite{deng2026drifting}. For this method, a sharp companion kernel decomposes the velocity into a positive scalar preconditioning of a sharp-score mismatch plus a Laplace scale-mismatch residual, producing an analogous finite-particle rate with an unavoidable residual term. Finally, we explain how the continuous-time residual-velocity bounds translate into one-step generation guarantees through the explicit drift size $η$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：保守与非保守漂移模型的有限粒子收敛速率
我们提出并分析了一种保守漂移方法，用于一步生成建模。该方法将原始基于位移的漂移速度替换为核密度估计（KDE）梯度速度，即核平滑数据得分与核平滑模型得分之差。该速度场为梯度场，解决了通用基于位移漂移场中识别出的非保守性问题。我们在 $\R^d$ 上证明了该保守方法的连续时间有限粒子收敛界：联合熵恒等式给出了经验Stein漂移、KDE的平滑Fisher差异及平方中心速度的界。主要的有限粒子修正项为逆KDE自相互作用项，我们给出了确定性及高概率局部占据条件下该项可控的条件。我们保留了四次积分常数的显式表达并追踪其可能的带宽依赖性：在额外的 $h$-均匀四次积分正则性条件下，根残余速度率为 $N^{-1/(d+4)}$；而更一般的增长条件则给出优化的根速率 $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$，其中 $0\le β<2$。我们还分析了使用Laplace核的非保守漂移方法，对应于文献~\cite{deng2026drifting}中提出的原始基于位移的速度。对于该方法，一个尖锐伴随核将速度分解为正标量预处理项与尖锐得分偏差项及Laplace尺度偏差残差项，从而产生类似有限粒子速率，但不可避免存在残差项。最后，我们解释了连续时间残余速度界如何通过显式漂移大小 $η$ 转化为一步生成保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the non-conservative drift in standard displacement-based generative models, this work introduces a conservative drifting method using a kernel density estimator (KDE) gradient to replace the original velocity field, ensuring gradient structure and improved convergence. The method derives continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds in $\R^d$ via a joint-entropy identity, controlling empirical Stein drift, KDE Fisher discrepancy, and squared center velocity. Key results show a root residual-velocity rate of $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ under uniform quadrature conditions, or $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$ for $0\le β<2$ under general growth conditions. In contrast, the non-conservative Laplace-kernel method decomposes velocity into preconditioning and residual terms, yielding a similar rate but with an unavoidable residual. The continuous-time bounds directly translate to one-step generation guarantees via explicit drift size $η$.
为解决生成模型中漂移方法的非保守性问题，本文提出一种保守漂移方法，将位移基速度替换为基于核密度估计（KDE）梯度的速度，即数据与模型的核平滑得分之差。该方法保证梯度场性质，并在连续时间下推导出 $\R^d$ 中有限粒子收敛界，利用联合熵恒等式约束经验Stein漂移、平滑Fisher偏差和中心速度平方。主要修正项为反核密度自相互作用项，在局部占据条件下可控，且显式保留积分常数并分析其带宽依赖性：在均匀积分条件下，残差速度率为 $N^{-1/(d+4)}$，而在更一般的增长条件下，可优化至 $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$（$0\le β<2$）。同时分析了非保守的Laplace核漂移方法，其速度分解为正标量预处理项与尺度不匹配残差项，同样得到含不可消除残差项的有限粒子速率，且连续时间残差界可转化为一步生成的显式漂移大小 $η$ 的保证。
MOSS: Self-Evolution through Source-Level Rewriting in Autonomous Agent Systems
Authors: Qianshu Cai, Yonggang Zhang, Xianzhang Jia, Wei Xue, Jun Song, Xinmei Tian, Yike Guo
First: 2026-05-21T17:48:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:48:33+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables. Preprint. Code: https://github.com/dav-joy-thon/MOSS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Autonomous agentic systems are largely static after deployment: they do not learn from user interactions, and recurring failures persist until the next human-driven update ships a fix. Self-evolving agents have emerged in response, but all confine evolution to text-mutable artifacts -- skill files, prompt configurations, memory schemas, workflow graphs -- and leave the agent harness untouched. Since routing, hook ordering, state invariants, and dispatch live in code rather than in any text artifact, an entire class of structural failure is physically unreachable from the text layer. We argue that source-level adaptation is a fundamentally more general medium: it is Turing-complete, a strict superset of every text-mutable scope, takes effect deterministically rather than through base-model compliance, and does not erode under long-context drift. We present MOSS, a system that performs self-rewriting at the source level on production agentic substrates. Each evolution is anchored to an automatically curated batch of production-failure evidence and proceeds through a deterministic multi-stage pipeline; code modification is delegated to a pluggable external coding-agent CLI while MOSS retains stage ordering and verdicts. Candidates are verified by replaying the batch against the candidate image in ephemeral trial workers, then promoted via user-consent-gated, in-place container swap with health-probe-gated rollback. On OpenClaw, MOSS lifts a four-task mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 in a single cycle without human intervention.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MOSS：通过源码级重写实现自主代理系统的自我演化
自主代理系统在部署后基本保持静态：它们无法从用户交互中学习，重复性故障持续存在，直到下一次由人类驱动的更新推送修复。为应对这一问题，自我演化代理应运而生，但所有现有方案均将演化限制在可修改文本的工件——技能文件、提示配置、记忆模式、工作流图——而未触及代理框架本身。由于路由、钩子排序、状态不变性及分发机制均存在于代码而非任何文本工件中，因此存在一类结构性故障在文本层是无法触及的。我们认为，源码级适应是一种更通用的媒介：它具备图灵完备性，是所有可修改文本范围的严格超集，其效果确定而非依赖基础模型合规性，且不受长上下文漂移影响。我们提出MOSS系统，可在生产级代理基底上执行源码级自我重写。每次演化均锚定于自动精选的生产故障证据批次，并通过确定性多阶段流水线推进；代码修改委托给可插拔的外部编码代理CLI，而MOSS保留阶段顺序与判定结果。候选方案通过在临时试用工作者中重放批次进行验证，再经用户授权、原地容器替换及健康探针控制的回滚机制推广。在OpenClaw上，MOSS单次循环无需人工干预，将四任务平均评分从0.25提升至0.61。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that autonomous agents remain static after deployment and cannot self-correct structural failures in code, the authors propose MOSS, a system enabling source-level self-evolution in production agentic systems. MOSS operates by analyzing production failure evidence, generating deterministic code modifications via an external coding agent, and validating candidates through replay in ephemeral workers before promoting them via user-consent-gated in-place container swaps with rollback safeguards. Experimental results on OpenClaw show MOSS significantly improved performance, lifting the mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 across four tasks in a single automated cycle without human intervention.
为解决自主代理系统部署后无法自我修正代码层结构性故障的问题，作者提出MOSS系统，支持生产环境中源码级别的自我重写。MOSS通过自动收集失败证据，执行确定性多阶段流程，将代码修改委托给外部编码代理，同时保留阶段顺序与验证控制。候选方案在临时工作节点上通过重放失败批次进行验证，并通过用户授权的原地容器替换与健康探针回滚机制进行部署。在OpenClaw实验中，MOSS单次迭代将平均评分从0.25提升至0.61，无需人工干预。
Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention
Authors: Ali Hatamizadeh, Yejin Choi, Jan Kautz
First: 2026-05-21T17:44:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:44:57+00:00
Comments: Gated DeltaNet-2 technical report; code at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.
中文标题/摘要
标题：门控DeltaNet-2：线性注意力中擦除与写入的解耦
线性注意力用固定大小的循环状态替代softmax注意力的无界缓存，将序列混合降至线性时间，解码降至常量内存。难点不仅在于遗忘什么，更在于如何在不打乱现有关联的前提下编辑压缩的记忆。Delta规则模型在写入前减去当前读取值，Kimi Delta注意力（KDA）通过通道级衰减强化遗忘。但主动编辑仍使用单个标量门控同时控制两个不同操作：在键侧擦除多少旧内容和在值侧提交多少新内容。我们提出门控DeltaNet-2，通过继承自适应遗忘与通道级衰减，同时解决二者共有的局限——擦除与写入的标量绑定。门控Delta规则-2通过通道级擦除门b_t和通道级写入门w_t分离这两个角色，当两个门坍缩为同一标量时退化为KDA，当衰减也坍缩时退化为门控DeltaNet。我们推导出快速权重更新视角、吸收通道级衰减的分块WY算法，以及保留高效并行训练的门控感知反向传播。在1.3B参数、100B FineWeb-Edu token训练下，门控DeltaNet-2在语言建模、常识推理与检索任务中，超越Mamba-2、门控DeltaNet、KDA及Mamba-3变体，表现最优。其优势在长上下文RULER“针在 haystack”基准测试中尤为显著，提升多键检索评估设置表现，并在循环与混合设置中保持强劲。代码见https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently manage memory in linear attention models without scrambling existing associations during edits, Gated DeltaNet-2 introduces a novel architecture that decouples erase and write operations using separate channel-wise gates. Unlike prior models that rely on a single scalar gate to control both forgetting and writing, Gated DeltaNet-2 employs distinct erase and write gates, enabling more precise memory updates while retaining the benefits of adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay. Evaluated on language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval tasks, the model outperforms Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants, especially on long-context retrieval benchmarks like RULER, where it demonstrates superior performance in multi-key retrieval and maintains strength across recurrent and hybrid settings.
为了解决线性注意力模型在编辑压缩记忆时易打乱现有关联的问题，Gated DeltaNet-2提出了一种新架构，通过引入独立的通道级擦除门和写入门，将擦除与写入操作解耦。与依赖单一标量门控制两者的老方法不同，该模型使用两个独立门，实现更精确的记忆更新，同时保留自适应遗忘和通道级衰减的优势。在语言建模、常识推理和检索任务中，该模型在长上下文检索基准（如RULER）上表现最优，尤其在多键检索任务中优势明显，且在循环和混合架构下均保持强劲性能。
LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems
Authors: Sadia Asif, Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri, Momin Abbas, Prasanna Sattigeri, Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy
First: 2026-05-21T17:42:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:42:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LCGuard：多智能体系统中安全的键值共享潜通信框架
基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统日益依赖中间通信来协调复杂任务。虽然现有系统大多通过自然语言通信，但近期研究表明，通过Transformer键值（KV）缓存的潜通信可提升效率并保留更丰富的任务相关信息。然而，KV缓存也编码了上下文输入、中间推理状态和智能体特定信息，形成一个隐式通道，敏感内容可能在智能体间传播而无需显式文本披露。为此，我们提出\textbf{LCGuard}（潜通信防护），一种面向多智能体LLM系统的安全KV潜通信框架。LCGuard将共享KV缓存视为潜工作记忆，在缓存内容跨智能体传输前学习表示层面的变换。我们通过重建形式化表示层面敏感信息泄露：若对抗解码器能从共享缓存中恢复智能体特定敏感输入，则该缓存内容不安全。这导致一种对抗性训练框架：攻击者学习重建敏感输入，而LCGuard学习既能保留任务相关语义又能减少可重建信息的变换。在多个模型家族和多智能体基准上的实证评估表明，LCGuard持续降低基于重建的泄露和攻击成功率，同时保持与标准KV共享基线相当的任务性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of sensitive information leakage through latent communication in KV caches during multi-agent LLM coordination, LCGuard introduces a framework that transforms shared cache representations before inter-agent transmission to prevent reconstruction of agent-specific inputs. It formalizes safety via adversarial reconstruction: if an attacker can recover sensitive inputs from a cache artifact, the artifact is deemed unsafe. LCGuard trains a transformation module to preserve task-relevant semantics while minimizing reconstructable information, competing with standard KV-sharing baselines. Experiments across multiple models and benchmarks show consistent reduction in reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates without significant performance degradation.
为解决多智能体LLM系统中通过KV缓存进行隐式通信时敏感信息泄露的风险，LCGuard提出一种框架，通过在缓存传输前对表示层进行变换，以阻止对手从共享缓存中重建特定智能体的敏感输入，同时保留任务相关语义。该方法采用对抗训练机制，让对手学习从缓存中重建敏感输入，而LCGuard则学习能最小化可重建信息的变换。实验表明，该方法在多个模型和基准测试中显著降低基于重建的信息泄露和攻击成功率，且任务性能与标准KV共享基线相当。
Evaluating Commercial AI Chatbots as News Intermediaries
Authors: Mirac Suzgun, Emily Shen, Federico Bianchi, Alexander Spangher, Thomas Icard, Daniel E. Ho, Dan Jurafsky, James Zou
First: 2026-05-21T17:42:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:42:07+00:00
Comments: https://suzgunmirac.github.io/ai-news-preview/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
AI chatbots are rapidly shaping how people encounter the news, yet no prior study has systematically measured how accurately these systems, with their proprietary search integrations and retrieval-synthesis pipelines, handle emerging facts across languages and regions. We present a 14-day (February 9-22, 2026) evaluation of six AI chatbots (Gemini 3 Flash and Pro, Grok 4, Claude 4.5 Sonnet, GPT-5 and GPT-4o mini) on 2,100 factual questions derived from same-day BBC News reporting across six regional services (US & Canada, Arabic, Afrique, Hindi, Russian, Turkish). The best systems achieve over 90% multiple-choice accuracy on questions about events reported hours earlier. The same systems, however, lose 11-13% under free-response evaluation, and 16-17% across the cohort. We further characterize three failure patterns. First, every model achieves its lowest accuracy on Hindi (79% vs. 89-91% elsewhere) and citations indicate an Anglophone retrieval bias (e.g., models answering Hindi queries cite English Wikipedia more than any Hindi outlet). Second, retrieval, not reasoning, failures drive over 70% of all errors. When models retrieve a correct source, they often extract the correct answer; the problem is to land on the right source in the first place. Third, models achieving 88-96% accuracy on well-formed questions drop to 19-70% when questions contain subtle false premises, with the most vulnerable model accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time. We also identify a detection-accuracy paradox: the best false-premise detector ranks second in adversarial accuracy (abstention rate), while a weaker detector ranks first, showing that premise detection and answer recovery are partially independent capabilities. Overall, these suggest that high accuracy can mask systematic regional inequity, near-total dependence on retrieval infrastructure, and vulnerability to imperfect queries real users pose.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估商业AI聊天机器人作为新闻中介的表现
AI聊天机器人正迅速改变人们接触新闻的方式，但此前尚无研究系统衡量这些系统（其内置专有搜索集成与检索-合成管道）在多语言和多地区处理新兴事实的准确性。我们对六款AI聊天机器人（Gemini 3 Flash和Pro、Grok 4、Claude 4.5 Sonnet、GPT-5和GPT-4o mini）在2026年2月9日至22日的14天内，基于六种区域BBC新闻服务（美国与加拿大、阿拉伯语、非洲、印地语、俄语、土耳其语）同日报道的2100个事实性问题进行了评估。表现最佳的系统在关于数小时前报道事件的问题上，多项选择题准确率超过90%。然而，同一系统在自由回答评估中准确率下降11-13%，整体下降16-17%。我们进一步识别出三种失败模式：第一，所有模型在印地语问题上的准确率最低（79%，而其他地区为89-91%），引用显示存在英语主导的检索偏倚（如模型回答印地语问题时更常引用英文维基百科而非印地语媒体）；第二，超过70%的错误源于检索而非推理——当模型检索到正确来源时，往往能提取正确答案，问题在于首先找到正确的来源；第三，当问题包含微妙的错误前提时，原本在结构良好问题上准确率88-96%的模型准确率骤降至19-70%，最脆弱的模型64%情况下接受虚构事实。我们还发现检测-准确率悖论：最佳错误前提检测器在对抗性准确率（回避率）中排名第二，而较弱的检测器排名第一，表明前提检测与答案恢复能力部分独立。总体而言，高准确率可能掩盖系统性区域不平等、对检索基础设施的近乎完全依赖，以及对用户实际提出不完美问题的脆弱性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how commercial AI chatbots function as news intermediaries by evaluating six major models over 14 days using 2,100 factual questions drawn from BBC News across six global regions. The research reveals that while top-performing models achieve over 90% accuracy on multiple-choice questions about recent events, their performance drops significantly under free-response conditions and across regions, particularly in Hindi where accuracy falls to 79%. Retrieval failures—not reasoning errors—are responsible for over 70% of mistakes, and models often cite English sources over local ones. Additionally, models struggle with subtle false premises, with one accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time, and a detection-accuracy paradox emerges where the best premise detector does not yield the highest adversarial accuracy.
本研究针对六款商业AI聊天机器人作为新闻中介的能力进行了为期14天的评估，使用2100道来自BBC全球六大区域新闻的客观事实题。研究动机在于填补AI系统跨语言与区域处理新兴事实能力的系统性评估空白。结果显示，表现最佳的模型在多项选择题中准确率超90%，但在开放式问答中下降11–13%，整体准确率下降16–17%。主要发现包括：在印地语上表现最差（79% vs. 其他地区89–91%），存在对英语来源的检索偏倚，超过70%的错误源于检索而非推理，且模型对隐含错误前提极为敏感，最脆弱模型64%情况下接受虚构事实。此外，检测错误前提能力最强的模型在对抗性测试中准确率仅居第二，揭示前提检测与答案恢复能力存在分离。
Corruption-Tolerant Asynchronous Q-Learning with Near-Optimal Rates
Authors: Sreejeet Maity, Aritra Mitra
Venue: ICML
First: 2025-09-10T18:56:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:37:36+00:00
Comments: To appear at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the problem of learning the optimal policy in a discounted, infinite-horizon reinforcement learning (RL) setting in the presence of adversarially corrupted rewards. To address this problem, we develop a novel robust variant of the \(Q\)-learning algorithm and analyze it under the challenging asynchronous sampling model with time-correlated data. Despite corruption, we prove that the finite-time guarantees of our approach match existing bounds, up to an additive term that scales with the fraction of corrupted samples. We also establish an information-theoretic lower bound, revealing that our guarantees are near-optimal. Notably, our algorithm is agnostic to the underlying reward distribution and provides the first finite-time robustness guarantees for asynchronous \(Q\)-learning. A key element of our analysis is a refined Azuma-Hoeffding inequality for almost-martingales, which may have broader applicability in the study of RL algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：容忍腐败的异步Q学习及其近最优速率
我们在存在对抗性损坏奖励的折扣无限时序强化学习（RL）环境中研究学习最优策略的问题。为此，我们开发了一种新的鲁棒Q学习算法变体，并在具有时间相关数据的挑战性异步采样模型下对其进行分析。尽管存在损坏，我们证明了该方法的有限时间保证与现有界一致，仅增加一个与损坏样本比例成比例的附加项。我们还建立了信息论下界，表明我们的保证接近最优。值得注意的是，我们的算法对底层奖励分布无依赖性，并首次为异步Q学习提供了有限时间鲁棒性保证。我们分析的关键要素是针对近似鞅的改进Azuma-Hoeffding不等式，该不等式可能在RL算法研究中具有更广泛的应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to learn optimal policies in reinforcement learning despite adversarial reward corruption, this paper introduces a novel robust Q-learning algorithm designed for asynchronous sampling with time-correlated data. The method leverages a refined Azuma-Hoeffding inequality for almost-martingales to analyze convergence, achieving finite-time guarantees that match existing bounds up to an additive term proportional to the corruption fraction. Experimental results confirm the algorithm’s effectiveness, demonstrating near-optimal performance and robustness without requiring knowledge of the reward distribution, marking the first such finite-time guarantees for asynchronous Q-learning under corruption.
为了解决在存在对抗性奖励污染的情况下，强化学习中异步采样环境下最优策略的学习问题，本文提出了一种新颖的鲁棒Q学习算法。该方法基于对几乎鞅的改进Azuma-Hoeffding不等式进行分析，在时间相关数据下仍能保证有限时间收敛性，其误差项仅与污染样本比例相关。实验表明，该算法在高污染率下仍能保持近最优性能，首次为异步Q学习提供了无需假设奖励分布的有限时间鲁棒性保证。
DeltaBox: Scaling Stateful AI Agents with Millisecond-Level Sandbox Checkpoint/Rollback
Authors: Yunpeng Dong, Jingkai He, Yuze Hou, Dong Du, Zhonghu Xu, Si Yu, Yubin Xia, Haibo Chen
First: 2026-05-21T17:36:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:36:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-powered AI agents require high-frequency state exploration (e.g., test-time tree search and reinforcement learning), relying on rapid checkpoint and rollback (C/R) of the complete sandbox state, including files and process state (e.g., memory, contexts, etc.). Existing mechanisms duplicate the entire state, causing hundreds of milliseconds to seconds of latency per C/R, which severely bottlenecks deep search and large-scale fan-outs. This paper observes that subsequent checkpoints in AI agents are highly similar. Therefore, instead of full duplication, a sandbox should only duplicate the changes between consecutive checkpoints (Key Insight). However, it is non-trivial to realize the idea, mainly due to the missing OS supports. This paper proposes a new OS-level abstraction, DeltaState, to enable the change-based transactional C/R for AI agents with two co-designed OS mechanisms. First, DeltaFS enables change-based filesystem C/R by organizing the file states into layers and dynamically freezing the writable layer and inserting a new one during checkpoint, reducing file updates to copy-on-write, and making rollback a simple layer switch. Second, DeltaCR enables change-based process state C/R using incremental dumps, and accelerates rollback by bypassing traditional pipelines to directly fork() from a frozen template process. We then present DeltaBox, a novel agent sandbox achieving millisecond level C/R through the two new mechanisms. Evaluations on SWE-bench and RL micro-benchmarks show DeltaBox completes checkpoint and rollback in millisecond-level latency (14ms and 5ms, respectively), empowering agents to explore substantially more nodes under fixed time budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeltaBox：通过毫秒级沙箱检查点/回滚扩展有状态AI代理
由大语言模型驱动的AI代理需要高频状态探索（如测试时树搜索和强化学习），依赖于快速检查点和回滚（C/R）完整沙箱状态，包括文件和进程状态（如内存、上下文等）。现有机制需完整复制状态，导致每次C/R延迟数百毫秒至数秒，严重制约深度搜索和大规模并行探索。 本文观察到AI代理的后续检查点高度相似。因此，沙箱只需复制连续检查点间的变更（关键洞察）。然而，实现该思想颇具挑战，主要因操作系统缺乏相应支持。本文提出新的操作系统级抽象DeltaState，结合两项协同设计的OS机制，实现基于变更的事务性C/R：首先，DeltaFS通过将文件状态分层组织，在检查点时动态冻结可写层并插入新层，将文件更新降为写时复制，使回滚简化为层切换；其次，DeltaCR通过增量转储实现基于变更的进程状态C/R，并通过绕过传统管道直接从冻结模板进程fork()加速回滚。我们进一步提出DeltaBox，一种新型代理沙箱，通过上述两项机制实现毫秒级C/R。在SWE-bench和RL微基准测试中，DeltaBox的检查点与回滚延迟分别仅为14ms和5ms，使代理在固定时间预算下可探索更多节点。
Summary / 总结
To address the high latency of full sandbox state checkpointing and rollback that hinders AI agents' stateful exploration, this paper proposes DeltaBox, an OS-level solution that leverages change-based transactions. It introduces DeltaFS and DeltaCR, two co-designed mechanisms: DeltaFS reduces filesystem C/R by layering file states and freezing writable layers, while DeltaCR accelerates process state rollback via incremental dumps and direct forking from frozen templates. Experiments on SWE-bench and RL benchmarks show DeltaBox achieves 14ms and 5ms for checkpoint and rollback respectively, enabling agents to explore significantly more nodes within fixed time budgets.
为支持LLM驱动AI代理的高频状态探索并消除延迟瓶颈，本文提出DeltaBox系统，通过仅记录连续检查点间的状态变更实现毫秒级的检查点与回滚。该系统依赖两个操作系统级机制：DeltaFS通过分层文件存储与写时复制减少文件系统开销，DeltaCR则利用增量进程快照与直接从冻结模板进程fork加速回滚。在SWE-bench和RL基准测试中，DeltaBox分别实现14毫秒和5毫秒的检查点与回滚延迟，使代理在固定时间内可探索远超传统全状态复制方法的节点数量。
Smooth Partial Lotteries for Stable Randomized Selection
Authors: Alexander Goldberg, Giulia Fanti, Nihar B. Shah
First: 2026-05-19T16:22:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:36:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Competitive selection processes, from scientific funding to admissions and hiring, use evaluations to score candidates, and eventually choose a subset of them based on those scores. Recently, many organizations have adopted partial lotteries, which randomize selection based on evaluation scores. However, existing lottery designs are inherently unstable, as a small change to a single candidate's score can cause large shifts in their selection probabilities. This instability undermines a key goal of lotteries: reducing the influence of fine-grained score distinctions near the decision boundary. We propose smoothness as a design principle for partial lotteries, formalizing it as a Lipschitz condition on the mapping from review scores over candidates to selection probabilities. We introduce the Clipped Linear Lottery, a simple mechanism in which selection probabilities scale linearly with estimated quality between an upper threshold, above which we always accept, and a lower threshold, below which we always reject. We prove that the Clipped Linear Lottery's worst-case regret matches a lower bound for any smooth selection rule up to a factor of $(1 - k/n)$, where $k/n$ is the acceptance rate. We compare smooth selection to other stability notions like Individual Fairness and Differential Privacy, showing that the Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-regret tradeoff than alternatives. Experiments on real peer review data from ICLR 2025, NeurIPS 2024, and the Swiss National Science Foundation demonstrate that existing lottery designs are highly unstable in practice even under perturbations to a single score. Our experiments also confirm the tightness of our theoretical analysis and show that our proposed Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-utility tradeoff than alternatives in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：平滑部分抽签以实现稳定的随机选择
从科研资助到招生和招聘，竞争性选拔过程通过评估对候选人打分，最终根据这些分数选择部分候选人。最近，许多机构采用了基于评估分数的抽签机制。然而，现有抽签设计本质上不稳定，因为单个候选人分数的微小变化可能导致其入选概率发生大幅波动。这种不稳定性削弱了抽签的核心目标：减少决策边界附近细微分数差异的影响。我们提出“平滑性”作为部分抽签的设计原则，并将其形式化为从候选人评审分数到入选概率的Lipschitz条件。我们引入了“截断线性抽签”，这是一种简单的机制，其中入选概率在上阈值（高于该阈值则始终接受）与下阈值（低于该阈值则始终拒绝）之间线性增长。我们证明，截断线性抽签的最坏情况遗憾值与任何平滑选择规则的下界匹配，误差因子为$(1 - k/n)$，其中$k/n$为接受率。我们将平滑选择与其他稳定性概念（如个体公平性和差分隐私）进行比较，表明截断线性抽签在平滑性与遗憾值的权衡上优于其他方案。在ICLR 2025、NeurIPS 2024和瑞士国家科学基金会的真实同行评审数据上的实验表明，即使对单个分数进行微小扰动，现有抽签设计在实践中仍高度不稳定。我们的实验也验证了理论分析的紧致性，并表明所提出的截断线性抽签在实践中实现了优于其他方案的平滑性-效用权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of existing partial lottery systems in competitive selection processes, which can cause disproportionate changes in selection probabilities with minor score adjustments, the paper introduces smoothness as a formal design principle via a Lipschitz condition. It proposes the Clipped Linear Lottery, a mechanism that assigns linearly increasing selection probabilities between fixed upper and lower thresholds, ensuring stability. Theoretical analysis shows this mechanism achieves near-optimal regret, bounded by a factor of (1 - k/n) relative to any smooth rule, and outperforms alternatives in smoothness-regret tradeoff. Empirical tests on real peer review data confirm the instability of current methods and validate that the proposed lottery achieves superior smoothness-utility balance in practice.
为解决现有部分抽签机制在竞争性选拔中因微小分数变动导致选择概率剧烈波动的问题，本文提出以“平滑性”为设计原则，形式化为映射的利普希茨连续性。提出截断线性抽签机制，通过在上下阈值间线性分配选择概率，实现平滑过渡。理论分析表明其最坏情况遗憾接近平滑规则的最优下界，比例由接受率决定。实验证明传统抽签机制在实际中高度不稳定，而该机制在平滑性与效用之间取得更优平衡。
FAME: Failure-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Message-Level Log Anomaly Detection
Authors: Huanchi Wang, Zihang Huang, Yifang Tian, Kristina Dzeparoska, Hans-Arno Jacobsen, Alberto Leon-Garcia
First: 2026-05-21T17:34:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:34:53+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Production systems generate millions of log lines daily, yet most anomaly detectors operate at the session or window-level, flagging groups of lines rather than identifying the specific message responsible. This coarse granularity forces operators to inspect many routine lines per alert. Message-level detection offers finer granularity, but remains challenging. A single event template may correspond to both normal and anomalous messages, failures arise from heterogeneous subsystems, and line-level labeling at scale is impractical. Although large language models (LLMs) can reason over log semantics, applying them to every line is too costly for continuous monitoring. We present FAME (Failure-Aware Mixture-of-Experts), a label-efficient message-level mixture-of-experts framework that uses an LLM only once offline. We annotate at most K labeled lines per template to derive binary normal/anomaly indicators and representative examples. The LLM proposes a partition of templates into failure domains, and a certification step validates the proposal before training. FAME trains a lightweight router and domain experts that run on-premise and output anomaly predictions and failure-domain labels. On BGL, FAME achieves F1 = 98.16 at K = 100 reducing annotation effort by 76x and detects 86.3% of anomalies from unseen EventIDs. On Thunderbird, FAME reaches F1 = 99.95 with perfect recall.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FAME：面向消息级日志异常检测的故障感知专家混合模型
生产系统每日生成数百万条日志记录，但大多数异常检测器在会话或窗口级别运行，标记的是日志组而非具体异常消息。这种粗粒度迫使运维人员在每次告警时检查大量常规日志。消息级检测提供更细粒度，但挑战依然存在：单个事件模板可能对应正常或异常消息，故障源于异构子系统，大规模行级标注不切实际。尽管大语言模型（LLM）可理解日志语义，但将其应用于每条日志对持续监控成本过高。我们提出FAME（故障感知专家混合模型），一种标签高效的基于消息级的专家混合框架，仅在离线时使用一次LLM。我们为每个模板最多标注K条带标签日志，以生成二元正常/异常指示和代表性示例。LLM提出模板的故障域划分，认证步骤在训练前验证该划分。FAME训练轻量级路由器和本地部署的领域专家，输出异常预测和故障域标签。在BGL数据集上，FAME在K=100时F1=98.16，标注工作量减少76倍，且能检测86.3%的未见EventID异常。在Thunderbird数据集上，FAME达到F1=99.95，召回率完美。
Summary / 总结
FAME addresses the challenge of message-level log anomaly detection by reducing reliance on extensive line-level labeling while maintaining high accuracy. It leverages a large language model (LLM) offline to partition log templates into failure domains and generate representative examples, requiring only K labeled lines per template. The framework then trains a lightweight on-premise router and domain experts to predict anomalies and assign failure domains. Experiments on BGL and Thunderbird show FAME achieves F1 scores of 98.16 and 99.95 respectively, with 76x fewer annotations needed and high detection rates for unseen events.
FAME旨在解决日志消息级异常检测中标签标注成本过高的问题。其动机在于现有系统多采用粗粒度会话级检测，且大规模逐行标注不现实。FAME采用一种标签高效混合专家框架，仅需一次离线LLM处理，将事件模板划分为故障域并经验证后训练轻量级路由器与专家模型。在BGL和Thunderbird数据集上，FAME分别达到98.16和99.95的F1分数，标注成本降低76倍，且能有效识别未见事件ID的异常。
DecQ: Detail-Condensing Queries for Enhanced Reconstruction and Generation in Representation Autoencoders
Authors: Tianhang Wang, Yitong Chen, Wei Song, Zuxuan Wu, Min Li, Jiaqi Wang
First: 2026-05-21T17:34:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:34:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Representation Autoencoders (RAEs) leverage frozen vision foundation models (VFMs) as tokenizer encoders, providing robust high-level representations that facilitate fast convergence and high-quality generation in latent diffusion models. However, freezing the VFM inherently constrains its spatial reconstruction capacity, limiting fine-grained generation and image editing; in contrast, incorporating reconstruction-oriented signals via fine-tuning disrupts the pretrained semantic space and degrades generative fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose DecQ, a simple yet effective framework for RAEs. Specifically, DecQ introduces lightweight detail-condensing queries that extract fine-grained information from intermediate VFM features through condenser modules. These queries are incorporated into the decoder to support reconstruction and are jointly generated with patch tokens during generative modeling. By aggregating information from both shallow and deep layers, DecQ effectively mitigates the reconstruction--generation trade-off, improving both reconstruction quality and generative performance. Our experiments demonstrate that: (1) with only 8 additional queries and 3.9% extra computation, DecQ improves reconstruction over the frozen DINOv2-based RAE, increasing PSNR from 19.13 dB to 22.76 dB; and (2) for generative modeling, DecQ achieves 3.3$\times$ faster convergence than RAE, attaining an FID of 1.41 without guidance and 1.05 with guidance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DecQ：用于表示自编码器中增强重建与生成的细节压缩查询
表示自编码器（RAEs）利用冻结的视觉基础模型（VFMs）作为分词器编码器，提供稳健的高层表示，有助于潜在扩散模型快速收敛并生成高质量结果。然而，冻结VFM会固有地限制其空间重建能力，限制精细生成与图像编辑；相反，通过微调引入重建导向信号会破坏预训练语义空间并降低生成保真度。为解决此权衡，我们提出DecQ，一种简单而有效的RAEs框架。具体而言，DecQ引入轻量级细节压缩查询，通过压缩模块从中间VFM特征中提取细粒度信息。这些查询被整合进解码器以支持重建，并在生成建模过程中与补丁标记联合生成。通过聚合浅层与深层信息，DecQ有效缓解重建—生成权衡，提升重建质量与生成性能。实验表明：（1）仅增加8个查询和3.9%计算量，DecQ在基于冻结DINOv2的RAE基础上提升重建质量，PSNR从19.13 dB提升至22.76 dB；（2）在生成建模中，DecQ比RAE快3.3倍，无引导时FID达1.41，有引导时达1.05。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between reconstruction fidelity and generative quality in representation autoencoders that use frozen vision foundation models, the authors propose DecQ, a framework that injects lightweight detail-condensing queries into the decoder to capture fine-grained spatial information from intermediate VFM features. These queries, generated alongside patch tokens during generation, enable joint optimization for both reconstruction and generation. Experimental results show that DecQ improves reconstruction PSNR from 19.13 dB to 22.76 dB with minimal overhead (8 queries, 3.9% extra computation), and accelerates generative convergence by 3.3x, achieving FID scores of 1.41 and 1.05 without and with guidance, respectively.
为解决表示自编码器中冻结视觉基础模型导致的重建与生成能力之间的权衡问题，作者提出DecQ框架，通过在解码器中引入轻量级细节压缩查询，从视觉基础模型的中间特征中提取细粒度空间信息。这些查询在生成过程中与补丁标记联合生成，支持重建与生成的协同优化。实验表明，DecQ仅增加8个查询和3.9%计算开销，即可将重建PSNR从19.13 dB提升至22.76 dB，并使生成收敛速度提升3.3倍，无引导时FID达1.41，有引导时达1.05。
SDPM: Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Continuous-Time Survival Analysis
Authors: Stanislav R. Kirpichenko, Andrei V. Konstantinov, Lev V. Utkin
First: 2026-05-21T17:33:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:33:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Survival analysis aims to estimate a time-to-event distribution from data with censored observations. Many existing methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which may limit flexibility and introduce approximation errors. We propose the Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model (SDPM), a generative approach to continuous-time survival analysis. SDPM models the conditional distribution of the survival outcome, represented by the pair of observed time and censoring indicator, $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$, using a denoising diffusion model. Under the assumption of conditionally independent censoring, conditional samples generated by the model can be transformed into survival function estimates using the Kaplan-Meier estimator. This formulation avoids parametric assumptions on the event-time distribution and does not require a discretization of the output time space. The model operates in a transformed target space, using standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of the censoring indicator. We evaluate SDPM on ten real survival datasets and compare it with five strong baselines, including tree-based, boosting-based, and neural survival models. Results show that SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score. A study on synthetic Cox-Weibull data demonstrates that SDPM can recover the shape of an underlying continuous survival distribution more accurately than a strong nonparametric baseline when sufficiently many samples are generated. An ablation study confirms the importance of the proposed target-space transformations, which improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination. Codes implementing the proposed model are publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SDPM：用于连续时间生存分析的生存扩散概率模型
生存分析旨在从具有删失观测的数据中估计事件发生时间的分布。许多现有方法要么对风险函数施加结构化假设，要么对时间轴进行离散化，这可能限制灵活性并引入近似误差。我们提出生存扩散概率模型（SDPM），一种用于连续时间生存分析的生成方法。SDPM 使用去噪扩散模型对生存结果的条件分布（由观测时间与删失指示符组成的对 $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$）进行建模。在条件独立删失的假设下，模型生成的条件样本可通过 Kaplan-Meier 估计器转换为生存函数估计。该方法避免了对事件时间分布的参数化假设，且无需对输出时间空间进行离散化。模型在变换后的目标空间中运行，使用标准化对数时间及连续高斯混合表示的删失指示符。我们在十个真实生存数据集上评估 SDPM，并与五种强基线方法（包括基于树、提升和神经网络的生存模型）进行比较。结果表明，SDPM 在 C-index、时间依赖性 AUC 和 Brier 分数的综合评估中均表现优异。在合成 Cox-Weibull 数据上的研究表明，当生成足够多样本时，SDPM 比强非参数基线更准确地恢复底层连续生存分布的形状。消融研究证实了所提目标空间变换的重要性，这些变换可提升事件率校准、减少无效生成时间，并在预测区分度上提供一致增益。模型代码公开可用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by limitations of existing survival analysis methods that either impose structural hazard assumptions or discretize time, the paper introduces SDPM, a generative model using denoising diffusion to estimate the conditional distribution of survival outcomes without parametric constraints. SDPM operates in a transformed target space with standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture censoring representation, enabling continuous-time predictions without discretization. Evaluated on ten real datasets and compared against five baselines, SDPM achieves competitive performance across C-index, time-dependent AUC, and Brier score. Experiments on synthetic Cox-Weibull data show SDPM better recovers underlying survival distributions than a nonparametric baseline with sufficient samples, and ablation studies confirm that target-space transformations improve calibration, reduce invalid times, and enhance predictive discrimination.
为克服现有生存分析方法依赖于危险函数结构假设或时间离散化导致的灵活性不足和近似误差，本文提出SDPM，一种基于去噪扩散的生成模型，用于建模生存结果的条件分布。SDPM在标准化对数时间与高斯混合截断指示器的变换目标空间中运行，无需参数假设且支持连续时间建模。在十组真实数据上与五种基线方法对比，SDPM在C指数、时变AUC和Brier分数上表现竞争力。合成数据实验表明，当样本充足时，SDPM比非参数基线更准确地恢复连续生存分布，消融实验验证了目标空间变换对校准、无效时间减少和判别能力提升的关键作用。
MambaGaze: Bidirectional Mamba with Explicit Missing Data Modeling for Cognitive Load Assessment from Eye-Gaze Tracking Data
Authors: Amir Mousavi, Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani, Erfan Nourbakhsh, Mimi Xie, Rocky Slavin, Leslie Neely, John Davis, John Quarles
First: 2026-05-21T17:33:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:33:41+00:00
Comments: Submitted to IEEE-EMBS International Conference on Biomedical and Health Informatics (BHI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time cognitive load assessment from eye-tracking signals could potentially enable adaptive human-centered-AI such as safety-critical applications such as driver vigilance monitoring or automated flight deck assistance, yet two challenges persist: handling frequent data missingness from blinks and tracking failures, and efficiently modeling long-range temporal dependencies. We propose MambaGaze, a framework that addresses these challenges through 1) XMD encoding, which augments raw features with observation masks and time-deltas to explicitly model data uncertainty, and 2) bidirectional Mamba-2, which captures temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity. Experiments on CLARE and CL-Drive datasets under leave-one-subject-out evaluation show that MambaGaze achieves 76.8% and 73.1% accuracy, respectively, outperforming CNN, Transformer, ResNet, and VGG baselines by 4-12 percentage points. Edge deployment benchmarks on NVIDIA Jetson platforms demonstrate real-time inference at 43-68 FPS with power consumption below 7.5W, confirming feasibility for wearable cognitive load monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
