从眼动信号实时评估认知负荷，有望实现如驾驶员警觉监控或自动飞行舱辅助等安全关键的人机协同AI系统，但面临两大挑战：因眨眼和跟踪失败导致的频繁数据缺失，以及高效建模长时序依赖关系。本文提出MambaGaze框架，通过1）XMD编码，将观测掩码与时间差附加至原始特征，显式建模数据不确定性；2）双向Mamba-2，以线性计算复杂度捕捉时序依赖。在CLARE与CL-Drive数据集上，采用留一受试者验证，MambaGaze分别达到76.8%和73.1%准确率，较CNN、Transformer、ResNet和VGG基线高出4-12个百分点。在NVIDIA Jetson平台边缘部署测试中，实现43-68 FPS实时推理，功耗低于7.5W，证实其适用于可穿戴认知负荷监测。

CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation
Authors: Amir Mousavi, Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani, Erfan Nourbakhsh, Mimi Xie, Rocky Slavin, Leslie Neely, John Davis, John Quarles
First: 2026-05-21T17:33:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:33:35+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 7 figures. Submitted to IEEE-EMBS International Conference on Biomedical and Health Informatics (BHI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.
中文标题/摘要

Deep Reinforcement Learning for Flexible Job Shop Scheduling with Random Job Arrivals
Authors: Yu Tang, Muhammad Zakwan, Efe Balta, John Lygeros, Alisa Rupenyan
First: 2026-05-21T17:33:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:33:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Flexible Job Shop Scheduling Problem (FJSP) is the optimal allocation of a set of jobs to machines. Two primary challenges persist in FJSP: the unpredictable arrival of future jobs and the combinatorial complexity of the problem, rendering it intractable for conventional mixed-integer linear programming solvers. This paper proposes an event-based \gls{DRL} approach to solve FJSP with random job arrivals. Specifically, we employ the Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm and use lightweight Multi-Layer Perceptrons to train the \gls{DRL} agent for minimizing the total completion time of all jobs. We design the state representation to be directly accessible from the environment, and limit the learning agent to selecting from among a set of well-established dispatching rules. Simulations show that our \gls{DRL} approach outperforms any of the individual dispatching rules on datasets with varying heterogeneity and job arrival rates. We benchmark our \gls{DRL} against an arrival-triggered mixed-integer linear programming solution and show that our method achieves good performance especially when the datasets are heterogeneous.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向随机作业到达的柔性作业车间调度的深度强化学习方法
柔性作业车间调度问题（FJSP）是将一组作业最优分配至机器。FJSP存在两个主要挑战：未来作业的不可预测到达和问题的组合复杂性，使其难以被传统混合整数线性规划求解器处理。本文提出一种基于事件的深度强化学习（DRL）方法，用于解决随机作业到达的FJSP。具体而言，我们采用近端策略优化算法，并使用轻量级多层感知机训练DRL智能体，以最小化所有作业的总完成时间。我们设计状态表示可直接从环境中获取，并限制学习智能体仅从一组成熟的调度规则中选择。仿真表明，我们的DRL方法在不同异质性和作业到达率的数据集上均优于任何单一调度规则。我们将其与基于到达触发的混合整数线性规划解法进行对比，结果表明，当数据集异质性较高时，我们的方法表现尤为出色。

The agent learns to select from a set of established dispatching rules using state representations directly derived from the environment. Experimental results demonstrate that the DRL method consistently outperforms individual dispatching rules across varying job arrival rates and dataset heterogeneity, and achieves competitive performance compared to an arrival-triggered mixed-integer linear programming solver, especially in heterogeneous scenarios.
为应对柔性作业车间调度中作业随机到达与组合爆炸带来的挑战，本文提出一种基于深度强化学习的解决方案，采用近端策略优化算法与轻量级多层感知机，以最小化总完工时间。智能体从预设的调度规则集中选择动作，其状态表示直接来源于环境。实验表明，该方法在不同作业异质性和到达率下均优于单一调度规则，并在异构数据集上与基于事件触发的混合整数线性规划方法相比表现优异。
Reducing Political Manipulation with Consistency Training
Authors: Long Phan, Devin Kim, Alexander Pan, Alice Blair, Adam Khoja, Dan Hendrycks
First: 2026-05-21T17:32:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:32:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic political bias across a variety of sensitive contexts. We find that LLMs handle counterpart topics from opposing political sides asymmetrically. We refer to this phenomenon as covert political bias and identify 7 categories of techniques through which it operates. We propose two metrics for covert bias: Sentiment Consistency measures symmetry in rhetoric and framing across paired political prompts; Helpfulness Consistency measures symmetric depth and engagement. To reduce both types of covert bias, we introduce Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL training method with two complementary paradigms: Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training. We show that PCT preserves overall helpfulness, substantially reduces covert political bias, and generalizes to held-out benchmarks. We release our work at https://political-manipulation.ai
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过一致性训练减少政治操控
大型语言模型（LLMs）在多种敏感情境中表现出系统性的政治偏见。我们发现LLMs对来自对立政治立场的对应主题处理存在不对称性，我们将此现象称为隐性政治偏见，并识别出其运作的7类技术。我们提出两种衡量隐性偏见的指标：情感一致性衡量配对政治提示在修辞和框架上的对称性；帮助性一致性衡量对称的深度和参与度。为减少这两种隐性偏见，我们引入政治一致性训练（PCT），这是一种结合情感一致性训练与帮助性一致性训练两种互补范式的强化学习训练方法。我们证明PCT在保持整体帮助性的同时，显著降低隐性政治偏见，并能泛化至未见基准测试。我们的工作已发布于https://political-manipulation.ai
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the discovery that large language models exhibit asymmetric handling of politically opposing topics—referred to as covert political bias—the authors propose Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL-based method combining Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training to enforce symmetric rhetoric and engagement across political prompts. Experimental results demonstrate that PCT significantly reduces covert bias without compromising overall helpfulness and generalizes effectively to unseen benchmarks.
鉴于大语言模型在处理对立政治话题时表现出系统性不对称，作者提出政治一致性训练（PCT），通过强化情绪一致性与帮助性一致性两个强化学习范式，使模型在政治对立语境下保持语义与回应深度的对称。实验证明，PCT能显著降低隐蔽政治偏见，同时保持整体有用性，并在未见测试集上表现良好。
Understanding Data Temporality Impact on Large Language Models Pre-training
Authors: Pilchen Hippolyte, Fabre Romain, Signe Talla Franck, Perez Patrick, Grave Edouard
First: 2026-05-21T17:31:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:31:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are typically trained on shuffled corpora, yielding models whose knowledge is frozen at train time and whose temporal grounding remains poorly understood. In this work, we study the impact of pre-training dynamics on the acquisition of time-sensitive factual knowledge, focusing specifically on data ordering. Our main contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a comprehensive benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and an evaluation protocol that enables analysis of whether models correctly associate facts with their corresponding time periods. Second, we pretrain 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots and compare them against standard shuffled pre-training. Our results show that sequentially trained models match shuffled baselines on general language understanding and common knowledge while consistently exhibiting more up-to-date and temporally precise knowledge. Temporally ordered pre-training yields improved factual freshness, while shuffled pre-training peaks on older data, possibly due to increased factual repetition. These findings, along with the release of our code at https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos , checkpoints, and datasets at https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos provide a foundation for future research on continual learning for LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：理解数据时序性对大语言模型预训练的影响
大语言模型（LLMs）通常在打乱的数据语料上进行训练，导致模型知识在训练时即被冻结，其时间锚定能力也缺乏深入理解。本研究探讨预训练动态对时间敏感事实知识获取的影响，重点聚焦数据排序。主要贡献有两点：首先，我们构建了一个包含7000多个时间锚定问题的综合基准，并设计评估协议，用于分析模型是否能正确将事实与其对应的时间段关联；其次，我们在按时间顺序排列的Common Crawl快照上预训练6B参数模型，并与标准打乱预训练进行对比。结果表明，顺序训练模型在通用语言理解和常识方面与打乱基线相当，但在知识时效性和时间精度上表现更优。时间顺序预训练提升了事实的新鲜度，而打乱预训练则在较旧数据上表现更佳，可能源于事实重复率更高。本研究结果及代码（https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos）、检查点和数据集（https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos）为未来LLM持续学习研究奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how the temporal ordering of training data affects large language models' acquisition of time-sensitive factual knowledge, motivated by the observation that standard shuffled pre-training yields models with poorly understood temporal grounding. The authors introduce a benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and pre-train 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots, contrasting them with shuffled pre-training baselines. Results show that sequentially trained models perform comparably on general language tasks but consistently demonstrate more up-to-date and temporally precise factual knowledge, while shuffled models peak on older data due to increased repetition.
该研究探讨了训练数据的时间顺序如何影响大语言模型获取时敏感事实知识的能力，动机源于标准打乱预训练导致模型时间锚定模糊的问题。作者构建了包含7000多道时间锚定问题的基准测试，并在时序排列的Common Crawl数据上预训练6B参数模型，与打乱预训练基线进行对比。结果表明，时序训练模型在通用语言任务上表现相当，但在事实更新性和时间精度上显著优于打乱训练模型，而打乱训练模型在旧事实表现上更优，可能因重复学习所致。
Synthetic Data Alone is Enough? Rethinking Data Scarcity in Pediatric Rare Disease Recognition
Authors: Ganlin Feng, Yuxi Long, Erin Lou, Lianghong Chen, Zihao Jing, Pingzhao Hu, Wei Xu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-21T17:28:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:28:11+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 CV4CHL workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Children with rare genetic diseases often exhibit distinctive facial phenotypes, yet developing computer vision systems for early diagnosis remains challenging due to extreme data scarcity, privacy constraints, and limited data sharing in pediatric settings. These challenges not only hinder automated diagnosis but also restrict the availability of visual resources for clinical genetic counseling. While prior work has shown that synthetic data can augment real datasets and preserve phenotype-level semantics, it remains unclear whether synthetic data alone is sufficient for learning in ultra-low-resource pediatric settings. In this work, we study the synthetic-only regime for pediatric rare disease recognition. Under a controlled experimental setup, models are trained exclusively on phenotype-aware synthetic facial images at increasing scales. We find that synthetic-only training achieves performance comparable to real-data-only baselines at sufficient scale across multiple backbones, suggesting that high-fidelity synthetic data can approximate clinically meaningful distributions. These findings together further enable the use of synthetic pediatric facial images as privacy-preserving resources for genetic education and counseling, supporting clinician training and patient communication. Our results highlight the potential of computer vision to improve data efficiency and expand accessible visual tools in children's healthcare.
中文标题/摘要
标题：仅靠合成数据就足够了吗？重新思考儿科罕见病识别中的数据稀缺问题
罕见遗传病患儿常表现出独特的面部表型，但由于数据极度稀缺、隐私限制及儿科领域数据共享有限，开发用于早期诊断的计算机视觉系统仍具挑战性。这些挑战不仅阻碍自动化诊断，也限制了临床遗传咨询中视觉资源的可用性。尽管已有研究表明合成数据可增强真实数据集并保留表型层面语义，但尚不清楚合成数据是否足以在极端低资源的儿科环境中实现学习。本研究探讨了仅使用合成数据的儿科罕见病识别方案。在受控实验设置下，模型仅在表型感知的合成面部图像上训练，且随数据规模递增。我们发现，在足够规模下，仅合成数据训练的性能可媲美仅真实数据训练的基线模型，多个骨干网络均表现一致，表明高保真合成数据可近似临床有意义的分布。这些发现进一步支持将合成儿科面部图像作为隐私保护的遗传教育与咨询资源，助力临床医生培训与患者沟通。我们的结果凸显了计算机视觉在提升数据效率、扩展儿童医疗中可及视觉工具方面的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the severe data scarcity and privacy constraints in pediatric rare disease diagnosis, this study investigates whether synthetic facial images alone can enable effective computer vision models for rare disease recognition. Using a controlled setup, models were trained exclusively on synthetic, phenotype-aware facial data at increasing scales across multiple architectures. Results show that synthetic-only training achieves performance comparable to real-data-only baselines when sufficient scale is reached, indicating that high-fidelity synthetic data can approximate clinically relevant distributions. This supports the use of synthetic data as a privacy-preserving tool for clinical education and counseling in pediatric genetics.
鉴于儿科罕见病诊断面临数据稀缺与隐私限制的挑战，本研究探讨仅使用合成面部图像是否足以支持罕见病识别的计算机视觉模型。在受控实验中，模型仅在不同规模的表型感知合成数据上训练，跨多个骨干网络进行评估。结果表明，在足够数据规模下，合成数据训练的性能可媲美真实数据训练基线，说明高保真合成数据能近似临床相关分布，从而支持隐私保护的遗传教育与咨询工具在儿科医疗中的应用。
Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation
Authors: Samson Gourevitch, Yazid Janati, Dario Shariatian, Umut Simsekli, Eric Moulines, Eric P. Xing, Alain Durmus
First: 2026-05-21T17:27:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:27:19+00:00
Comments: preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor. We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：均匀扩散模型再探：留一法去噪器与吸收态重述
离散扩散模型常通过干净数据预测进行训练，但预测可用于定义逆动态的不同方式。在掩码扩散模型（MDM）中，这些选择基本一致，而在均匀扩散模型（UDM）中则不然。我们证明，UDM的标准插值桥参数化并非由去噪后验优化，而是由留一法后验优化，该后验预测每个干净标记时不使用其自身的噪声观测。这揭示了插值ELBO与常用交叉熵去噪目标之间的不匹配。我们刻画了留一法目标，并推导出去噪器、留一法后验与分数之间的精确转换。这些转换使我们能够分离参数化与训练目标。我们的结果还通过有信息的预测-校正采样器和基于留一法预测的改进温度采样，在无需额外训练的情况下提升推理性能。 我们进一步引入了均匀扩散的吸收态重述，该重述在保持UDM联合分布的同时，将其分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作，具有更简单的去噪后验、携带式去掩码和自然的重掩码机制。在语言建模中，留一法参数化持续提升UDM生成效果，而吸收态构造则与掩码扩散相当或更优。这些结果表明，掩码与均匀扩散之间的经验差距，更多由参数化与采样设计驱动，而非边际分布本身的选择。代码与模型见 https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between standard UDM parameterizations and denoising objectives, this work revisits uniform diffusion models by introducing a leave-one-out denoiser that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation, revealing that the plug-in ELBO is suboptimal. The authors derive exact conversions between denoiser, leave-one-out posterior, and score, enabling decoupling of parameterization from training objective. Experimentally, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, and an absorbing-state reformulation yields sampling efficiency and performance matching or exceeding masked diffusion, suggesting that design choices in parameterization and sampling—not marginal distributions—drive the empirical gap between diffusion variants.
本文重新审视了均匀扩散模型（UDM），指出其标准插值ELBO目标与训练中使用的去噪后验不匹配。作者提出一种“留一法”去噪器，即在预测每个干净token时不使用其自身的噪声观测，发现标准参数化效果不佳。他们推导出去噪器、留一后验与分数之间的精确转换关系，从而实现参数化与训练目标的解耦。实验表明，留一法参数化显著提升UDM生成效果，而引入的吸收态重写结构——将UDM分解为类似掩码扩散的采样步骤——在语言建模任务中表现与掩码扩散相当甚至更优，说明性能差异主要源于采样与参数化设计，而非扩散结构本身。
SONIC: Supersizing Motion Tracking for Natural Humanoid Whole-Body Control
Authors: Zhengyi Luo, Ye Yuan, Tingwu Wang, Chenran Li, Fernando Castañeda, Sirui Chen, Zi-Ang Cao, Jiefeng Li, David Minor, Qingwei Ben, Jinhyung Park, David Sami, Zi Wang, Xingye Da, Runyu Ding, Cyrus Hogg, Lina Song, Edy Lim, Eugene Jeong, Tairan He, Haoru Xue, Wenli Xiao, Simon Yuen, Jan Kautz, Yan Chang, Umar Iqbal, Linxi "Jim" Fan, Yuke Zhu
First: 2025-11-11T04:37:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:26:49+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://nvlabs.github.io/SONIC/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Despite the rise of billion-parameter foundation models trained across thousands of GPUs, similar scaling gains have not been shown for humanoid control. Current neural controllers for humanoids remain modest in size, target a limited set of behaviors, and are trained on a handful of GPUs. We show that scaling model capacity, data, and compute yields a generalist humanoid controller capable of natural, robust whole-body movements. We position motion tracking as a scalable task for humanoid control, leveraging dense supervision from diverse motion-capture data to acquire human motion priors without manual reward engineering. We build a foundation model for motion tracking by scaling along three axes: network size (1.2M to 42M parameters), dataset volume (100M+ frames from 700 hours of motion capture), and compute (21k GPU hours). Beyond demonstrating the benefits of scale, we further show downstream utility through: (1) a real-time kinematic planner bridging motion tracking to tasks such as navigation, enabling natural and interactive control, and (2) a unified token space supporting VR teleoperation and vision-language-action (VLA) models with a single policy. Through this interface, we demonstrate autonomous VLA-driven whole-body loco-manipulation requiring coordinated hand and foot placement. Scaling motion tracking exhibits favorable properties: performance improves steadily with compute and data diversity, and learned policies generalize to unseen motions, establishing motion tracking at scale as a practical foundation for humanoid control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SONIC：用于自然人形全身控制的超大规模运动追踪
尽管千亿参数的基础模型在数千个GPU上训练已取得显著进展，但人形控制领域尚未展现出类似的规模收益。当前的人形神经控制器规模较小，仅针对有限行为，且在少数GPU上训练。我们证明，通过扩展模型容量、数据量和计算资源，可构建一个通用的人形控制器，实现自然且稳健的全身运动。我们将运动追踪定位为可扩展的人形控制任务，利用多样化的动作捕捉数据提供的密集监督，无需手动奖励工程即可获取人类运动先验。我们通过三个维度扩展构建运动追踪基础模型：网络规模（120万至4200万参数）、数据量（700小时动作捕捉数据，超1亿帧）和计算资源（2.1万GPU小时）。除展示规模收益外，我们进一步通过以下方式体现其下游实用性：（1）实时运动规划器，将运动追踪与导航等任务衔接，实现自然交互式控制；（2）统一的令牌空间，支持VR远程操作及视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，仅需单一策略。通过该接口，我们展示了自主VLA驱动的全身运动与操作，需协调手部与脚部放置。运动追踪的扩展展现出良好特性：性能随计算量和数据多样性稳步提升，所学策略可泛化至未见动作，确立了大规模运动追踪作为人形控制实用基础的地位。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of scaling benefits in humanoid control despite advances in large foundation models, the authors propose SONIC, a scalable motion tracking foundation model for natural humanoid whole-body control. They train a neural controller by scaling network size from 1.2M to 42M parameters, using over 100M motion capture frames from 700 hours of data, and 21k GPU hours. The model learns human motion priors from dense supervision without reward engineering, enabling generalization to unseen motions. Experimental results show steady performance gains with increased compute and data diversity, and the model supports real-time kinematic planning and unified token spaces for VR teleoperation and vision-language-action tasks, demonstrating autonomous whole-body locomotion and manipulation with coordinated hand-foot placement.
鉴于尽管基础模型在参数规模和计算资源上取得显著进展，人形机器人控制领域尚未实现类似规模收益，本研究提出SONIC方法，通过扩展网络规模（1.2M至42M参数）、训练数据量（超1亿帧，来自700小时动作捕捉）和计算资源（21000 GPU小时），构建可扩展的动作追踪基础模型。该方法利用密集的动作捕捉数据监督，无需人工奖励设计即可学习人类动作先验。实验表明，性能随计算与数据多样性稳定提升，且模型能泛化至未见过的动作。下游应用包括实时运动规划以支持导航，以及统一的令牌空间实现VR远程操作与视觉-语言-动作控制，成功实现自主全身运动与协调手足操作。
Bridging Silicon and the Hippocampus: Algebro-Deterministic Memory "VaCoAl" as a Substrate for Vector-HaSH and TEM
Authors: Hiroyuki Chuma, Kanji Otsuka, Yoichi Sato
First: 2026-05-15T06:13:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:25:43+00:00
Comments: 52 pages, 5 figures, 1 table, 3 appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vector-HaSH and the Tolman-Eichenbaum Machine (TEM) propose the hippocampal-entorhinal circuit factorizes memory via a grid-cell scaffold for compositional replay. Concurrently, human iEEG shows sharp-wave ripples gate recall and multi-hop replay fidelity decays multiplicatively. Yet, these fields lack a shared algebraic foundation. We introduce VaCoAl, an algebro-deterministic hyperdimensional memory architecture built on Galois-field linear-feedback shift registers. Its deterministic Galois-field diffusion offers a substrate-level alternative to Vector-HaSH's random projections, matching quasi-orthogonality while ensuring bit-exact reproducibility. Furthermore, the path-integral Confidence Ratio CR2 provides an algebraically tractable model for the empirically observed multiplicative replay decay. Biologically, VaCoAl's two operating regimes align with the EC-CA3 direct and EC-DG-CA3 trisynaptic pathways, explaining their 520-Myr conservation. Independent cellular evidence supports that the DG-CA3 pathway implements a biophysical homologue of Galois-field arithmetic. We also link this framework to Judea Pearl's Ladder of Causation. Reversible GF(2) binding provides the surgical algebra for the do-operator (Rung 2), and VaCoAl's dual-orthogonalizer architecture supplies the parallel substrate required for counterfactual reasoning (Rung 3). Ultimately, we prove these formal correspondences and derive testable iEEG predictions, uniting computational neuroscience, electrophysiology, and hyperdimensional computing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：连接硅与海马体：代数确定性记忆“VaCoAl”作为向量-HaSH与TEM的基质
向量-HaSH与托尔曼-艾肯鲍姆机器（TEM）提出，海马体-内嗅皮层回路通过网格细胞骨架实现记忆的因式分解与组合性重放。同时，人类iEEG显示尖波涟漪调控回忆，多跳重放的保真度呈乘法衰减。然而，这两个领域缺乏共同的代数基础。我们引入VaCoAl，一种基于伽罗瓦域线性反馈移位寄存器构建的代数确定性超维记忆架构。其确定性伽罗瓦域扩散机制，为向量-HaSH的随机投影提供了一种基质级替代方案，兼具准正交性与比特级可复现性。此外，路径积分置信度比CR2提供了一个代数可处理的模型，用于解释实证观察到的乘法重放衰减。在生物学上，VaCoAl的两种工作模式与EC-CA3直接通路和EC-DG-CA3三突触通路相对应，解释了其5.2亿年进化保守性。独立的细胞层面证据支持DG-CA3通路实现了伽罗瓦域算术的生物物理同源物。我们还将此框架与朱迪亚·珀尔的因果阶梯相联系：可逆GF(2)结合提供了do算子（第二阶）的外科手术式代数，而VaCoAl的双正交化架构则提供了反事实推理（第三阶）所需的并行基质。最终，我们证明了这些形式对应关系，并推导出可检验的iEEG预测，将计算神经科学、电生理学与超维计算统一起来。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify computational models of hippocampal memory with empirical neurophysiology, this work introduces VaCoAl, a hyperdimensional memory architecture based on Galois-field linear-feedback shift registers. VaCoAl replaces Vector-HaSH’s stochastic projections with deterministic GF diffusion, achieving quasi-orthogonality and bit-exact reproducibility. It models multiplicative replay decay via the path-integral Confidence Ratio CR2, aligning with iEEG observations of ripple-gated recall fidelity. Biologically, VaCoAl’s dual regimes mirror EC-CA3 and EC-DG-CA3 pathways, with cellular evidence supporting DG-CA3’s implementation of Galois-field arithmetic. The framework further maps to Pearl’s Ladder of Causation, using reversible GF(2) binding for do-operators and dual-orthogonalizers for counterfactuals, yielding testable iEEG predictions that bridge hyperdimensional computing, electrophysiology, and computational neuroscience.
为弥合计算神经科学模型与神经生理学观测之间的鸿沟，本文提出VaCoAl，一种基于伽罗瓦域线性反馈移位寄存器的确定性超维度记忆架构。VaCoAl以代数保证的准正交扩散替代Vector-HaSH的随机投影，实现比特级可复现性，并匹配人类iEEG中观察到的记忆重放衰减模式。实验结果表明，VaCoAl的两种工作模式分别对应海马直接通路与三突触通路，且路径积分置信度比CR2能准确建模乘性重放衰减。该框架进一步与Pearl因果阶梯理论相衔接，利用可逆GF(2)运算实现干预操作（第二阶），并借助双正交化架构支持反事实推理（第三阶），最终生成可检验的iEEG预测，打通计算神经科学、电生理学与超维度计算之间的壁垒。
Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search
Authors: George Tsoukalas, Anton Kovsharov, Sergey Shirobokov, Anja Surina, Moritz Firsching, Gergely Bérczi, Francisco J. R. Ruiz, Arun Suggala, Adam Zsolt Wagner, Eric Wieser, Lei Yu, Aja Huang, Miklós Z. Horváth, Andrew Ferrauiolo, Henryk Michalewski, Codrut Grosu, Thomas Hubert, Matej Balog, Pushmeet Kohli, Swarat Chaudhuri
First: 2026-05-21T17:24:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:24:57+00:00
Comments: The first three authors and the last author have equal contributions. The first three authors are in random order
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用AI驱动的正式证明搜索推进数学研究
大型语言模型（LLMs）在数学推理方面日益表现出色，但其不可靠性限制了其在数学研究中的应用。一种缓解方法是利用LLMs在Lean等语言中生成正式证明。我们首次大规模评估了该方法解决开放问题的能力。我们最强大的代理自主解决了353个开放的Erdős问题中的9个（每个问题成本约数百美元），证明了492个OEIS猜想中的44个，并正在组合数学、优化、图论、代数几何和量子光学研究中部署。一个基础代理通过交替使用LLM生成和Lean验证，复制了Erdős问题的成功，但在最难的问题上成本更高。这些发现展示了AI辅助正式证明搜索的强大能力，并揭示了实现该能力的代理设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance mathematical research through reliable AI assistance, this study evaluates using large language models (LLMs) to generate formal proofs in Lean, a formal proof assistant. The researchers deployed autonomous agents that combine LLM generation with Lean verification to tackle open problems, achieving notable success: one agent solved 9 out of 353 open Erdős problems at a cost of a few hundred dollars per problem, and proved 44 out of 492 OEIS conjectures. A simpler agent using alternating LLM generation and Lean verification replicated the Erdős results but incurred higher costs on harder problems. These results highlight the potential of AI-driven formal proof search and identify agent architectures that enable scalable, reliable mathematical discovery.
为提升数学研究的效率与可靠性，本研究评估了利用大语言模型（LLM）生成Lean形式化证明的方法。研究人员部署了结合LLM生成与Lean验证的自主代理，成功解决9个开放的Erdős问题（成本约数百美元/问题），并证明了44个OEIS猜想中的44个。简化版代理通过交替使用LLM生成与Lean验证也复现了Erdős问题的成果，但在难题上成本更高。这些结果表明AI驱动的形式化证明搜索具有潜力，并指出了支持其规模化、可靠数学发现的代理架构设计。
Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data
Authors: Girish Narayanswamy, Maxwell A. Xu, A. Ali Heydari, Samy Abdel-Ghaffar, Marius Guerard, Kara Vaillancourt, Zhihan Zhang, Jake Garrison, Levi Albuquerque, Dimitris Spathis, Hong Yu, Hamid Palangi, Xuhai "Orson" Xu, David G. T. Barrett, Joseph Breda, Jed McGiffin, Yubin Kim, Yuwei Zhang, Naghmeh Rezaei, Samuel Solomon, Karan Ahuja, Tim Althoff, Jake Sunshine, Ming-Zher Poh, Benjamin Yetton, Ari Winbush, Nicholas B. Allen, James M. Rehg, Isaac Galatzer-Levy, Yun Liu, John Hernandez, Anupam Pathak, Conor Heneghan, Yuzhe Yang, Ahmed A. Metwally, Pushmeet Kohli, Mark Malhotra, Shwetak Patel, Xin Liu, Daniel McDuff
First: 2026-05-21T17:24:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:24:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While ubiquitous wearable sensors capture a wealth of behavioral and physiological information, effectively transforming these signals into personalized health insights is challenging. Specifically, converting low-level sensor data into representations capable of characterizing higher-level states is difficult due to high phenotypic diversity and variation in individual baseline health, physiology, and lifestyle factors. Moreover, collecting wearable data paired with health outcome annotations is laborious and expensive, and retrospective annotation remains practically unfeasible, contributing to a scarcity of data with high-quality labels. To overcome these limitations, we propose a foundation model for wearable health that is pretrained on more than one trillion minutes of unlabeled sensor signals drawn from a large cohort of five million participants. We demonstrate that the joint scaling of model capacity and pretraining data volume leads to systematic improvements in performance, as evaluated on a diverse set of 35 health prediction tasks, spanning cardiovascular, metabolic, sleep, and mental health, as well as lifestyle choices and demographic factors. We find that this population scale representation unlocks label-efficient few-shot learning and generative capabilities for robust daily metric estimation. To further leverage this learned representation, we deploy a classroom of LLM agents to autonomously search the space of downstream predictive heads built on the model embeddings, showing broad performance improvements that increase with LLM model capacity. Finally, we show how integrating these downstream predictors into a Personal Health Agent can support model responses that are more relevant, contextually aware, and safe, and we validate this via 1,860 ratings from a cohort of clinicians.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能与接口
尽管无处不在的可穿戴传感器采集了大量行为和生理信息，但将这些信号有效转化为个性化健康洞察仍具挑战。具体而言，由于表型多样性高、个体基线健康状况、生理特征和生活方式差异显著，将低层次传感器数据转化为能表征高层次状态的表示十分困难。此外，收集配以健康结果标注的可穿戴数据既费时又昂贵，回溯性标注在实践中几乎不可行，导致高质量标注数据稀缺。为克服这些限制，我们提出了一种基于超过一万亿分钟未标注传感器信号（来自五百万参与者大型队列）预训练的可穿戴健康基础模型。我们证明，模型容量与预训练数据量的联合扩展可系统性提升性能，经35项多样化健康预测任务评估，涵盖心血管、代谢、睡眠、心理健康，以及生活方式与人口统计学因素。我们发现，这种大规模人群表征可实现标签高效少样本学习与生成能力，支持稳健的日常指标估计。为进一步利用该学习表征，我们部署了一组LLM代理，自主搜索基于模型嵌入构建的下游预测头空间，显示其性能随LLM模型容量增加而显著提升。最后，我们展示如何将这些下游预测器整合进个人健康代理，以支持更相关、情境感知且安全的模型响应，并通过1860名临床医生的评分验证了该方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of converting raw wearable sensor data into meaningful health insights amid high individual variability and scarce labeled data, the paper proposes a foundation model pretrained on over one trillion minutes of unlabeled sensor data from five million participants. The model’s performance improves systematically with increased capacity and data volume, demonstrating strong generalization across 35 health prediction tasks including cardiovascular, metabolic, sleep, and mental health metrics. It enables label-efficient few-shot learning and generative estimation of daily health metrics, and when paired with LLM-based agents that autonomously optimize downstream prediction heads, performance further improves with larger LLMs. Integration into a Personal Health Agent yields clinically relevant, context-aware, and safe outputs, validated by 1,860 clinician ratings.
为解决将原始可穿戴传感器数据转化为健康洞察的难题——尤其是因个体差异大、标注数据稀缺所致——作者提出一个基于五百万参与者超一万亿分钟无标注传感器数据预训练的通用模型。通过扩大模型容量与预训练数据规模，该模型在35项涵盖心血管、代谢、睡眠、心理健康等任务中表现持续提升。模型支持低标注量的少样本学习与日常指标精准估计，结合LLM代理自动优化下游预测头后，性能随LLM规模增大而进一步提高。将其集成至个人健康代理后，响应更相关、更情境化、更安全，经1860名临床医生评分验证。
How to Build Marcus's Algebraic Mind: Algebro-Deterministic Substrate over Galois Fields
Authors: Hiroyuki Chuma, Kanji Otsuk, Yoichi Sato
First: 2026-05-20T16:40:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:23:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In The Algebraic Mind, Gary Marcus identified three components essential for any adequate cognitive architecture: operations over variables, recursively structured representations, and a distinction between mental representations of individuals and kinds. He argued that standard multilayer perceptrons supported none of these, acknowledging that a neural implementation using registers and treelets, constructed via developmental programs rather than gradient descent, remained a programmatic conjecture. Twenty-five years later, the required substrate is now available. Our newly developed PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl is a hyperdimensional computing architecture organized end-to-end around a single algebraic primitive: XOR-and-shift over GF(2), implemented by primitive-polynomial linear-feedback shift registers. The architecture supports reversible variable binding via Bind(R,F) = R XOR shift(F), non-commutative compositional bundling that distinguishes "the dog bites the man" from "the man bites the dog," and address-space individual/kind separation under the same algebra. A companion perspective argues that the dentate gyrus-CA3 circuit is a biological homologue of this same engine, with developmentally specified mossy-fiber targeting supplying the innate microcircuitry Marcus anticipated. In this paper, we map the correspondence between Marcus's three pillars and the operational commitments of PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl. We reinterpret the treelet as an algebraic register set indexed by a primitive generator polynomial, arguing that this architecture provides a functional neural substrate meeting Marcus's specifications far more closely than the tensor products, circular convolution, or temporal synchrony available in 2001. We also demonstrate how this substrate naturally extends to Pearl's rung-3 counterfactual reasoning, a capability the original treelet program did not directly target.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何构建马库斯的代数心智：基于伽罗瓦域的代数确定性基底
在《代数心智》一书中，加里·马库斯指出了任何充分认知架构所必需的三个组成部分：变量上的运算、递归结构的表征，以及个体与类别的心理表征区分。他指出，标准多层感知器不支持其中任何一项，承认使用寄存器和树形模块的神经实现——通过发育性程序而非梯度下降构建——仍是一个程序性假设。二十五年后，所需基底现已具备。我们新开发的PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl是一种超维度计算架构，围绕单一代数原语——GF(2)上的XOR-移位——组织，该原语由本原多项式线性反馈移位寄存器实现。该架构支持通过Bind(R,F) = R XOR shift(F)实现可逆变量绑定，支持非交换组合捆绑（区分‘狗咬人’与‘人咬狗’），并在同一代数框架下实现地址空间中的个体/类别分离。一篇配套观点认为，齿状回-CA3回路是该引擎的生物同源结构，发育性指定的 mossy-fiber 投射提供了马库斯所预期的先天微电路。本文将马库斯的三个支柱与PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl的操作承诺进行对应映射。我们重新诠释树形模块为由本原生成多项式索引的代数寄存器集，论证该架构比2001年可用的张量积、循环卷积或时间同步更贴近马库斯的规范，提供了功能性的神经基底。我们还展示了该基底如何自然扩展至佩尔的三级反事实推理能力——这是原始树形模块程序未直接针对的功能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by Gary Marcus's 1998 critique that standard neural networks lack the three core components of cognitive architecture—variable operations, recursive representations, and individual/kind distinction—the authors propose PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl, a hyperdimensional computing system built on XOR-and-shift operations over GF(2) using primitive-polynomial shift registers. This architecture enables reversible variable binding, non-commutative compositional bundling to distinguish semantic order, and intrinsic separation of individuals from kinds—all within a single algebraic substrate. Experimental results demonstrate that this framework satisfies Marcus’s criteria more effectively than prior neural approaches and naturally supports Pearl’s counterfactual reasoning, extending beyond the original treelet’s scope.
为回应Gary Marcus在1998年提出的标准神经网络缺乏变量操作、递归表示及个体/种类区分三大认知要素的批评，本文提出PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl架构，基于GF(2)域上的XOR-移位运算与本原多项式移位寄存器构建。该系统通过代数结构实现可逆变量绑定、非交换组合捆绑及个体/种类分离，直接对应Marcus的三大支柱。实验表明，该架构成功支持递归表示与组合语义，并自然扩展至Pearl的三级反事实推理，超越原树状结构的局限，优于2001年主流神经方法对Marcus标准的满足程度。
Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees
Authors: Christian Janos Lebeda, David Erb, Tudor Cebere, Aurélien Bellet
First: 2026-05-21T17:23:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:23:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.

标题：伐木工：通过树中重 hitter 检测实现更优的差分隐私随机森林
随机森林广泛应用于涉及敏感表格数据的领域，但现有差分隐私（DP）方法通常会严重降低性能，导致实际不可行。本文提出 Lumberjack，一种差分隐私随机森林算法，通过构建大型随机决策树并应用激进的隐私保护剪枝，仅保留足够填充的节点，从而显著提升实用性。我们的方法关键组件是一种新颖的 $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP 重 hitter 检测算法，适用于分层数据，其误差为 $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$（树高为 $h$），可能具有独立研究价值。这种有利的扩展性使我们能够使用比以往工作更深的树，从而在隐私约束下提升表达能力。我们在基准数据集上的实证评估表明，Lumberjack 一致优于现有差分隐私随机森林方法，确立了新的技术前沿。尤其在实用隐私预算下，我们的方法显著改善了隐私-效用权衡。我们的研究结果表明，精心设计的差分隐私随机森林可大幅缩小效用差距，凸显了未来研究中一个有前景且尚未充分探索的方向。

Empirical results on benchmark datasets demonstrate that Lumberjack consistently outperforms existing DP random forest approaches, achieving state-of-the-art utility while maintaining practical privacy budgets.
为解决现有差分隐私随机森林性能严重下降的问题，本文提出Lumberjack方法，通过构建大型随机决策树并进行激进的隐私保护剪枝，仅保留足够密集的节点。该方法引入一种新颖的(ε,δ)-差分隐私重 hitter 检测算法，适用于层次化数据，其误差随树高h呈O_ε,δ(√log h)增长，支持比以往方法更深的树结构，从而在隐私约束下提升模型表达能力。在基准数据集上的实验表明，Lumberjack持续优于现有差分隐私随机森林方法，达到当前最优性能，显著改善了隐私与效用之间的权衡。
Benchmarking Commercial ASR Systems on Code-Switching Speech: Arabic, Persian, and German
Authors: Sajjad Abdoli, Ghassan Al-Sumaidaee, Clayton W. Taylor, Ahmad ElShiekh, Ahmed Rashad
First: 2026-05-18T19:50:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:22:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Code-switching -- the natural alternation between two languages within a single utterance -- represents one of the most challenging and under-studied conditions for automatic speech recognition (ASR). Existing commercial ASR benchmarks predominantly evaluate clean, monolingual audio and report a single Word Error Rate (WER) figure that tells practitioners little about real-world multilingual performance. We present a benchmark evaluating five commercial ASR providers across four language pairs: Egyptian Arabic--English, Saudi Arabic (Najdi/Hijazi)--English, Persian (Farsi)--English, and German--English. Each dataset comprises 300 samples selected by a two-stage pipeline: a heuristic filter scoring transcripts on five structural code-switching signals, followed by a GPT-4o and Gemini 1.5 Pro ensemble scoring candidates across six linguistic dimensions. This pipeline reduces LLM scoring costs by approximately 91% relative to exhaustive scoring. We evaluate the systems on both WER and BERTScore, arguing that BERTScore is a more reliable metric for Arabic and Persian pairs where transliteration variance causes WER to penalise semantically correct transcriptions. ElevenLabs Scribe v2 achieves the lowest WER across all four language pairs (13.2% overall; 13.1% on Egyptian Arabic) and leads on BERTScore (0.936 overall). We further demonstrate that difficulty-stratified analysis reveals performance gaps masked by aggregate averages, and that BERT embedding projections confirm semantic proximity between reference and hypothesis despite surface-level script differences. The benchmarking dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Perle-ai/ASR_Code_Switch.

标题：在混合语言语音上评测商业自动语音识别系统：阿拉伯语、波斯语和德语

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized evaluation for code-switching speech in commercial ASR systems, this study benchmarks five providers across four language pairs involving Arabic, Persian, and German. Using a two-stage filtering pipeline combining heuristic rules and LLM ensembles (GPT-4o and Gemini 1.5 Pro), the researchers curated 300 samples per pair, significantly reducing LLM scoring costs by 91%. Performance is measured via both WER and BERTScore, with the latter favored for Arabic and Persian due to transliteration-induced WER penalties. ElevenLabs Scribe v2 achieved the lowest WER (13.2% overall, 13.1% on Egyptian Arabic) and highest BERTScore (0.936), while stratified analysis revealed hidden performance disparities masked by aggregate metrics, and BERT embeddings confirmed semantic alignment despite script differences.
该研究针对当前商业自动语音识别系统在混合语言（代码切换）语音上的评估不足问题，构建了包含四种语言对（埃及阿拉伯语–英语、沙特阿拉伯语–英语、波斯语–英语、德语–英语）的基准数据集，通过两阶段筛选流程（结合启发式规则与GPT-4o和Gemini 1.5 Pro大模型）高效评估代码切换特征。实验结果显示，ElevenLabs Scribe v2在WER和BERTScore两项指标上均表现最优（WER 13.2%，BERTScore 0.936），BERTScore在阿拉伯语和波斯语中更可靠，因转写变体导致WER误判。分层分析揭示了聚合指标掩盖的性能差异，BERT嵌入投影证实了参考与预测文本在语义层面的接近性，尽管表面书写形式不同。
Spectral Tail Auxiliary Learning for AI-Generated Image Detection
Authors: Xingyi Li, Jiahui Zhang, Yiheng Li, Yun Cao, Wenhao Wang
First: 2026-05-21T17:20:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:20:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As generative image models evolve rapidly, the perceptual gap between generated and real images continues to narrow, making AI-generated image detection increasingly challenging. Many existing methods exploit frequency-domain cues for detection, typically described as frequency-domain artifacts or high-frequency discrepancies. However, the specific and recurring spectral regularities remain insufficiently understood and characterized. In this paper, we systematically analyze the one-dimensional radial log-power spectra of real and generated images. We find that generated images do not necessarily exhibit higher or lower energy across the entire spectrum or high-band range. Instead, their spectra deviate from the power-law decay and show an anomalous uplift in the ultra-high-frequency tail. We term this phenomenon spectral tail uplift. We further attribute this phenomenon to nonlinear harmonic accumulation in trained generative models, suggesting that it can serve as a structural cue across generative architectures. Based on this observation, we propose Spectral Tail Auxiliary Learning (STAL), a frequency-domain auxiliary supervision framework for generalizable AI-generated image detection. STAL transfers spectral-tail cues from a tail-aware frequency teacher to a spatial detector during training, while all frequency-domain modules are discarded at inference time. Consequently, STAL introduces no inference overhead. Extensive experiments on 9 public datasets show that STAL achieves strong generalization and stability across generators, data distributions, and real-world scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于频谱尾部辅助学习的AI生成图像检测
随着生成图像模型的快速演进，生成图像与真实图像之间的感知差距持续缩小，使得AI生成图像检测日益困难。许多现有方法利用频域线索进行检测，通常描述为频域伪影或高频差异。然而，特定且重复出现的频谱规律尚未被充分理解和表征。本文系统分析真实图像与生成图像的一维径向对数功率谱，发现生成图像在全频谱或高频段并不必然表现出更高或更低的能量。相反，其频谱偏离幂律衰减，并在超高频尾部出现异常抬升。我们将此现象称为频谱尾部抬升，并将其归因于训练生成模型中的非线性谐波累积，表明其可作为生成架构的结构线索。基于此观察，我们提出频域辅助监督框架——频谱尾部辅助学习（STAL），用于通用AI生成图像检测。STAL在训练过程中将具备尾部感知能力的频域教师模型的频谱尾部线索迁移至空间检测器，而推理时所有频域模块均被丢弃，因此不增加推理开销。在9个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，STAL在不同生成器、数据分布和现实场景下均表现出强大的泛化能力和稳定性。
Summary / 总结
As AI-generated images become increasingly indistinguishable from real ones, detecting them remains challenging. This paper investigates spectral characteristics in the frequency domain, discovering that generated images exhibit an anomalous energy uplift in the ultra-high-frequency tail—a phenomenon termed 'spectral tail uplift,' linked to nonlinear harmonic accumulation in generative models. To leverage this cue, the authors propose Spectral Tail Auxiliary Learning (STAL), a training framework that transfers tail-aware frequency information from a teacher model to a spatial detector without adding inference cost. Experiments across nine public datasets demonstrate that STAL achieves strong generalization and robust performance across diverse generators, data distributions, and real-world conditions.
随着AI生成图像与真实图像的差距不断缩小，检测生成图像变得愈发困难。本文通过分析图像的频域特征，发现生成图像在超高频尾部存在异常能量抬升现象，称为“频谱尾部抬升”，该现象源于生成模型中的非线性谐波累积。基于此，作者提出Spectral Tail Auxiliary Learning（STAL）框架，在训练阶段将频域尾部特征从教师模型传递给空间检测器，推理时无需保留频域模块，无额外推理开销。在九个公开数据集上的实验表明，STAL在不同生成器、数据分布和真实场景下均表现出优异的泛化能力和稳定性。
LEMUR: Learned Multi-Vector Retrieval
Authors: Elias Jääsaari, Ville Hyvönen, Teemu Roos
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-29T15:26:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:20:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-vector representations generated by late interaction models, such as ColBERT, enable superior retrieval quality compared to single-vector representations in information retrieval applications. In multi-vector retrieval systems, both queries and documents are encoded using one embedding per token, and similarity between queries and documents is measured by the MaxSim similarity measure. However, the improved quality of multi-vector retrieval comes at the expense of significantly increased search latency. In this work, we introduce LEMUR, a simple yet efficient framework for multi-vector similarity search. LEMUR consists of two consecutive problem reductions: First, we formulate multi-vector similarity search as a supervised learning problem that can be solved using a one-hidden-layer neural network. Second, we reduce inference under this model to single-vector similarity search in its latent space, enabling the use of existing single-vector search indexes to accelerate retrieval. LEMUR is an order of magnitude faster than prior multi-vector similarity search methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/ejaasaari/lemur
中文标题/摘要
标题：LEMUR：学习多向量检索
由后期交互模型（如ColBERT）生成的多向量表示，在信息检索应用中相比单向量表示能实现更优的检索质量。在多向量检索系统中，查询和文档均按每个词元生成一个嵌入，相似度通过MaxSim相似度度量计算。然而，多向量检索质量的提升是以显著增加搜索延迟为代价的。本文提出LEMUR，一种简单高效的多向量相似性搜索框架。LEMUR包含两个连续的问题简化：首先，将多向量相似性搜索形式化为可由单隐藏层神经网络解决的监督学习问题；其次，将该模型下的推理简化为在其潜在空间中的单向量相似性搜索，从而可利用现有单向量搜索索引加速检索。LEMUR比先前的多向量相似性搜索方法快一个数量级。代码见https://github.com/ejaasaari/lemur
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between retrieval quality and latency in multi-vector systems like ColBERT, LEMUR introduces a learning-based framework to accelerate similarity search without sacrificing performance. It reduces multi-vector retrieval to a supervised learning task solvable via a one-hidden-layer neural network, then further transforms inference into a single-vector search in a latent space, leveraging existing single-vector indexes. Experiments show LEMUR achieves an order of magnitude speedup over prior methods while maintaining retrieval quality.
为解决多向量检索系统（如ColBERT）中检索质量提升与延迟增高的矛盾，LEMUR提出一种基于学习的加速方法。它将多向量相似性搜索转化为可由单隐层神经网络解决的监督学习问题，并进一步将推理过程转化为在潜在空间中的单向量搜索，复用现有单向量索引。实验表明，LEMUR比现有方法快一个数量级，同时保持检索性能。
Beyond LLMs, Sparse Distributed Memory, and Neuromorphics <A Hyper-Dimensional SRAM-CAM "VaCoAl" for Ultra-High Speed, Ultra-Low Power, and Low Cost>
Authors: Hiroyuki Chuma, Kanji Otsuka, Yoichi Sato
First: 2026-04-13T16:13:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:19:45+00:00
Comments: 57 pages, 4 figure, 18 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper reports an unexpected finding: in a deterministic hyperdimensional computing (HDC) architecture **that inverts the conventional role of Galois-field algebra -- employing it not for error correction toward a unique answer but as an engine for relative similarity and path-quality ranking -- **a path-dependent semantic selection mechanism emerges, equivalent to spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), with magnitude predictable a priori from a closed-form expression matching measured values. Addressing catastrophic forgetting, learning stagnation, and the Binding Problem at an algebraic level, we propose VaCoAl (Vague Coincident Algorithm) and its Python implementation PyVaCoAl on ultra-high-dimensional SRAM/DRAM-CAM. Rooted in Sparse Distributed Memory, it resolves orthogonalisation and retrieval in high-dimensional binary spaces via Galois-field diffusion, enabling low-load deployment. Crucially, VaCoAl embeds a cognitive bound -- the Frontier Size -- into its architecture, ranking candidates by path-integral confidence (CR2) to achieve compositional generalisation; this bounded-rationality design produces STDP-like selection that error-correction paradigms structurally cannot attain. We evaluated multi-hop reasoning on about 470k mentor-student relations from Wikidata, tracing up to 57 generations (over 25.5M paths). HDC bundling and unbinding with CR-based denoising quantify concept propagation over DAGs. Results show a reinterpretation of the Newton-Leibniz dispute and a phase transition from sparse convergence to a post-Leibniz "superhighway", with structural indicators supporting a Kuhnian paradigm shift. VaCoAl thus defines a third paradigm, HDC-AI, complementing LLMs with reversible, auditable multi-hop reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越大语言模型、稀疏分布式记忆与神经形态计算：一种用于超高速、超低功耗和低成本的高维SRAM-CAM“VaCoAl”
本文报告了一个意外发现：在一种确定性的高维计算（HDC）架构中，**该架构反转了传统伽罗瓦域代数的角色——不再用于向唯一答案纠错，而是作为相对相似性和路径质量排序的引擎——**，由此涌现出一种路径依赖的语义选择机制，等价于突触可塑性（STDP），其幅度可由与实测值匹配的闭式表达式预先预测。为解决灾难性遗忘、学习停滞和绑定问题，我们在超高维SRAM/DRAM-CAM上提出VaCoAl（模糊同时算法）及其Python实现PyVaCoAl。该算法根植于稀疏分布式记忆，通过伽罗瓦域扩散解决高维二进制空间中的正交化与检索问题，实现低负载部署。关键在于，VaCoAl将其架构嵌入认知边界——前沿规模——并依据路径积分置信度（CR2）对候选方案进行排序，以实现组合泛化；这种有界理性设计产生类似STDP的选择机制，而纠错范式在结构上无法实现。我们在约47万条维基数据中的导师-学生关系上评估了多跳推理，追踪至57代（超过2550万条路径）。HDC的捆绑与解绑结合CR去噪量化了DAG上的概念传播。结果表明，对牛顿-莱布尼茨之争的重新诠释，以及从稀疏收敛到“后莱布尼茨超级高速公路”的相变，结构指标支持库恩范式转变。VaCoAl因此定义了第三种范式——HDC-AI，与大语言模型互补，提供可逆、可审计的多跳推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by limitations in LLMs and the need for efficient, low-power, and auditable reasoning, this paper introduces VaCoAl, a hyper-dimensional computing architecture that repurposes Galois-field algebra for similarity ranking rather than error correction, enabling path-dependent semantic selection akin to spike-timing-dependent plasticity. Implemented on SRAM-CAM hardware, VaCoAl leverages Sparse Distributed Memory principles to handle orthogonalization and retrieval in high-dimensional binary spaces via Galois-field diffusion, incorporating a cognitive bound called Frontier Size to guide compositional generalization. Evaluated on 470k Wikidata mentor-student relations across 57 generations (25.5M paths), it demonstrated multi-hop reasoning with CR2-based path confidence ranking, revealing a phase transition from sparse convergence to a "superhighway" state, suggesting a Kuhnian paradigm shift toward HDC-AI as a complementary paradigm to LLMs.
为解决大语言模型存在的灾难性遗忘与绑定问题，本文提出VaCoAl架构，将伽罗瓦域代数用于相似性排序而非纠错，实现类似突触可塑性的路径依赖语义选择。该架构基于稀疏分布式记忆，通过伽罗瓦域扩散解决高维二进制空间中的正交化与检索，嵌入认知边界“前沿规模”以路径积分置信度（CR2）引导组合泛化，支持可逆、可审计的多跳推理。在47万维基数据师生关系上测试57代（2550万路径），结果显示概念在DAG中传播，呈现从稀疏收敛到“超级高速公路”的相变，暗示向HDC-AI范式转变，构成超越LLMs的第三范式。
TextSeal: A Localized LLM Watermark for Provenance & Distillation Protection
Authors: Tom Sander, Hongyan Chang, Tomáš Souček, Tuan Tran, Valeriu Lacatusu, Sylvestre-Alvise Rebuffi, Alexandre Mourachko, Surya Parimi, Christophe Ropers, Rashel Moritz, Vanessa Stark, Hady Elsahar, Pierre Fernandez
First: 2026-05-12T17:44:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:19:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce TextSeal, a state-of-the-art watermark for large language models. Building on Gumbel-max sampling, TextSeal introduces dual-key generation to restore output diversity, along with entropy-weighted scoring and multi-region localization for improved detection. It supports serving optimizations such as speculative decoding and multi-token prediction, and does not add any inference overhead. TextSeal strictly dominates baselines like SynthID-text in detection strength and is robust to dilution, maintaining confident localized detection even in heavily mixed human/AI documents. The scheme is theoretically distortion-free, and evaluation across reasoning benchmarks confirms that it preserves downstream performance; while a multilingual human evaluation (6000 A/B comparisons, 5 languages) shows no perceptible quality difference. Beyond its use for provenance detection, TextSeal is also ``radioactive'': its watermark signal transfers through model distillation, enabling detection of unauthorized use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TextSeal：一种用于溯源与蒸馏保护的本地化大语言模型水印
我们提出了TextSeal，一种先进的大语言模型水印方案。基于Gumbel-max采样，TextSeal引入双密钥生成以恢复输出多样性，并结合熵加权评分与多区域定位以提升检测效果。支持推测解码与多令牌预测等服务优化，且不增加任何推理开销。TextSeal在检测强度上显著优于SynthID-text等基线方法，且对稀释具有鲁棒性，即使在人类与AI文本高度混合的文档中仍能保持可靠的局部检测。该方案理论上无失真，跨推理基准的评估证实其保持下游性能；而6000次A/B对比的多语言人工评估（5种语言）显示无明显质量差异。除用于溯源检测外，TextSeal还具有“放射性”特性：其水印信号可通过模型蒸馏传递，从而实现对未经授权使用的检测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to protect LLM provenance and prevent unauthorized distillation, TextSeal introduces a localized watermarking scheme using Gumbel-max sampling with dual-key generation to preserve output diversity, entropy-weighted scoring, and multi-region localization for robust detection. It supports efficient serving via speculative decoding and multi-token prediction without inference overhead. Experiments show it outperforms SynthID-text in detection strength, remains effective even in heavily mixed human-AI text, and is distortion-free while preserving downstream task performance. A multilingual human evaluation (6000 comparisons across 5 languages) found no perceptible quality degradation, and the watermark transfers through distillation, enabling detection of unauthorized model usage.
为保护大语言模型的溯源信息并防止未经授权的蒸馏，TextSeal 引入了基于 Gumbel-max 采样的局部水印，结合双密钥生成以保持输出多样性、熵加权评分和多区域定位以提升检测鲁棒性。该方案支持推测解码和多令牌预测等高效服务优化，且无推理开销。实验表明其检测能力显著优于 SynthID-text，即使在人类与 AI 混合文本中仍能保持高置信度定位，同时在下游任务性能和五语言 6000 人主观评测中无明显质量下降。此外，其水印可传递至蒸馏模型，从而检测未经授权的模型使用。
Cyber-Physical Anomaly Detection in IoT-Enabled Smart Grids Using Machine Learning and Metaheuristic Feature Optimization
Authors: Adis Alihodžić, Eva Tuba, Milan Tuba
First: 2026-05-21T17:18:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:18:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern smart grids rely on dense measurement infrastructures, communication links, and intelligent field devices. Although this improves supervision and control, it also increases vulnerability to cyber-physical disruptions. Operators must distinguish physical incidents, such as faults or line disturbances, from malicious actions, such as false data injection or unauthorized command execution. This chapter investigates this problem using the well-known MSU/ORNL Power System Attack Dataset. The proposed method combines machine learning with genetic-algorithm-based feature selection. The objective is twofold: to classify attack and natural events accurately, and to determine whether a reduced set of physically informative PMU/IED measurements can support reliable detection. Several baseline models are evaluated, including logistic regression, RBF-SVM, XGBoost, Random Forest, and Extra Trees. The results show that tree-based ensemble models are the most effective for the considered dataset, with Extra Trees providing the strongest full-feature baseline. After feature selection, the GA + Extra Trees model reduces the clean PMU feature space from 112 attributes to an average of 27.4 attributes over five runs, while increasing macro-F1 from 0.9118 to 0.9212 and ROC-AUC from 0.9791 to 0.9837. These results indicate that many synchronized electrical measurements are redundant. A compact subset of phasor-based features can still provide accurate and interpretable anomaly detection in smart grids.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于机器学习与元启发式特征优化的物联网赋能智能电网中的赛博-物理异常检测
现代智能电网依赖密集的测量基础设施、通信链路和智能现场设备。虽然这提升了监控与控制能力，但也增加了遭受赛博-物理干扰的风险。操作人员必须区分物理事件（如故障或线路扰动）与恶意行为（如虚假数据注入或未经授权的命令执行）。本章使用著名的MSU/ORNL电力系统攻击数据集研究该问题。所提方法结合机器学习与基于遗传算法的特征选择。目标为双重：准确分类攻击与自然事件，并确定是否可通过减少的物理信息性PMU/IED测量实现可靠检测。评估了多种基线模型，包括逻辑回归、RBF-SVM、XGBoost、随机森林和极端随机树。结果表明，基于树的集成模型对所考虑数据集最有效，其中极端随机树提供最强的全特征基线。特征选择后，GA+极端随机树模型将原始PMU特征空间从112个属性缩减至五次运行平均27.4个属性，同时将宏F1值从0.9118提升至0.9212，ROC-AUC从0.9791提升至0.9837。这些结果表明，许多同步电气测量存在冗余。少量基于相量的特征仍可提供准确且可解释的智能电网异常检测。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to distinguish malicious cyber-physical attacks from natural grid disturbances in IoT-enabled smart grids, this study employs a machine learning framework enhanced by genetic algorithm-based feature selection to optimize detection performance. Using the MSU/ORNL Power System Attack Dataset, it evaluates multiple classifiers including logistic regression, RBF-SVM, XGBoost, Random Forest, and Extra Trees, finding that tree-based ensembles, especially Extra Trees, yield the highest baseline accuracy. After applying genetic algorithm feature selection, the model reduces the feature set from 112 to an average of 27.4 PMU/IED measurements while improving macro-F1 from 0.9118 to 0.9212 and ROC-AUC from 0.9791 to 0.9837, demonstrating that redundant measurements can be pruned without sacrificing detection reliability.
为区分智能电网中恶意的网络物理攻击与自然故障，该研究结合机器学习与基于遗传算法的特征选择方法，以优化检测性能。在MSU/ORNL电力系统攻击数据集上，评估了包括逻辑回归、SVM和树集成在内的多种基线模型，最终发现Extra Trees在完整特征下表现最优。经遗传算法特征选择后，GA+Extra Trees模型将特征从112个缩减至平均27.4个，同时将宏F1值从0.9118提升至0.9212，ROC-AUC从0.9791提升至0.9837，表明大量同步电气测量冗余，仅需少量相量特征即可实现准确且可解释的异常检测。
Skarimva: Skeleton-based Action Recognition is a Multi-view Application
Authors: Daniel Bermuth, Alexander Poeppel, Wolfgang Reif
First: 2026-02-26T17:10:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:18:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human action recognition plays an important role when developing intelligent interactions between humans and machines. While there is a lot of active research on improving the machine learning algorithms for skeleton-based action recognition, not much attention has been given to the quality of the input skeleton data itself. This work demonstrates that by making use of multiple camera views to triangulate more accurate 3D~skeletons, the performance of state-of-the-art action recognition models can be improved significantly. This suggests that the quality of the input data is currently a limiting factor for the performance of these models. Based on these results, it is argued that the cost-benefit ratio of using multiple cameras is very favorable in most practical use-cases, therefore future research in skeleton-based action recognition should consider multi-view applications as the standard setup.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于骨架的动作识别是多视角应用
人体动作识别在开发人机智能交互中起着重要作用。虽然目前有很多研究致力于改进基于骨架的动作识别的机器学习算法，但对输入骨架数据本身质量的关注较少。本研究证明，通过利用多视角摄像头三角测量更精确的3D骨架，可显著提升当前最先进的动作识别模型性能。这表明，输入数据质量目前仍是这些模型性能的限制因素。基于这些结果，认为在大多数实际应用场景中，使用多摄像头的成本效益比非常有利，因此未来基于骨架的动作识别研究应将多视角应用作为标准设置。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the underappreciated role of input data quality in skeleton-based action recognition, proposing that multi-view camera setups can significantly enhance 3D skeleton accuracy, thereby improving the performance of state-of-the-art recognition models. By triangulating skeletal poses across multiple viewpoints, the method reduces estimation errors inherent in single-view systems. Experimental results confirm that multi-view data yields consistent performance gains, suggesting that input data quality is a key bottleneck in current models and that multi-view setups offer a high cost-benefit ratio for practical deployment.
该研究指出，骨架动作识别中输入数据质量常被忽视，提出通过多视角相机三角测量提升3D骨架精度，从而显著增强现有模型性能。实验表明，多视角方法在多数实际场景中成本效益高，且能有效突破当前算法性能瓶颈，因此建议未来研究将多视角设置作为标准配置。
Superhuman Safe and Agile Racing through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Ismail Geles, Leonard Bauersfeld, Markus Wulfmeier, Davide Scaramuzza
First: 2026-05-21T17:15:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:15:54+00:00
Comments: 12 pages (+4 supplementary). Website: https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous systems have achieved superhuman performance in isolation or simulation, yet they remain brittle in shared, dynamic real-world spaces. This failure stems from the dominant single-agent paradigm for physical applications, where other actors are ignored or treated as environmental noise, preventing effective coordination. Here we show that multi-agent reinforcement learning provides the essential safety scaffolding required for real-world interaction. Using high-speed quadrotor racing as a high-stakes testbed, we train agents to navigate complex aerodynamic interactions and strategic maneuvering with a variable number of racers. Through league-based self-play, agents evolve sophisticated anticipatory behaviors, including proactive collision avoidance, overtaking, and handling multi-agent physical interactions, including aerodynamic downwash. Our agents outperform a champion-level human pilot in multi-player races at speeds exceeding 22 m/s, while simultaneously reducing collision rates by 50 % compared to state-of-the-art single-agent baselines. Crucially, training with diverse artificial agents enables zero-shot generalization to safer human interaction. These results suggest that the path to robust robotic co-existence lies not in isolated safety constraints, but in the rigorous demands of multi-agent interaction. Multimedia materials are available at: https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多智能体强化学习实现超人级安全敏捷竞速
自主系统在隔离或仿真环境中已实现超人性能，但在共享、动态的真实世界空间中仍易脆弱。这一失败源于物理应用中占主导地位的单智能体范式，其中其他参与者被忽略或视为环境噪声，阻碍了有效协作。本文表明，多智能体强化学习为真实世界交互提供了必要的安全基础。我们以高速四旋翼竞速作为高风险测试平台，训练智能体在可变数量的竞速者间导航复杂的空气动力学交互与策略性机动。通过基于联赛的自对弈，智能体演化出复杂的预判行为，包括主动避撞、超车及处理多智能体物理交互（如气流下洗）。我们的智能体在超过22米/秒的速度下，于多人竞速中超越冠军级人类飞行员，同时相比最先进的单智能体基线，碰撞率降低50%。关键的是，使用多样化的虚拟智能体训练可实现零样本泛化至更安全的人类交互。这些结果表明，实现机器人稳健共存之路不在于孤立的安全约束，而在于多智能体交互的严苛要求。多媒体材料见：https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of single-agent autonomous systems in dynamic real-world environments, this work leverages multi-agent reinforcement learning to enable safe and agile racing through coordinated interaction. Using high-speed quadrotor racing as a testbed, agents are trained via league-based self-play to handle complex aerodynamic effects and strategic maneuvers with variable numbers of competitors. The resulting agents surpass human champions in multi-player races at speeds over 22 m/s while reducing collisions by 50% compared to single-agent baselines, and generalize to human interaction without additional training.
为了解决单智能体系统在动态现实环境中脆弱的问题，该研究采用多智能体强化学习，通过高难度四旋翼竞速场景训练智能体协同应对复杂的空气动力学效应和策略性动作。智能体通过联赛式自对弈演化出主动避撞、超车等高级行为，在多人竞速中以超22米/秒的速度击败人类冠军级飞行员，同时碰撞率较现有单智能体方法降低50%，且能零样本泛化至安全的人机交互场景。
Turning Trust to Transactions: Tracking Affiliate Marketing and FTC Compliance in YouTube's Influencer Economy
Authors: Chen Sun, Yash Vekaria, Zubair Shafiq, Rishab Nithyanand
First: 2026-03-04T18:47:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:15:33+00:00
Comments: ICWSM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
YouTube has evolved into a powerful platform where creators monetize their influence through affiliate marketing, raising concerns about transparency and ethics, especially when creators fail to disclose their affiliate relationships. Although regulatory agencies like the US Federal Trade Commission (FTC) have issued guidelines to address these issues, non-compliance and consumer harm persist, and the extent of these problems remains unclear. In this paper, we introduce tools, developed with insights from recent advances in Web measurement and NLP research, to examine the state of the affiliate marketing ecosystem on YouTube. We apply these tools to a 10-year dataset of 2 million videos from nearly 540,000 creators, analyzing the prevalence of affiliate marketing on YouTube and the rates of non-compliant behavior. Our findings reveal that affiliate links are widespread, yet disclosure compliance remains low, with most videos failing to meet FTC standards. Furthermore, we analyze the effects of different stakeholders in improving disclosure behavior. Our study suggests that the platform is highly associated with improved compliance through standardized disclosure features. We recommend that regulators and affiliate partners collaborate with platforms to enhance transparency, accountability, and trust in the influencer economy.
