标题：从信任到交易：追踪YouTube影响者经济中的联盟营销与FTC合规性
YouTube已演变为一个强大的平台，创作者通过联盟营销变现影响力，引发了关于透明度和伦理的担忧，尤其是在创作者未披露其联盟关系时。尽管美国联邦贸易委员会（FTC）等监管机构已发布相关指南，但违规行为和消费者损害仍持续存在，其范围尚不明确。本文引入基于近期Web测量与自然语言处理研究进展开发的工具，以考察YouTube联盟营销生态系统的现状。我们对近10年近200万条视频（来自近54万创作者）的数据集应用这些工具，分析YouTube上联盟营销的普遍性及非合规行为率。研究发现，联盟链接广泛存在，但披露合规率仍很低，多数视频未达到FTC标准。此外，我们分析了不同利益相关方对改善披露行为的影响。研究表明，平台通过标准化披露功能与合规性提升高度相关。我们建议监管机构与联盟合作伙伴协同平台，以增强影响者经济中的透明度、问责制与信任。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns over transparency and FTC compliance in YouTube's affiliate marketing ecosystem, this study leverages Web measurement and NLP tools to analyze a 10-year dataset of 2 million videos from nearly 540,000 creators. The research finds affiliate links are common but disclosure compliance is low, with most videos failing to meet FTC standards. It further shows that platform interventions, such as standardized disclosure features, significantly improve compliance rates, suggesting that platform design can play a key role in enforcing ethical practices.
鉴于YouTube网红经济中联盟营销的透明度和FTC合规性问题，本研究结合网络测量与自然语言处理技术，分析了近54万创作者的200万条视频数据。研究发现联盟链接普遍但披露合规率极低，多数视频未达FTC标准。进一步分析表明，平台提供的标准化披露功能显著提升合规率，说明平台设计在引导创作者行为方面具有关键作用。
Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier
Authors: Berk Hayta, Hannah Laus, Simon Mittermaier, Felix Krahmer
First: 2026-05-21T17:15:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:15:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：证据深度学习中的插值损失：一种包含软最大分类器的不确定性估计简化框架
现实世界基于传感器的学习系统需要可靠且计算高效的不确定性估计。证据深度学习（EDL）通过Dirichlet分布建模类别概率，其中Dirichlet参数由神经网络映射预测，实现单次通过的不确定性估计。然而，该方法可能带来计算挑战，因为Dirichlet期望目标比标准监督学习损失更复杂，影响其分析与实现。我们通过近似EDL诱导的一阶经验风险最小化问题的目标，使用在Dirichlet均值处评估的插值损失，证明在温和假设下，近似误差随证据增长而衰减，适用于包括均方误差和交叉熵损失在内的广泛损失函数。作为特例，我们的分析为在不确定性估计中使用软最大提供了理论依据，因为在特定证据到Dirichlet映射下，本框架包含标准软最大分类器。我们在Google语音命令数据集上验证了所提出的简化目标，结果表明其预测准确性和选择性预测性能与经典EDL相当，同时使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程更易实现。据我们所知，这是首次通过EDL获得语音识别任务中覆盖-准确率权衡的实证分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for computationally efficient yet reliable uncertainty estimation in real-world sensor systems, this work simplifies Evidential Deep Learning by approximating its complex Dirichlet-based objective with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean. Under mild assumptions, this approximation error diminishes with increasing evidence and applies broadly to standard losses like mean-squared error and cross-entropy, including softmax as a special case. Experiments on the Google Speech Commands dataset confirm that the simplified framework matches classical EDL in predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance while being easier to implement using standard deep learning tools, and it is the first to empirically analyze coverage-accuracy trade-offs in speech recognition via EDL.
为满足现实传感器系统对高效且可靠不确定性估计的需求，本文通过在Dirichlet均值处评估插值损失，简化了基于Dirichlet分布的证据深度学习方法。该方法基于一阶经验风险最小化，并在温和假设下证明了近似误差随证据增加而减小，支持使用均方误差和交叉熵等标准损失函数。在Google语音命令数据集上的实验表明，该简化框架在预测准确性和选择性预测性能上与经典EDL相当，同时更易于使用标准深度学习工具实现，并首次在语音识别任务中提供了基于EDL的覆盖率-准确性权衡结果。
SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation
Authors: Javad Parsa, Enis Simsar, Amir Joudaki, Thomas Hofmann, André M. H. Teixeira
First: 2026-05-21T17:13:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:13:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SeqLoRA：用于持续多概念生成的双层正交自适应
参数高效的微调可快速个性化文本到图像扩散模型，但组合多个自定义概念仍因表示干扰而具挑战性。现有模块化方法要么依赖昂贵的后处理融合，要么冻结自适应子空间，限制了表达能力和概念保真度。为解决此权衡，我们提出顺序正则化LoRA（SeqLoRA），一种约束持续学习框架，通过双层优化联合优化LoRA因子。理论上，我们为算法建立了强收敛保证，并将残差层激活建模为矩阵次高斯过程，推导出灾难性遗忘的高概率界。我们进一步证明，从数据学习LoRA基底比冻结基底方法更有效降低残差干扰能量。在多概念图像生成实验中，SeqLoRA在最多101个概念下提升了身份保真度和可扩展性，同时避免昂贵融合并减少组合生成中的属性干扰。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of maintaining concept fidelity and avoiding representation interference when adapting text-to-image models to multiple custom concepts, the authors propose SeqLoRA, a bilevel optimization framework that jointly tunes LoRA factors to minimize residual interference. By modeling residual activations as a sub-Gaussian process and deriving theoretical bounds on catastrophic forgetting, SeqLoRA ensures stable learning while improving identity preservation. Experimental results show that SeqLoRA outperforms existing methods in multi-concept generation, preserving identity across up to 101 concepts without costly fusion or frozen subspaces, and reducing attribute interference in composed outputs.
为解决文本到图像扩散模型在持续多概念生成中因表示干扰导致的性能下降，SeqLoRA提出一种双层优化框架，联合调整LoRA因子并施加正交约束，避免昂贵的融合或冻结子空间。理论上，该方法证明了收敛性保障，并通过残差激活的子高斯过程建模推导出灾难性遗忘的概率界，同时表明从数据学习LoRA基底比冻结基底更有效降低干扰。实验表明，SeqLoRA在支持多达101个概念的同时，显著提升身份保真度与可扩展性，无需昂贵融合且减少属性干扰。
Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones
Authors: William Smits
First: 2026-05-21T17:11:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:11:19+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures, 5 appendix sections. Submitted to Data Mining and Knowledge Discovery (DAMI)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example). Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有局部自适应不确定性区域的三元决策树
决策树通过硬性二元阈值划分特征空间，对远离决策边界的实例和直接位于边界上的实例赋予相同的置信度。我们引入三元决策树，在每个分割节点增加一个以最优阈值为中心、半宽为delta的不确定性区域。该区域内实例的预测由两个子树加权融合生成，并被标记为边界不确定，提示下游应用可对这些预测进行差异化处理。关键的是，delta在每个节点处基于标准CART分割查找过程中已有的统计量本地计算，无需外部噪声指定。我们提出并评估了五种delta估计方法：质量平台（分割准则曲线平台宽度）、类别重叠（经验类别分布重叠）、增益比（分割质量相对于分割熵的比率）、节点自助法（节点级重采样下的阈值方差）和边界距离（受SVM启发的最近跨类别训练样本距离）。在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上进行5折交叉验证，所有五种方法结合概率路由均显著优于标准CART（Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p < 0.001）。边界距离方法效率最高（每单位边界不确定标记率获得0.104准确率提升），在72个数据集中胜出42个，且无需额外超参数。在三个Breiman合成基准数据集上的分析表明，边界距离方法在干净数据上具有自校准能力，而节点自助法和质量平台方法最能追踪理论不可约误差。在四个医学和金融数据集上的实验展示了实际价值：在乳腺X光筛查中，节点自助法通过将10.8%的筛查病例标记为边界不确定，实现了+0.71%的决定准确率提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of standard decision trees that assign uniform confidence regardless of proximity to decision boundaries, this work introduces ternary decision trees that incorporate locally-adaptive uncertainty zones around split thresholds. Each node computes a delta value using five distinct statistical methods—quality-plateau, class-overlap, gain-ratio, node-bootstrap, and margin—to define a region where predictions are blended from both child subtrees and flagged as uncertain. Evaluated across 72 datasets via 5-fold cross-validation, all methods significantly outperformed CART in decided accuracy (p < 0.001), with the margin method achieving the highest efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of uncertainty flagging) and requiring no extra hyperparameters. On synthetic benchmarks, margin self-calibrates on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best reflect theoretical irreducible error; on real-world medical and financial datasets, node-bootstrap improved mammography accuracy by +0.71% by flagging 10.8% of cases as uncertain.
为解决传统决策树在决策边界附近对样本置信度分配不区分的问题，本文提出一种三元决策树，每个节点围绕最优阈值设置局部自适应的不确定性区域，宽度为delta。位于该区域内的样本通过两个子树加权融合预测，并标记为边界不确定，提示下游应用可差异化处理。五种delta估计方法被评估：质量平台、类别重叠、增益比、节点自助法和边界距离法。在72个数据集上，所有方法均显著优于CART（p<0.001），其中边界距离法效率最高（每单位不确定标记获得0.104准确率提升），在42个数据集上胜出且无需额外超参数。在合成数据上，边界距离法在干净数据中自校准，而节点自助法和质量平台法更贴近理论不可约误差。在实际应用中，节点自助法在乳腺筛查中通过标记10.8%样本为不确定，使准确率提升0.71%。
Proxy-Based Approximation of Shapley and Banzhaf Interactions
Authors: Santo M. A. R. Thies, Hubert Baniecki, R. Teal Witter, Eyke Hüllermeier, Maximilian Muschalik, Fabian Fumagalli
First: 2026-05-21T17:09:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:09:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Shapley and Banzhaf interactions capture the complex dynamics inherent in modern machine learning applications. However, current estimators for these higher-order interactions trade off between speed and accuracy. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProxySHAP. ProxySHAP reconciles the high sample efficiency of tree-based proxy models with a principled path to consistency via residual correction. On a theoretical level, we derive a polynomial-time generalization of interventional TreeSHAP to compute exact interaction indices for tree ensembles, successfully bypassing exponential tree-depth dependencies in prior methods. Furthermore, we formally analyze the residual adjustment strategy, characterizing the specific conditions under which Maximum Sample Reuse (MSR) corrects proxy bias without its variance scaling exponentially with interaction size. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that ProxySHAP sets a new state-of-the-art standard for approximation quality, including in large-scale applications with thousands of features. By achieving the lowest error in both small- and large-budget regimes, ProxySHAP significantly outperforms the prior best estimators ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ, while also delivering superior performance on downstream explainability tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于代理模型的Shapley与Banzhaf交互近似
Shapley和Banzhaf交互捕捉了现代机器学习应用中固有的复杂动态。然而，当前这些高阶交互的估计方法在速度与精度之间存在权衡。为克服这一局限，我们提出了ProxySHAP。ProxySHAP将基于树的代理模型的高样本效率与通过残差校正实现的一致性路径相结合。理论上，我们推导出干预式TreeSHAP的多项式时间泛化形式，用于计算树集成的精确交互指标，成功绕过了先前方法中指数级的树深度依赖。此外，我们正式分析了残差调整策略，刻画了最大样本重用（MSR）在何种条件下能校正代理偏差，且其方差不会随交互规模指数增长。大量基准测试表明，ProxySHAP在近似质量上达到新标杆，包括在数千特征的大规模应用中。在小预算和大预算场景下，ProxySHAP均实现最低误差，显著优于此前最佳估计器ProxySPEX和KernelSHAP-IQ，同时在下游可解释性任务中也表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient yet accurate estimation of Shapley and Banzhaf interactions in complex ML models, the paper introduces ProxySHAP, a method that combines tree-based proxy models with residual correction to achieve both high sample efficiency and consistency. Theoretical analysis derives a polynomial-time generalization of TreeSHAP for exact interaction computation in tree ensembles, eliminating exponential depth dependencies. Empirical results show ProxySHAP achieves the lowest error across both small- and large-budget settings, outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods like ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ, and delivering superior downstream explainability performance.
为解决现代机器学习中Shapley与Banzhaf交互估计在效率与精度间的权衡问题，本文提出ProxySHAP方法，结合树模型代理与残差校正机制，在保持高采样效率的同时确保一致性。理论分析推导出树集成模型中多项式时间的交互计算方法，消除了先前方法的指数级树深度依赖。实验表明，ProxySHAP在小预算与大预算场景下均取得最低误差，显著优于ProxySPEX与KernelSHAP-IQ等现有方法，并在下游可解释性任务中表现更优。
The Distillation Game: Adaptive Attacks & Efficient Defenses
Authors: Youssef Allouah, Mahdi Haghifam, Sanmi Koyejo, Reza Shokri
First: 2026-05-21T17:09:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:09:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Distillation attacks create a deployment trade-off for model providers: the same outputs that make a model more useful can also make it easier to imitate. We study this trade-off through a minimax game between a utility-constrained teacher and an adaptive student. Our framework yields tractable one-sided response rules: an adaptive evaluation rule in which the student reweights high-value examples, and a teacher-side defense template that suppresses outputs most useful for distillation. From a cheap proxy for example value, we derive Product-of-Experts (PoE), a simple forward-pass-only defense that combines the teacher with a proxy student during generation. Empirically, adaptive evaluation reveals a large passive--adaptive gap: on state-of-the-art defenses, adaptive students recover substantially more capability than passive evaluation suggests on GSM8K and MATH. Under this stronger evaluation, the apparent robustness gap between expensive defenses and PoE narrows considerably, while PoE remains substantially cheaper and preserves higher-quality reasoning traces. Overall, our results suggest that strong distillation remains difficult to stop, and that progress on antidistillation should be judged against adaptive students rather than passive ones. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ysfalh/distillation-game.
中文标题/摘要
标题：蒸馏博弈：自适应攻击与高效防御
蒸馏攻击为模型提供者制造了部署权衡：使模型更具实用性的输出，也可能更容易被模仿。我们通过一个效用受限教师与自适应学生之间的极小极大博弈来研究这一权衡。我们的框架得出可处理的一侧响应规则：学生端的自适应评估规则，即重新加权高价值样本；以及教师端的防御模板，抑制最有利于蒸馏的输出。基于一个廉价的样本价值代理，我们推导出Product-of-Experts（PoE），一种仅需前向传播的简单防御方法，它在生成过程中结合教师与代理学生。实证表明，自适应评估揭示了显著的被动—自适应能力差距：在当前最先进防御上，自适应学生在GSM8K和MATH任务中恢复的能力远超被动评估所显示的水平。在更强的自适应评估下，昂贵防御与PoE之间的表观鲁棒性差距显著缩小，而PoE仍保持成本更低且保留更高质量推理轨迹。总体而言，我们的结果表明，强力蒸馏仍难以阻止，且对抗蒸馏的进展应以自适应学生而非被动学生为评判标准。代码见：https://github.com/ysfalh/distillation-game。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between model utility and distillation vulnerability, this work models the problem as a minimax game between a utility-constrained teacher and an adaptive student. The authors derive tractable response strategies: the student adaptively reweights high-value examples during evaluation, while the teacher defends by suppressing outputs most useful for imitation. They propose PoE, a lightweight forward-pass-only defense that combines the teacher with a proxy student during generation. Experiments show that adaptive evaluation reveals a large performance gap over passive evaluation, with adaptive students recovering significantly more capability on GSM8K and MATH. Under this stronger benchmark, PoE’s robustness gap with expensive defenses narrows considerably while maintaining lower cost and higher-quality reasoning traces.
为应对模型在提升实用性时同时增加被蒸馏风险的困境，该研究将问题建模为教师与自适应学生之间的极小极大博弈。作者推导出可计算的响应策略：学生在评估时动态加权高价值样本，教师则通过抑制易被模仿的输出进行防御。他们提出PoE防御方法，仅在前向传播阶段结合教师与代理学生生成输出，利用廉价代理评估样本价值。实验表明，自适应评估揭示了被动评估与自适应评估间的显著性能差距，在GSM8K和MATH上自适应学生恢复的能力远超被动评估。在更强的自适应评估下，昂贵防御与PoE的鲁棒性差距明显缩小，而PoE仍保持成本更低且推理轨迹质量更高。
Optimization over the intersection of manifolds
Authors: Yan Yang, Bin Gao, Ya-xiang Yuan
First: 2026-05-21T17:08:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:08:00+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optimization over the intersection of two manifolds arises in a broad range of applications, but is hindered by the coupled geometry of the feasible region. In this paper, we prove that the regularities -- clean intersection and intrinsic transversality -- are equivalent, which yields a tractable projection onto the tangent space of the intersection. Therefore, we propose a geometric method that employs a retraction on only one manifold and updates the iterate along two orthogonal directions. Specifically, the iterates stay on one manifold, and the two directions are responsible for asymptotically approaching the other manifold and decreasing the objective function, respectively. Under intrinsic transversality, we derive the convergence rate for both the feasibility and optimality measures, and show that every accumulation point is first-order stationary. Numerical experiments on problems stemming from sparse and low-rank optimization, including fitting spherical data, approximating hyperbolic embeddings on real data, and computing compressed modes, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在流形交集上的优化
在两个流形交集上的优化广泛应用于各类问题，但受限于可行区域的耦合几何结构。本文证明了正则性——干净交集与内在横截性——是等价的，从而得到一个可处理的交集切空间投影。因此，我们提出了一种几何方法，仅在其中一个流形上使用重映射，并沿两个正交方向更新迭代点。具体而言，迭代点保持在一个流形上，两个方向分别负责渐近逼近另一个流形和降低目标函数。在内在横截性条件下，我们推导了可行性与最优性度量的收敛速率，并证明每个极限点均为一阶平稳点。数值实验针对稀疏与低秩优化问题，包括球形数据拟合、真实数据上的双曲嵌入逼近及压缩模式计算，验证了所提方法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize over the intersection of two manifolds, which commonly appears in sparse and low-rank problems but is complicated by their coupled geometry, this paper establishes that clean intersection and intrinsic transversality are equivalent, enabling a tractable projection onto the intersection’s tangent space. The authors propose a geometric method that uses a retraction on one manifold and updates iterates along two orthogonal directions: one to approach the other manifold and one to decrease the objective. Under intrinsic transversality, the method guarantees convergence rates for feasibility and optimality, and ensures all accumulation points are first-order stationary. Experiments on spherical fitting, hyperbolic embedding, and compressed mode computation confirm its effectiveness.
为解决在两个流形交集上优化时因几何耦合带来的困难，本文证明了“干净交集”与“内在横截性”的等价性，从而实现对交集切空间的可计算投影。所提方法仅在其中一个流形上使用重映射，并沿两个正交方向更新迭代点：一个方向用于渐近逼近另一流形，另一个方向用于降低目标函数。在满足内在横截性条件下，该方法对可行性与最优性均有收敛速率保证，且所有极限点均为一阶平稳点。在球面拟合、超球嵌入及压缩模式计算等数值实验中，该方法表现有效。
ChronoMedKG: A Temporally-Grounded Biomedical Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Clinical Reasoning
Authors: Md Shamim Ahmed, Farzaneh Firoozbakht, Lukas Galke Poech, Jan Baumbach, Richard Röttger
First: 2026-05-21T17:04:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:04:28+00:00
Comments: 9 pages main text plus appendices, 8 figures. Dataset and benchmark paper. ChronoMedKG released under CC BY 4.0 and ChronoTQA/code under MIT (Zenodo: 10.5281/zenodo.19697542). Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) treat disease associations as static facts, but temporal information is crucial for clinical reasoning, e.g., a symptom diagnostic of one disease at age 3 may imply a different disease at age 13. Existing KGs such as PrimeKG, Hetionet, and iKraph do not encode when a finding becomes clinically relevant over the course of a disease. This limits their usefulness for longitudinal clinical reasoning and retrieval augmentation. We introduce ChronoMedKG, a temporal biomedical knowledge graph that contains 460,497 evidence-linked triples (filtered from 13M raw extractions) covering 13,431 diseases. Each association is tied to temporal components like onset window or progression stage, which are backed by PMID-traceable evidence and a multi-signal credibility score. The graph is constructed through a disease-autonomous multi-agent pipeline in which multiple frontier LLMs independently extract knowledge from PubMed and PMC literature. Only those relations are kept that are supported by multi-model consensus, survive credibility filtering, as well as ontology alignment. ChronoMedKG scored 92.7% agreement against Orphadata and adds temporal grounding for 6,250 diseases absent from HPOA, Orphadata, and Phenopackets, including 1,657 Orphanet-coded rare diseases. We further introduce ChronoTQA, a benchmark of 3,341 questions across eight task types (six temporal plus two static controls), with a 12-question supplementary probe. Frontier LLMs lose roughly 30 points moving from static to temporal questions; ChronoMedKG retrieval rescues 47-65% of their long-tail failures, against 17-29% for HPOA-RAG. As such, ChronoMedKG provides a crucial temporal axis for retrieval-augmented clinical systems that was previously absent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ChronoMedKG：一个具有时间锚定的生物医学知识图谱及临床推理基准
生物医学知识图谱（KGs）将疾病关联视为静态事实，但临床推理中时间信息至关重要，例如，3岁时的某症状可能在13岁时指向不同疾病。现有KG如PrimeKG、Hetionet和iKraph未编码疾病过程中某发现何时变得临床相关，这限制了其在纵向临床推理和检索增强中的实用性。 我们提出ChronoMedKG，一个包含460,497个证据关联三元组（从1300万原始提取中筛选）覆盖13,431种疾病的时序生物医学知识图谱。每个关联均绑定起始窗口或进展阶段等时间成分，且有PMID可追溯证据及多信号可信度评分支持。该图谱通过疾病自主多智能体管道构建，多个前沿大语言模型独立从PubMed和PMC文献中提取知识。仅保留经多模型共识支持、通过可信度过滤及本体对齐的关系。 ChronoMedKG与Orphadata一致性达92.7%，并为HPOA、Orphadata和Phenopackets中缺失的6,250种疾病提供时间锚定，包括1,657种Orphanet编码的罕见病。我们进一步引入ChronoTQA，一个包含3,341个问题、八类任务（六类时序加两类静态对照）及12题补充探针的基准测试。前沿大语言模型从静态问题转为时序问题时性能下降约30分；ChronoMedKG检索可挽救其长尾失败的47-65%，而HPOA-RAG仅挽救17-29%。因此，ChronoMedKG为检索增强型临床系统提供了此前缺失的关键时间轴。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to incorporate temporal dynamics into clinical reasoning, this paper introduces ChronoMedKG, a temporally-grounded biomedical knowledge graph built from 13M PubMed/PMC extractions, filtering to 460,497 evidence-linked triples covering 13,431 diseases, each annotated with onset windows or progression stages backed by PMID-traceable evidence and credibility scores. 为支持临床推理中的时间动态建模，本文提出ChronoMedKG，一个包含46万条带时间标注（如发病窗口、进展阶段）的医学知识图谱，由1300万篇文献提取并经多模型共识与可信度筛选后保留。图谱覆盖13,431种疾病，其中6,250种此前未被HPOA或Orphadata收录，含1,657种罕见病。与Orphadata对比准确率达92.7%。配套的ChronoTQA基准测试3,341道题，涵盖8类任务（含6类时序题）；前沿大模型在时序题上性能下降约30分，而ChronoMedKG检索可恢复47–65%失败案例，远超HPOA-RAG的17–29%。
HarnessAPI: A Skill-First Framework for Unified Streaming APIs and MCP Tools
Authors: Edwin Jose
First: 2026-05-21T17:03:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:03:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Every Python function deployed as an LLM tool must today exist in two forms: an HTTP endpoint for human-facing clients and CI pipelines, and an MCP tool registration for agent runtimes such as Claude and Cursor. These representations share business logic yet diverge in all the surrounding machinery (routing, validation, serialisation, streaming, and schema maintenance), and they drift apart as the underlying code evolves. We present HarnessAPI, a Python framework that eliminates this duplication by treating a typed skill folder as the single source of truth. From one handler.py plus Pydantic schemas, the framework automatically derives a streaming HTTP endpoint with Server-Sent Events, an interactive OpenAPI/Swagger UI, and a zero-configuration MCP tool, all served from a single process. Dual-mode content negotiation lets the same handler serve SSE-streaming and JSON-returning clients with no handler changes. A dynamic code-generation mechanism ensures Pydantic type annotations propagate correctly to FastMCP's inspection layer, resolving a technical limitation that prevents naive closure-based registration. Measured across six representative skills using cloc, HarnessAPI reduces framework-facing boilerplate by 74% compared with a manually maintained dual-stack implementation (FastAPI server + FastMCP server). HarnessAPI subclasses FastAPI, inheriting its full middleware, dependency-injection, and deployment ecosystem. It is available at https://github.com/edwinjosechittilappilly/harnessapi and on PyPI (pip install harnessapi)
中文标题/摘要
标题：HarnessAPI：面向统一流式API与MCP工具的技能优先框架
当前，每个部署为LLM工具的Python函数必须同时存在两种形式：面向人类客户端和CI流水线的HTTP端点，以及用于代理运行时（如Claude和Cursor）的MCP工具注册。这两种表示共享业务逻辑，但在周边机制（路由、验证、序列化、流式传输和模式维护）上存在差异，且随着底层代码演进而逐渐脱节。我们提出HarnessAPI，一个Python框架，通过将类型化的技能文件夹视为单一真实来源，消除这种重复。从一个handler.py文件和Pydantic模式，该框架可自动推导出支持Server-Sent Events的流式HTTP端点、交互式OpenAPI/Swagger UI，以及零配置MCP工具，所有服务均来自同一进程。双模式内容协商机制允许同一处理器无需修改即可服务SSE流式和JSON返回客户端。动态代码生成机制确保Pydantic类型注解正确传递至FastMCP的检查层，解决了阻止简单闭包注册的技术限制。在六个代表性技能上使用cloc测量，HarnessAPI相比手动维护的双栈实现（FastAPI服务器+FastMCP服务器）减少了74%的框架相关样板代码。HarnessAPI继承自FastAPI，拥有其完整的中间件、依赖注入和部署生态系统。可在https://github.com/edwinjosechittilappilly/harnessapi获取，或通过PyPI安装（pip install harnessapi）

Beyond Acoustic Emotion Recognition: Multimodal Pathos Analysis in Political Speech Using LLM-Based and Acoustic Emotion Models
Authors: Juergen Dietrich
First: 2026-05-21T17:03:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:03:37+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate whether acoustic emotion recognition models can serve as proxies for the Pathos dimension in political speech analysis, as operationalised by the TRUST multi-agent large language model (LLM) pipeline. Using a Bundestag plenary speech by Felix Banaszak (51 segments, 245 s) as a case study, we compare three analysis modalities: (1) emotion2vec_plus_large, an acoustic speech emotion recognition (SER) model whose continuous Arousal and Valence values are derived via post-hoc Russell Circumplex projection; (2) Gemini 2.5 Flash, an LLM analysing the full speech audio together with its transcript in an open-ended, context-aware fashion; and (3) TRUST-Pathos scores from a three-advocate LLM supervisor ensemble. Spearman rank correlations reveal that Gemini Valence correlates strongly with TRUST-Pathos (rho = +0.664, p < 0.001), whereas emotion2vec Valence does not (rho = +0.097, p = 0.499). We further demonstrate, via a systematic quality evaluation of the Berlin Database of Emotional Speech (EMO-DB) using Gemini in an open-ended annotation paradigm, that standard SER benchmark corpora suffer from acted speech, cultural bias, and category incompatibility. Our results suggest that LLM-based multimodal analysis captures semantically defined political emotion substantially better than acoustic models alone, while acoustic features remain informative for low-level Arousal estimation. Future work will extend this approach to video-based analysis incorporating facial expression and gaze.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越声学情绪识别：基于LLM与声学情绪模型的政治演讲多模态情感分析
我们探讨声学情绪识别模型是否可作为政治演讲分析中‘Pathos’维度的代理，该维度由TRUST多智能体大语言模型（LLM）管道定义。以联邦议院议员Felix Banaszak的全体会议演讲（51个片段，245秒）为案例，我们比较三种分析模式：(1) emotion2vec_plus_large，一种声学语音情绪识别（SER）模型，其连续的唤醒度与效价值通过事后Russell环形空间投影获得；(2) Gemini 2.5 Flash，一种在开放且上下文感知方式下同时分析完整语音音频及其转录文本的LLM；(3) 由三位倡导者LLM监督器组成的TRUST-Pathos评分。Spearman秩相关分析显示，Gemini效价与TRUST-Pathos高度相关（rho = +0.664, p < 0.001），而emotion2vec效价则无显著相关（rho = +0.097, p = 0.499）。我们进一步通过Gemini在开放标注范式下对柏林情绪语音数据库（EMO-DB）进行系统质量评估，表明标准SER基准语料库存在表演性语音、文化偏见及类别不兼容问题。结果表明，基于LLM的多模态分析在捕捉语义定义的政治情绪方面显著优于声学模型，而声学特征仍对低级唤醒度估计具有信息价值。未来工作将扩展此方法至视频分析，结合面部表情与注视方向。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether acoustic emotion recognition models can effectively proxy the Pathos dimension in political speech analysis, as defined by the TRUST LLM pipeline. Using a 245-second Bundestag speech segmented into 51 parts, the researchers compare three modalities: an acoustic SER model (emotion2vec_plus_large) projecting arousal and valence via Russell Circumplex, an LLM (Gemini 2.5 Flash) analyzing audio and transcript together, and TRUST-Pathos scores from a multi-LLM ensemble. Results show Gemini Valence correlates strongly with TRUST-Pathos (rho = +0.664, p < 0.001), while emotion2vec Valence does not (rho = +0.097, p = 0.499). Further evaluation of the EMO-DB corpus reveals acoustic models suffer from acted speech, cultural bias, and category mismatch, suggesting LLM-based multimodal analysis better captures semantic political emotion, though acoustic features remain useful for low-level arousal estimation.
为更准确分析政治演讲中的情感基调，本研究探讨声学情绪识别模型是否能作为TRUST多智能体大语言模型定义的Pathos维度代理。以德国联邦议院演讲为案例，比较了三种分析方式：声学SER模型（emotion2vec_plus_large）通过Russell环形空间投影获得连续的唤醒与效价值、LLM（Gemini 2.5 Flash）结合音频与文本进行开放式上下文分析、以及TRUST-Pathos多LLM监督器集成得分。结果显示，Gemini效价与TRUST-Pathos高度相关（rho = +0.664, p < 0.001），而emotion2vec效价无显著相关（rho = +0.097, p = 0.499）。对EMO-DB语料库的系统评估表明，声学模型存在表演性语音、文化偏见和类别不兼容问题。研究结论认为，基于LLM的多模态分析比声学模型更准确捕捉政治语境中的语义情绪，但声学特征仍对低级唤醒估计有参考价值。
Post-Training is About States, Not Tokens: A State Distribution View of SFT, RL, and On-Policy Distillation
Authors: Dong Nie
First: 2026-05-21T17:03:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:03:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model post-training methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT), reinforcement learning (RL), and distillation are often analyzed through their loss functions: maximum likelihood, policy gradients, forward KL, reverse KL, or related objective-level variants. We study a complementary factor: the state distribution on which supervision is applied. For an autoregressive policy, a state is a prompt plus generated prefix. SFT trains on fixed dataset states, while RL and on-policy distillation (OPD) train on states induced by the current learner. We formalize post-training as state-distribution shaping and run a controlled smallscale study using Qwen3-0.6B-Base on GSM8K, with TruthfulQA and MMLU as retention evaluations. Our results show three phenomena. First, a mild SFT run improves GSM8K with little forgetting, while a stress SFT run causes substantial retention loss. Second, OPD from a degraded SFT teacher surpasses that teacher on GSM8K, TruthfulQA, and MMLU, despite using the teacher as its only supervision source. Third, a lightweight on-policy RL run improves GSM8K while preserving retention. These results support a state-centric view of post-training: the source and locality of training states can be as important as the form of the supervision signal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：后训练关注状态而非标记：SFT、RL与在线蒸馏的状态分布视角
监督微调（SFT）、强化学习（RL）和蒸馏等大语言模型后训练方法常通过其损失函数（如最大似然、策略梯度、前向KL、反向KL或相关目标变体）进行分析。我们研究一个互补因素：监督所应用的状态分布。对于自回归策略，状态由提示与生成前缀组成。SFT在固定数据集状态上训练，而RL和在线蒸馏（OPD）则在当前学习者诱导的状态上训练。我们将后训练形式化为状态分布塑造，并在GSM8K数据集上使用Qwen3-0.6B-Base进行受控小规模研究，以TruthfulQA和MMLU作为保留能力评估。结果呈现三种现象：第一，轻度SFT训练可提升GSM8K表现且遗忘少，而高强度SFT训练则导致显著保留能力损失；第二，即使仅以退化SFT教师为监督源，OPD仍优于该教师在GSM8K、TruthfulQA和MMLU上的表现；第三，轻量级在线RL训练可提升GSM8K表现同时保留能力。这些结果支持后训练的状态中心视角：训练状态的来源与局部性可能与监督信号的形式同等重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that post-training methods like SFT, RL, and on-policy distillation are often evaluated through loss functions alone, this study investigates how the distribution of training states—comprising prompts and generated prefixes—affects model performance. Using Qwen3-0.6B-Base on GSM8K with TruthfulQA and MMLU as retention benchmarks, the authors find that mild SFT improves GSM8K with minimal forgetting, while aggressive SFT causes significant retention loss. Surprisingly, on-policy distillation from a degraded SFT teacher outperforms the teacher on all tasks, and a lightweight on-policy RL run enhances GSM8K without harming retention. These findings suggest that the state distribution during training, not just the supervision signal, critically shapes post-training outcomes.
该研究受启发于现有方法常仅通过损失函数分析SFT、RL和on-policy distillation，转而关注训练所依赖的状态分布（提示加生成前缀）。在GSM8K上使用Qwen3-0.6B-Base并以TruthfulQA和MMLU评估保留能力的实验表明：轻度SFT可提升GSM8K且遗忘少，而激进SFT导致显著遗忘；令人意外的是，从退化SFT教师处进行的on-policy distillation反而在三项任务上超越教师；轻量级on-policy RL亦能提升GSM8K同时保持保留能力。结果支持状态中心视角：训练状态的来源与局部性与监督信号形式同等重要。
LLM Readiness Harness: Evaluation, Observability, and CI Gates for LLM/RAG Applications
Authors: Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano
First: 2026-03-28T18:03:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:00:55+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 4 figures, 15 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a readiness harness for LLM and RAG applications that turns evaluation into a deployment decision workflow. The system combines automated benchmarks, OpenTelemetry observability, and CI quality gates under a minimal API contract, then aggregates workflow success, policy compliance, groundedness, retrieval hit rate, cost, and p95 latency into scenario-weighted readiness scores with Pareto frontiers. We evaluate the harness on ticket-routing workflows and BEIR grounding tasks (SciFact and FiQA) with full Azure matrix coverage (162/162 valid cells across datasets, scenarios, retrieval depths, seeds, and models). Results show that readiness is not a single metric: on FiQA under sla-first at k=5, gpt-4.1-mini leads in readiness and faithfulness, while gpt-5.2 pays a substantial latency cost; on SciFact, models are closer in quality but still separable operationally. Ticket-routing regression gates consistently reject unsafe prompt variants, demonstrating that the harness can block risky releases instead of merely reporting offline scores. The result is a reproducible, operationally grounded framework for deciding whether an LLM or RAG system is ready to ship.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型就绪性工具：面向LLM/RAG应用的评估、可观测性与CI门禁
我们提出了一种面向LLM和RAG应用的就绪性工具，将评估转化为部署决策流程。该系统在最小API契约下整合自动化基准测试、OpenTelemetry可观测性与CI质量门禁，并将工作流成功率、策略合规性、依据性、检索命中率、成本及p95延迟聚合为加权就绪性评分，结合帕累托前沿。我们在工单路由工作流和BEIR依据性任务（SciFact与FiQA）上评估该工具，覆盖Azure矩阵全部162个有效单元（涵盖数据集、场景、检索深度、种子与模型）。结果表明，就绪性并非单一指标：在FiQA中，sla-first策略下k=5时，gpt-4.1-mini在就绪性与忠实性上领先，而gpt-5.2则付出显著延迟代价；在SciFact中，模型质量接近但操作上仍可区分。工单路由回归门禁持续拒绝不安全提示变体，证明该工具可阻止高风险发布，而非仅报告离线分数。最终成果是一个可复现、具备操作基础的框架，用于判断LLM或RAG系统是否具备发布条件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable, automated deployment decisions for LLM and RAG systems, the authors introduce a readiness harness that integrates automated benchmarks, OpenTelemetry observability, and CI gates under a minimal API contract. The system computes scenario-weighted readiness scores by aggregating metrics such as policy compliance, groundedness, retrieval hit rate, cost, and p95 latency using Pareto frontiers. Evaluated on ticket-routing workflows and BEIR grounding tasks (SciFact and FiQA) across full Azure matrix coverage, results show readiness is multi-dimensional: on FiQA with SLA-first policy at k=5, gpt-4.1-mini outperformed gpt-5.2 in readiness and faithfulness despite higher latency; on SciFact, models were operationally distinguishable despite similar quality. The harness also consistently blocked unsafe prompt variants in regression gates, proving its ability to prevent risky releases.
为实现LLM和RAG系统的可靠自动化部署决策，作者提出一个准备度工具集，整合自动化基准、OpenTelemetry可观测性和CI门禁，通过最小API契约统一管理。系统通过聚合策略合规性、接地性、检索命中率、成本和延迟等指标，结合帕累托前沿计算场景加权准备度得分。在票务路由和BEIR接地任务（SciFact和FiQA）上，覆盖Azure全矩阵（162/162有效单元），结果显示准备度是多维的：在FiQA SLA优先场景下，gpt-4.1-mini在准备度和忠实性上优于gpt-5.2，但后者延迟显著升高；在SciFact上，模型质量相近但操作上可区分。回归门禁持续拦截危险提示变体，证明该工具可阻止高风险发布而非仅报告离线分数。
Representation over Routing: Overcoming Surrogate Hacking in Multi-Timescale PPO
Authors: Jing Sun
First: 2026-04-15T06:03:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:58:14+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Temporal credit assignment in reinforcement learning has long been a central challenge. Inspired by the multi-timescale encoding of the dopamine system in neurobiology, recent research has sought to introduce multiple discount factors into Actor-Critic architectures, such as Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), to balance short-term responses with long-term planning. However, this paper reveals that blindly fusing multi-timescale signals in complex delayed-reward tasks can lead to severe algorithmic pathologies. We systematically demonstrate that exposing a temporal attention routing mechanism to policy gradients results in surrogate objective hacking, while adopting gradient-free uncertainty weighting triggers irreversible myopic degeneration, a phenomenon we term the Paradox of Temporal Uncertainty. To address these issues, we propose a Target Decoupling architecture: on the Critic side, we retain multi-timescale predictions to enforce auxiliary representation learning, while on the Actor side, we strictly isolate short-term signals and update the policy based solely on long-term advantages. Rigorous empirical evaluations across multiple independent random seeds in the LunarLander-v2 environment demonstrate that our proposed architecture achieves statistically significant performance improvements. Without relying on hyperparameter hacking, it consistently surpasses the ''Environment Solved'' threshold with minimal variance, completely eliminates policy collapse, and escapes the hovering local optima that trap single-timescale baselines. The source code to reproduce our experiments is publicly available at https://github.com/ben-dlwlrma/Representation-Over-Routing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：表示优于路由：克服多时间尺度PPO中的代理黑客
强化学习中的时间信用分配长期以来一直是核心挑战。受神经生物学中多时间尺度多巴胺系统的启发，近期研究试图在Actor-Critic架构（如近端策略优化PPO）中引入多个折扣因子，以平衡短期响应与长期规划。然而，本文揭示，在复杂的延迟奖励任务中盲目融合多时间尺度信号会导致严重的算法病态。我们系统性地证明，将时间注意力路由机制暴露于策略梯度会导致代理目标函数被篡改；而采用无梯度的不确定性加权则会引发不可逆的近视退化现象，我们称之为“时间不确定性悖论”。为解决这些问题，我们提出目标解耦架构：在Critic侧保留多时间尺度预测以强化辅助表示学习；在Actor侧严格隔离短期信号，仅基于长期优势更新策略。在LunarLander-v2环境中，通过多个独立随机种子的严格实证评估表明，所提架构实现统计显著的性能提升。无需依赖超参数调优，其表现始终稳定超越“环境已解决”阈值，方差极小，完全消除策略崩溃，并摆脱单时间尺度基线陷入的悬停局部最优。实验代码公开于https://github.com/ben-dlwlrma/Representation-Over-Routing。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to resolve temporal credit assignment in reinforcement learning, this paper investigates multi-timescale PPO architectures inspired by dopamine’s biological encoding. It identifies critical failures—surrogate objective hacking and myopic degeneration—when routing mechanisms are exposed to policy gradients or use gradient-free uncertainty weighting. To remedy this, the authors introduce a Target Decoupling architecture: the Critic retains multi-timescale predictions for auxiliary representation learning, while the Actor strictly isolates short-term signals and updates solely on long-term advantages. Experiments across multiple random seeds in LunarLander-v2 show statistically significant performance gains, consistent surpassing the 'Environment Solved' threshold with low variance, eliminating policy collapse, and avoiding local optima that trap standard baselines.
为解决强化学习中的时序信用分配问题，本文研究受多尺度多巴胺系统启发的多时间尺度PPO架构，发现当路由机制暴露于梯度或采用无梯度不确定性加权时，会出现代理目标篡改和近视退化等严重问题。为此，作者提出目标解耦架构：在评论家侧保留多时间尺度预测以辅助表征学习，而在行动者侧严格隔离短期信号，仅依据长期优势更新策略。在LunarLander-v2环境中多随机种子实验表明，该架构显著提升性能，稳定超越‘环境已解决’阈值，消除策略崩溃，避免单时间尺度基线陷入的局部最优。
Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning
Authors: Adrien Weihs, Hayden Schaeffer
First: 2026-05-21T16:57:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:57:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多神经算子在多任务学习中实现近最优速率
我们研究了在共享多任务设置下学习算子集合的逼近与统计复杂性，重点聚焦于多神经算子（MNO）架构。对于广泛类别的利普希茨多重算子映射，我们推导出逼近与统计泛化能力的近最优上界。在下界方面，我们建立了参数复杂性的诅咒并证明了相应的最小最大速率。这些结果共同表明，跨任务共享表示不会增加整体成本：多任务算子学习遵循与单算子学习相同的缩放规律。我们还比较了MNO与基于拼接任务输入的DeepONet多任务扩展版本，从最坏情况逼近复杂度视角看，两种架构满足几乎相同的渐近速率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the theoretical efficiency of multi-task operator learning, this work analyzes the approximation and statistical complexity of learning multiple operators under shared representations using the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. The authors derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and generalization for broad classes of Lipschitz operator maps, while also proving minimax lower bounds that reveal a curse of parametric complexity. Their results demonstrate that shared representations across tasks do not incur additional computational cost, as multi-task learning follows the same asymptotic scaling laws as single-task learning. Empirical comparisons with a concatenated-input DeepONet variant show both architectures achieve nearly identical worst-case approximation rates.
为理解多任务算子学习的理论效率，本文研究了在共享表示下学习多个算子的逼近与统计复杂性，重点分析了Multiple Neural Operators（MNO）架构。作者针对广泛类别的Lipschitz多算子映射，推导出逼近与泛化误差的近最优上界，并建立参数复杂度诅咒的下界及对应的最小最大收敛速率。结果表明，跨任务共享表示不会增加整体计算成本，多任务算子学习遵循与单任务学习相同的渐近缩放规律。实验对比显示，MNO与基于拼接输入的DeepONet扩展架构在最坏情况逼近复杂度上具有几乎相同的渐近速率。
The Value of Covariance Matching in Gaussian DDPMs and the Lanczos Sampler
Authors: Md Sahil Akhtar, Aymane El Gadarri, Vivek F. Farias, Adam D. Jozefiak
First: 2026-05-21T16:57:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:57:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A central error measure in Gaussian DDPMs is the path-space KL divergence between the exact reverse chain and the learned Gaussian reverse process. This quantity is especially relevant for procedures such as classifier guidance, which perturb the entire reverse trajectory rather than only the terminal sample. Prior analyses show that standard isotropic reverse covariances suffer an unavoidable $Ω(1/T)$ path-KL error as the number of denoising steps $T$ grows. We show that matching the full posterior covariance breaks this barrier, yielding an order-wise improvement that reduces the path KL to $O(1/T^2)$. To make full covariance matching practical, we introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method for sampling from the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products, which are available through Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense covariance storage and auxiliary covariance models. We prove that LGS approximation error decays exponentially in the number of Lanczos steps, where each Lanczos step requires a single Jacobian-vector product. Empirically, using only just three such steps improves sample quality over strong diagonal-covariance baselines, including OCM-DDPM, across standard image benchmarks. This identifies full covariance matching as both theoretically valuable and practically accessible for fast DDPM sampling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协方差匹配在高斯DDPM及Lanczos采样器中的价值
高斯DDPM中的核心误差度量是精确逆链与学习到的高斯逆过程之间的路径空间KL散度。该量对分类器引导等扰动整个逆轨迹而非仅终端样本的程序尤其重要。先前分析表明，标准各向同性逆协方差在去噪步数T增大时不可避免地产生Ω(1/T)的路径KL误差。我们证明，匹配完整后验协方差可突破此限制，实现阶数级改进，将路径KL降至O(1/T²)。为使完整协方差匹配实用，我们引入Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS），这是一种无需训练、无需矩阵存储的方法，仅通过后验均值的雅可比-向量积即可计算最优逆协方差下的采样，避免密集协方差存储及辅助协方差模型。我们证明LGS近似误差随Lanczos步数呈指数衰减，每步仅需一次雅可比-向量积。实验证明，仅用三步Lanczos即可在标准图像基准上超越包括OCM-DDPM在内的强对角协方差基线，提升样本质量。这表明完整协方差匹配在理论与实践中均具有价值且易于快速DDPM采样实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve sampling efficiency in Gaussian DDPMs, especially under perturbations like classifier guidance, this work demonstrates that matching the full posterior covariance rather than using isotropic covariances reduces path-space KL divergence from Ω(1/T) to O(1/T²). To enable practical implementation, the authors introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method that approximates the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products derived from Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense storage and auxiliary models, and its error decays exponentially with the number of Lanczos steps. Empirically, just three Lanczos steps significantly outperform diagonal-covariance baselines like OCM-DDPM on standard image benchmarks, confirming that full covariance matching is both theoretically sound and practically feasible for fast DDPM sampling.
为提升高斯DDPM在如分类器引导等扰动场景下的采样效率，本文证明匹配完整后验协方差可将路径空间KL散度从Ω(1/T)降至O(1/T²)。为实现实用化，作者提出Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS），一种无需训练、无需存储稠密协方差矩阵的方法，仅通过后验均值的雅可比-向量积即可计算协方差-向量积。理论分析表明LGS误差随Lanczos步数呈指数衰减，实验表明仅三步Lanczos即可显著优于OCM-DDPM等对角协方差基线，在标准图像数据集上提升样本质量，证实了完整协方差匹配的理论价值与工程可行性。
Spectral Dynamics in Deep Networks: Feature Learning, Outlier Escape, and Learning Rate Transfer
Authors: Clarissa Lauditi, Cengiz Pehlevan, Blake Bordelon
First: 2026-05-08T15:28:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:56:03+00:00
Comments: Updating related works + discussion
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the evolution of hidden-weight spectra in wide neural networks trained by (stochastic) gradient descent. We develop a two-level dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) that jointly tracks bulk and outlier spectral dynamics for spiked ensembles whose spike directions remain statistically dependent on the random bulk. We apply this framework to two settings: (1) infinite-width nonlinear networks in mean-field/$μ$P scaling and (2) deep linear networks in the proportional high-dimensional limit, where width, input dimension, and sample size diverge with fixed ratios. Our theory predicts how outliers evolve with training time, width, output scale, and initialization variance. In deep linear networks, $μ$P yields width-consistent outlier dynamics and hyperparameter transfer, including width-stable growth of the leading NTK mode toward the edge of stability (EoS). In contrast, NTK parameterization exhibits strongly width-dependent outlier dynamics, despite converging to a stable large-width limit. We show that this bulk+outlier picture is descriptive of simple tasks with small output channels, but that tasks involving large numbers of outputs (ImageNet classification or GPT language modeling) are better described by a restructuring of the spectral bulk. We develop a toy model with extensive output channels that recapitulates this phenomenon and show that edge of the spectrum still converges for sufficiently wide networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度网络中的谱动力学：特征学习、异常值逃逸与学习率迁移
我们研究了通过（随机）梯度下降训练的宽神经网络中隐藏权重谱的演化。我们开发了一种两级动力学平均场理论（DMFT），联合追踪尖峰集合中尖峰方向与随机主体部分统计相关时的总体与异常值谱动力学。我们将该框架应用于两种情形：（1）在平均场/μP缩放下的无限宽度非线性网络；（2）在比例高维极限下的深度线性网络，其中宽度、输入维度和样本量以固定比例发散。我们的理论预测了异常值如何随训练时间、宽度、输出尺度和初始化方差演化。在深度线性网络中，μP产生宽度一致的异常值动力学和超参数迁移，包括主导NTK模式向稳定性边缘（EoS）的宽度稳定增长。相比之下，NTK参数化虽收敛至大宽度稳定极限，但表现出强烈的宽度依赖性异常值动力学。我们表明，该总体+异常值图景适用于输出通道数小的简单任务，但涉及大量输出（如ImageNet分类或GPT语言建模）的任务更适合用谱总体重构描述。我们构建了一个具有大量输出通道的玩具模型，再现了这一现象，并证明在足够宽的网络中，谱边缘仍会收敛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding how spectral properties of hidden weights evolve during training in deep neural networks, the authors develop a two-level dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) to jointly track bulk and outlier spectral dynamics in spiked ensembles. Applied to both infinite-width nonlinear networks under mean-field/$μ$P scaling and deep linear networks in proportional high-dimensional limits, the theory predicts how outliers respond to training time, width, output scale, and initialization variance. Results show that under $μ$P scaling, outlier dynamics remain width-consistent and hyperparameters transfer well, with the leading NTK mode growing toward the edge of stability (EoS); in contrast, NTK parameterization exhibits strong width dependence despite converging to a stable large-width limit. The bulk+outlier model works for small-output tasks but fails for large-output tasks like ImageNet or GPT, where spectral bulk restructuring dominates — a phenomenon reproduced in a toy model showing convergence to the EoS only for sufficiently wide networks.
为理解深度神经网络训练过程中隐藏权重谱的演化，作者构建了两级动力学平均场理论（DMFT），联合追踪尖峰集合中本体与异常值谱的动态。该理论应用于无限宽非线性网络（mean-field/μP缩放）和深度线性网络（比例高维极限），预测异常值随训练时间、宽度、输出尺度和初始化方差的变化。结果显示，在μP缩放下，深度线性网络表现出宽度一致的异常值动态和超参数迁移，包括主导NTK模式向稳定性边缘稳定增长；而NTK参数化虽收敛于大宽度极限，但异常值动态强烈依赖宽度。该本体+异常值模型适用于小输出通道任务，但在大输出任务（如ImageNet或GPT）中失效，此时谱本体重构主导；一个具有大量输出的玩具模型证实，足够宽的网络中谱边缘仍会收敛。
N3P: Accelerated Automated Parking via a Learning-Based Naturalistic Three-Stage Scheme
Authors: Yifan Xue, Toktam Mohammadnejad, Faizan M Tariq, Sangjae Bae, David Isele, Yosuke Sakamoto, Nadia Figueroa, Jovin D'sa
First: 2026-05-21T16:56:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:56:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE Intelligent Transportation Systems Conference (ITSC 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous parking requires efficient path planning that ensures kinematic feasibility and collision avoidance in constrained environments. Hybrid A* is widely used but computationally expensive, while reinforcement learning (RL) methods lack reliability and often struggle with long-horizon geometric constraints, leading to suboptimal trajectories. We present N3P, a fast learning-based three-stage framework for automated parking. By introducing an intermediate preparatory pose and using a learning module to predict it, N3P decomposes the maneuver into simpler subproblems, thereby reducing computational complexity and accelerating path generation. We validate the framework by integrating it with Hybrid A* algorithms. Experiments in perpendicular and parallel parking scenarios show that N3P-enhanced Hybrid A* speeds up planning by more than 80%. It also outperforms RL baselines in success rate and trajectory quality, producing shorter trajectories with fewer gear changes, while achieving comparable or lower planning time in most cases.
中文标题/摘要
标题：N3P：基于学习的自然三阶段方案加速自动泊车
自动驾驶泊车需要高效路径规划，以确保在受限环境中运动学可行性和避障。混合A*算法虽常用但计算成本高，而强化学习方法可靠性不足，常难以处理长时域几何约束，导致轨迹次优。本文提出N3P，一种快速学习型三阶段框架用于自动泊车。通过引入中间预备姿态并利用学习模块预测该姿态，N3P将操作分解为更简单的子问题，从而降低计算复杂度并加速路径生成。我们通过与混合A*算法集成验证该框架。在垂直和平行泊车场景的实验表明，N3P增强的混合A*规划速度提升超80%。其成功率和轨迹质量优于强化学习基线，生成更短轨迹、换挡次数更少，且在多数情况下规划时间相当或更低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of Hybrid A* and the unreliability of reinforcement learning in geometrically constrained parking, N3P introduces a learning-based three-stage framework that decomposes parking into simpler subproblems via an intermediate preparatory pose predicted by a neural module. Integrated with Hybrid A* in experiments across perpendicular and parallel parking, N3P accelerates path generation by over 80% and outperforms RL baselines in success rate and trajectory quality, producing shorter paths with fewer gear shifts while maintaining or reducing planning time.
为解决混合A*算法计算效率低和强化学习在几何约束环境下可靠性差的问题，N3P提出一种基于学习的三阶段框架，通过预测中间预备姿态将泊车任务分解为更简单的子问题，从而降低计算复杂度并加速路径生成。与混合A*结合后，N3P在垂直和平行泊车场景中规划速度提升超80%，同时在成功率和轨迹质量上优于强化学习基线，生成更短路径、换挡次数更少，且规划时间通常相当或更低。
Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems via Decentralized Memory
Authors: Guangya Hao, Yunbo Long, Zhuokai Zhao
First: 2026-05-21T16:55:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:55:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-evolving multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising route to LLM agents that continually improve from experience, with persistent memory at their foundation. However, existing designs almost exclusively adopt a centralized repository shared across agents, incurring communication and coordination overhead, raising privacy concerns, and collapsing agent diversity. We propose DecentMem, a decentralized memory framework in which each agent maintains its own dual-pool memory -- an exploitation pool of consolidated past trajectories and an exploration pool of LLM-generated candidates for unseen contexts. The two pools are reweighted online based on stage-wise feedback from an LLM-as-a-judge. Theoretically, we prove that this design guarantees global reachability of the solution space and achieves $O(\log T)$ cumulative regret, matching the stochastic bandit lower bound up to constants. In practice, across three MAS frameworks (AutoGen, DyLAN, AgentNet), three Qwen3 backbones (4B/8B/14B), two Gemma4 backbones (E2B/E4B) and five benchmarks spanning math, code, QA, and embodied tasks, DecentMem improves average accuracy by up to 23.8% over the strongest centralized memory baseline and by up to 52.5% over the no-memory baseline, while reducing token usage by up to 49%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过去中心化记忆实现自演化的多智能体系统
自演化的多智能体系统（MAS）已成为一种有前景的路径，使大语言模型（LLM）代理能够从经验中持续改进，其基础是持久记忆。然而，现有设计几乎都采用跨代理共享的集中式存储库，导致通信与协调开销、隐私担忧，并削弱代理多样性。我们提出DecentMem，一种去中心化记忆框架，每个代理维护自己的双池记忆——一个包含整合历史轨迹的利用池，以及一个包含LLM生成的、用于未见情境候选方案的探索池。两个池根据LLM作为裁判提供的阶段性反馈在线重新加权。理论上，我们证明该设计保证解空间的全局可达性，并实现$O(\log T)$累积遗憾，达到随机多臂老虎机下界（常数因子内）。实践中，在三个MAS框架（AutoGen、DyLAN、AgentNet）、三个Qwen3主干（4B/8B/14B）、两个Gemma4主干（E2B/E4B）和五个涵盖数学、代码、问答及具身任务的基准测试中，DecentMem相比最强的集中式记忆基线平均准确率提升最高达23.8%，相比无记忆基线提升最高达52.5%，同时将token使用量降低最高达49%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, privacy-preserving, and diverse self-evolving multi-agent systems, the paper introduces DecentMem, a decentralized memory framework where each agent maintains two memory pools: one for consolidated past experiences and one for novel LLM-generated candidates. These pools are dynamically reweighted using feedback from an LLM-as-a-judge, enabling adaptive learning without centralized coordination. Theoretical analysis confirms global reachability and near-optimal regret bounds. Experimentally, DecentMem outperforms centralized memory baselines by up to 23.8% accuracy and no-memory baselines by up to 52.5% across diverse agent frameworks and benchmarks, while cutting token usage by up to 49%.
为实现可扩展、隐私保护且保持多样性的自进化多智能体系统，作者提出DecentMem框架，每个智能体独立维护两个记忆池——一个用于整合历史轨迹，一个用于生成探索候选方案，并由LLM作为裁判在线调整权重。理论上，该设计保证解空间全局可达性，累积遗憾为O(log T)，达到随机多臂老虎机下界。实验表明，在多种智能体框架与LLM模型组合下，DecentMem相较集中式记忆基线准确率提升最高达23.8%，相较无记忆基线提升最高达52.5%，同时节省令牌使用量最高达49%。
Can AI Make Conflicts Worse? An Alignment Failure in LLM Deployment Across Conflict Contexts
Authors: Andrii Kryshtal
First: 2026-05-21T16:55:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:55:39+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 8 pages, 2 figures. Code and evaluation framework: https://github.com/akryshtal/conflict-sensitivity-eval-bloom
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AI models are already deployed in societies affected by armed conflict, and journalists, humanitarian workers, governments and ordinary citizens rely on them for information or for their work processes. No established practice exists for checking whether their outputs can make those conflicts worse. We tested nine model configurations from four providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, xAI) on 90 multi-turn scenarios designed to surface misaligned behaviour in conflict contexts: false equivalence between documented atrocities, denial of genocide, and failure to recognise ethnic slurs, among others. When such outputs feed into journalism, humanitarian reporting, or public debate, they can deepen divisions in fragile societies. Failure rates span 6\% to 47\% between the best and worst performing models, which makes model choice a safety question in its own right and when users pushed for ``balance'' in cases where international courts have already assigned responsibility, five of nine configurations failed 80 to 100 percent of the time. We release the first evaluation framework for this domain and propose adding it to alignment evaluation portfolios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能会让冲突加剧吗？大语言模型在冲突情境中的对齐失败
人工智能模型已在受武装冲突影响的社会中部署，记者、人道工作者、政府和普通民众依赖它们获取信息或完成工作。目前尚无既定实践用于检验其输出是否会加剧冲突。我们测试了四家供应商（OpenAI、Anthropic、DeepSeek、xAI）的九种模型配置，在90个多轮场景中检测其在冲突情境下的对齐失败行为，如对已记录暴行的虚假等同、否认种族灭绝、未能识别种族侮辱等。当此类输出进入新闻报道、人道主义报道或公共辩论时，可能加深脆弱社会的分裂。模型表现从6%到47%不等，模型选择本身即构成安全问题；当用户要求在国际法庭已认定责任的案例中保持“平衡”时，九种配置中有五种失败率达80%至100%。我们发布了该领域的首个评估框架，并建议将其纳入对齐评估体系。
