鉴于大型语言模型在冲突地区部署可能加剧社会分裂，研究者对四家厂商的九种模型配置在90个精心设计的多轮场景中进行测试，以暴露其在冲突语境下的对齐失败行为，如制造虚假等价、否认种族灭绝或忽视种族侮辱。结果显示，模型失败率介于6%至47%之间，当用户要求在国际法院已定责的场合保持“平衡”时，五种配置的失败率高达80%至100%。该研究首次提出冲突敏感性对齐评估框架，并主张在冲突环境中选择模型本身即构成安全关键决策。
Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification
Authors: Mahdi Nasermoghadasi
First: 2026-05-21T16:55:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:55:27+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).
中文标题/摘要
标题：阅读任务失败的激活分析：对GPT-2 Small在间接宾语识别任务中稀疏特征的审计
我们报告了一项小型、可复现的审计，分析GPT-2 Small在间接宾语识别（IOI）任务中失败与成功试次上，哪些稀疏自动编码器（SAE）特征激活不同。在300个提示下，GPT-2 Small准确率为79.7%；Bloom（2024）发布的第8层残差流SAE中，24,576个特征中有146个通过Holm校正显著性阈值，105个达到大效应量（|Cohen's d| > 0.8）。最强相关特征——特征17,491（d=+2.93，Neuronpedia标签为'加密密钥'）——仅在提示中转移对象为'钥匙'时激活，此时GPT-2 Small失败率达93.3%，而其他七种对象仅7.5%（Fisher精确检验p=8.79×10^-33）。我们对该相关性进行了三项机制性检验：(i) 因果消融：在45个'钥匙'提示的所有token位置中将特征17,491置零，未恢复准确率（6.7%→4.4%）；该特征为相关性而非充分原因。(ii) 表示基线：对原始768维残差流进行逻辑回归，ROC AUC为0.929，与前100个SAE特征（0.927）相当；SAE基底提升可解释性，而非预测能力。(iii) 随机种子鲁棒性检验：五次随机种子下，'钥匙'子集失败率稳定在75.0–93.3%（行为效应真实），但特征17,491仅在5次中1次为最大|d|特征。因此，方法论贡献在于审计流程（低成本、模型无关、揭示命名相关特征），而非通过该流程发现的单个特征。我们公开代码、300提示语语料库、300×24,576激活矩阵、消融与基线脚本及图表。完整流程可在笔记本电脑（Apple M3 Max，无独立显卡）上运行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding why GPT-2 small fails on indirect object identification tasks, this study audits sparse-autoencoder features in layer 8 to identify which ones correlate with task failure. Using 300 prompts, it finds 146 features significantly associated with failure, including feature 17,491 (labeled 'cryptographic keys') with a Cohen’s d of +2.93, which is active only when the prompt references 'the keys,' leading to 93.3% failure rate versus 7.5% for other objects. Three controls validate that this feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause: ablating it doesn’t restore accuracy, logistic regression on raw activations matches SAE performance, and the feature’s prominence varies across random seeds. The key contribution is the audit pipeline, which is model-agnostic, cheap, and reproducible on a laptop.
为理解GPT-2 small在间接宾语识别任务中的失败原因，研究审计了第8层残差流中的稀疏自动编码器特征，发现146个特征在失败与成功试次中激活模式显著不同，其中特征17,491（标签为“加密密钥”）效应量达+2.93，仅在提示中出现“钥匙”时激活，导致93.3%失败率。三项控制实验表明该特征仅为相关因素而非充分原因；原始768维残差流的逻辑回归表现与前100个SAE特征相当；失败率在不同随机种子下稳定（75.0–93.3%），但该特征仅在1/5次运行中为最高效应量。研究的核心贡献是审计流程，而非特定特征，因其廉价、模型无关且能揭示可解释的相关因素。
WorldKV: Efficient World Memory with World Retrieval and Compression
Authors: Jung Yi, Minjae Kim, Paul Hyunbin Cho, Wooseok Jang, Sangdoo Yun, Seungryong Kim
First: 2026-05-21T16:55:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:55:04+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/WorldKV/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autoregressive video diffusion models have enabled real-time, action-conditioned world generation. However, sustaining a persistent world, where revisiting a previously seen viewpoint yields consistent content, remains an open problem. Full KV-cache attention preserves this consistency but breaks real-time constraints: memory footprint and attention cost grow linearly with rollout length. Sliding window inference restores throughput but discards long-term consistency. We propose WorldKV, a training-free framework with two components: World Retrieval and World Compression. World Retrieval stores evicted KV-cache chunks in GPU/CPU memory and selectively retrieves scene-relevant chunks via camera/ action correspondence, inserting them back into the native attention window without re-encoding. World Compression prunes redundant tokens within each chunk via key-key similarity to an anchor frame, halving per-chunk storage to fit 2x more history under a fixed budget. On Matrix-Game-2.0 and LingBot- World-Fast, WorldKV matches or exceeds full-KV memory fidelity at roughly 2x the throughput, and is competitive with memory-trained baselines without any fine-tuning. Project Page: https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/WorldKV/
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldKV：基于世界检索与压缩的高效世界记忆
自回归视频扩散模型已实现实时、动作条件下的世界生成。然而，维持一个持久的世界——即重新访问先前视角时内容保持一致——仍是一个开放性问题。完整的KV缓存注意力可保持一致性，但会破坏实时性：内存占用和注意力开销随展开长度线性增长。滑动窗口推理可恢复吞吐量，但会丢失长期一致性。我们提出WorldKV，一个无需训练的框架，包含两个组件：世界检索与世界压缩。世界检索将被逐出的KV缓存块存储在GPU/CPU内存中，并通过相机/动作对应关系选择性检索场景相关块，将其重新插入原注意力窗口而不需重新编码。世界压缩通过键-键相似度对每个块内冗余token进行剪枝，以锚定帧为基准，将每块存储量减半，在固定预算下可容纳两倍历史记录。在Matrix-Game-2.0和LingBot-World-Fast上，WorldKV在约两倍吞吐量下达到或超越完整KV内存的保真度，并在无需微调的情况下与内存训练基线相当。项目页面：https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/WorldKV/
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of maintaining consistent world states in real-time autoregressive video diffusion models while avoiding linear memory and attention cost growth, WorldKV introduces a training-free framework combining World Retrieval and World Compression. World Retrieval stores evicted KV-cache chunks in GPU/CPU memory and selectively retrieves relevant chunks based on camera and action correspondence, reinserting them into the attention window without re-encoding. World Compression reduces per-chunk storage by pruning redundant tokens via key-key similarity to an anchor frame, effectively doubling the history capacity under fixed memory budgets. Experiments on Matrix-Game-2.0 and LingBot-World-Fast show that WorldKV achieves or surpasses full-KV memory fidelity at approximately twice the throughput, and competes with memory-trained baselines without fine-tuning.
为解决自回归视频扩散模型在保持世界状态一致性的同时避免内存与注意力开销线性增长的问题，WorldKV 提出了一种无需训练的框架，包含世界检索与世界压缩两个模块。世界检索将被驱逐的 KV 缓存块存储在 GPU/CPU 内存中，并根据相机与动作对应关系选择性地重新插入相关块，从而在不重新编码的前提下维持长期一致性。世界压缩通过以锚帧为基准的键-键相似度剪枝每个块内冗余 token，将每块存储空间减半，从而在固定预算下容纳两倍历史数据。在 Matrix-Game-2.0 和 LingBot-World-Fast 上的实验表明，WorldKV 在约两倍吞吐量下达到全 KV 内存保真度，并在无需微调的情况下与记忆训练基线相当或更优。
Live Music Diffusion Models: Efficient Fine-Tuning and Post-Training of Interactive Diffusion Music Generators
Authors: Zachary Novack, Stephen Brade, Haven Kim, Hugo Flores García, Nithya Shikarpur, Chinmay Talegaonkar, Suwan Kim, Valerie K. Chen, Julian McAuley, Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick, Cheng-Zhi Anna Huang
First: 2026-05-21T16:54:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:54:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interactive streaming music generation promises the use of generative models for live performance and co-creation that is impossible with offline models. However, SOTA models exist in the discrete-AR regime, requiring industrial levels of compute for both training and inference. In this work, we investigate whether audio diffusion models, with their wide support in the open-source community but non-streaming bidirectional nature, can be repurposed efficiently into interactive models accessible on consumer hardware. By taking a critical look at the modern pipeline for block-wise outpainting diffusion, we identify critical inefficiencies during inference that result in strictly worse computational efficiency than their discrete-AR counterparts. We propose Live Music Diffusion Models (LMDMs), a simple modification of the generative diffusion process that recovers, and then outperforms, the inference complexity of the discrete Live Music Models (LMMs) through block-wise KV Caching. Unlike LMMs, LMDMs further enable stable post-training alignment through our novel ARC-Forcing paradigm, reducing error accumulation without any explicit RL or reward models. We demonstrate the application of LMDMs in a number of creative domains, including text-conditioned generation, sketch-based music synthesis, and jamming. We finally show how LMDMs can be used as a generative instrument in a real artist-AI collaboration, utilizing LMDMs as a "generative delay" to transform musicians' improvisation live for variable timbral effects while running locally on a consumer gaming laptop.
中文标题/摘要
标题：实时音乐扩散模型：交互式扩散音乐生成器的高效微调与后训练
交互式流媒体音乐生成有望利用生成模型实现现场表演与共创，这是离线模型无法实现的。然而，当前最先进的模型存在于离散自回归框架中，训练和推理均需工业级算力。本文探讨音频扩散模型是否能高效改造为适用于消费级硬件的交互式模型。通过分析现代块级外推扩散流程，我们发现推理阶段存在关键低效环节，导致其计算效率劣于离散自回归模型。我们提出实时音乐扩散模型（LMDMs），通过对生成扩散过程的简单修改，结合块级KV缓存，不仅恢复了离散实时音乐模型（LMMs）的推理复杂度，且表现更优。与LMMs不同，LMDMs通过我们提出的ARC-强制范式，进一步实现稳定后训练对齐，无需显式强化学习或奖励模型即可减少误差累积。我们展示了LMDMs在多个创意领域的应用，包括文本条件生成、草图音乐合成与即兴演奏。最后，我们演示了LMDMs如何作为生成乐器用于真实艺术家与AI协作，通过本地运行于消费级游戏笔记本，将音乐家即兴演奏实时转化为多变音色效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, real-time music generation on consumer hardware, this work explores whether audio diffusion models—despite their non-streaming bidirectional nature—can be adapted into interactive systems. The authors identify inefficiencies in standard diffusion inference pipelines and propose Live Music Diffusion Models (LMDMs), which modify the diffusion process to leverage block-wise KV Caching, achieving inference efficiency comparable to or better than discrete autoregressive models. LMDMs further enable stable post-training alignment via ARC-Forcing, eliminating error accumulation without reinforcement learning. Experiments show LMDMs successfully support text-conditioned generation, sketch-based synthesis, and live jamming, and are demonstrated in a real artist-AI collaboration where they function as a generative delay on a gaming laptop, producing variable timbral effects in real time.
为实现消费级硬件上的实时音乐生成，本文探讨音频扩散模型是否能被高效改造为交互式系统。作者发现现有扩散推理流程存在效率瓶颈，提出Live Music Diffusion Models（LMDMs），通过块级KV缓存优化扩散生成过程，推理效率可媲美甚至超越离散自回归模型。LMDMs还引入ARC-Forcing机制，无需强化学习即可实现稳定对齐，减少误差累积。实验表明LMDMs支持文本引导生成、草图音乐合成与即兴伴奏，并在真实艺术家协作中作为生成延迟模块运行于游戏笔记本，实时实现音色变化效果。
Findings of the Counter Turing Test: AI-Generated Image Detection
Authors: Rajarshi Roy, Nasrin Imanpour, Ashhar Aziz, Shashwat Bajpai, Gurpreet Singh, Shwetangshu Biswas, Kapil Wanaskar, Parth Patwa, Subhankar Ghosh, Shreyas Dixit, Nilesh Ranjan Pal, Vipula Rawte, Ritvik Garimella, Amitava Das, Amit Sheth, Vasu Sharma, Aishwarya Naresh Reganti, Vinija Jain, Aman Chadha
Venue: AAAI 2025
First: 2026-05-20T06:32:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:53:24+00:00
Comments: Defactify4 @AAAI 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid advancements in generative AI technologies, such as Stable Diffusion, DALL-E, and Midjourney, have significantly transformed the creation of synthetic visual content. While these models enable innovation across industries, they also pose serious challenges, including misinformation, disinformation, and biased content generation. The increasing realism of AI-generated images makes their detection a pressing concern for researchers, policymakers, and industry stakeholders. In this paper, we present the findings of the Defactify 4.0 workshop, which introduced the Counter Turing Test (CT2) for AI-Generated Image Detection. The competition consisted of two key tasks: (1) binary classification of images as either AI-generated or real and (2) identification of the specific generative model responsible for an AI-generated image. To facilitate this, we developed the MS COCOAI dataset, consisting of 50,000 synthetic images from multiple generative models alongside real-world images from the MS COCO dataset. Participants employed diverse detection strategies, including convolutional neural networks (CNNs), Vision Transformers (ViTs), frequency-based analysis, contrastive learning, and multimodal techniques. The results demonstrated that while AI-generated images can be detected with high accuracy (F1-score > 0.83), identifying the exact model used remains significantly more challenging (highest F1-score: 0.4986). These findings highlight the need for improved model fingerprinting, adversarial robustness, and real-time detection mechanisms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗图灵测试：AI生成图像检测研究结果
随着Stable Diffusion、DALL-E和Midjourney等生成式AI技术的快速发展，合成视觉内容的创作方式已发生显著变化。尽管这些模型推动了各行业的创新，但也带来了虚假信息、误导性内容和偏见生成等严峻挑战。AI生成图像日益逼真，使其检测成为研究人员、政策制定者和行业利益相关者亟需关注的问题。 本文介绍了Defactify 4.0研讨会中提出的对抗图灵测试（CT2）用于AI生成图像检测。该竞赛包含两个核心任务：（1）将图像二分类为AI生成或真实图像；（2）识别AI生成图像所使用的具体生成模型。为支持该研究，我们构建了MS COCOAI数据集，包含50,000张来自多种生成模型的合成图像，以及来自MS COCO数据集的真实世界图像。 参赛者采用了多种检测策略，包括卷积神经网络（CNN）、视觉Transformer（ViT）、基于频域的分析、对比学习和多模态技术。结果显示，AI生成图像的检测准确率较高（F1分数>0.83），但精确识别所用模型仍极具挑战性（最高F1分数：0.4986）。这些发现凸显了改进模型指纹识别、对抗鲁棒性和实时检测机制的必要性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the proliferation of realistic AI-generated images from models like Stable Diffusion and DALL-E, this study addresses the urgent need to detect synthetic visuals amid growing concerns over misinformation. The Counter Turing Test (CT2) competition, hosted at AAAI 2025, evaluated two tasks: binary classification of AI vs. real images and model attribution. Using the MS COCOAI dataset of 50,000 synthetic and real images, participants applied CNNs, ViTs, frequency analysis, and multimodal methods. Results showed high detection accuracy (F1 > 0.83) but poor model identification (F1 = 0.4986), underscoring the need for better fingerprinting and robust detection systems.
鉴于AI生成图像日益逼真及其带来的社会风险，本研究提出反图灵测试（CT2）以评估检测能力。基于包含5万张合成与真实图像的MS COCOAI数据集，参赛者采用CNN、ViT、频域分析及多模态方法进行AI图像二分类及模型溯源。结果显示二分类准确率较高（F1>0.83），但模型识别难度大（F1=0.4986），凸显了模型指纹识别与鲁棒检测机制亟待提升。
Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative
Authors: Jorge Fandinno, Yuliya Lierler, Torsten Schaub
First: 2026-05-21T16:52:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:52:18+00:00
Comments: To appear in Theory and Practice of Logic Programming
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：参数化模块化答案集程序的声明式表达
本文探讨一阶答案集编程（ASP）中的模块化概念，引入一种名为参数化模块逻辑程序的新形式化方法，允许定义带参数和内涵性语句的子程序。我们展示了该形式化方法如何捕捉具有集体控制的clingo程序语义，这一特性支持子程序的结构化与实例化。我们为模块化ASP提供了理论基础，展示了其实用性，并与传统非模块化ASP建立了联系。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to structure and reuse logic programs in first-order answer set programming, this paper introduces parametric modular logic programs, a formalism enabling subprograms with parameters and intensional statements. The method defines modular ASP constructs that can be instantiated and controlled collectively, mirroring clingo’s collective control features. Experimental results show that this approach successfully captures the semantics of complex clingo programs while preserving modularity, offering a declarative alternative to procedural instantiation methods.
为了解决答案集编程中程序结构化与复用的需求，本文提出参数化模块化逻辑程序形式化方法，支持通过参数和内涵语句定义子程序。该方法可捕捉clingo程序中集体控制语义，实现模块化实例化与组合。实验表明，该方法在保持传统ASP表达能力的同时，显著提升了程序结构清晰度与复用性。
AnyMo: Geometry-Aware Setup-Agnostic Modeling of Human Motion in the Wild
Authors: Baiyu Chen, Zechen Li, Wilson Wongso, Lihuan Li, Xiachong Lin, Hao Xue, Benjamin Tag, Flora Salim
First: 2026-05-21T16:52:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:52:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
As wearable and mobile devices become increasingly embedded in daily life, they offer a practical way to continuously sense human motion in the wild. But inertial signals are highly dependent on the sensing setup, including body location, mounting position, sensor orientation, device hardware, and sampling protocol. This setup dependence makes it difficult to learn motion representations that transfer across devices and datasets, and limits the broader use of wearable IMUs beyond closed-set recognition. We introduce AnyMo, a geometry-aware framework for setup-agnostic human motion modeling. AnyMo uses physics-grounded IMU simulation over dense body-surface placements to generate diverse and plausible synthetic signals, pre-trains a graph encoder from paired synthetic placement views and masked partial observations, tokenizes multi-position IMU into full-body motion tokens, and aligns these tokens with an LLM for motion-language understanding. We evaluate AnyMo on three complementary tasks: zero-shot activity recognition across 14 unseen downstream datasets, cross-modal retrieval, and wearable IMU motion captioning, where it improves average Accuracy/F1/R@2 by 11.7\%/11.6\%/22.6\% on HAR, increases zero-shot IMU-to-text and text-to-IMU retrieval MRR by 15.9\% and 28.6\%, respectively, and improves zero-shot captioning BERT-F1 by 18.8\%. These results support AnyMo as a generalist model for wearable motion understanding in the wild. Project page: https://baiyuchen.com/project/AnyMo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnyMo：面向野外人体运动的几何感知、设置无关建模框架
随着可穿戴和移动设备日益融入日常生活，它们为在野外持续感知人体运动提供了实用途径。但惯性信号高度依赖于传感设置，包括身体位置、安装位置、传感器朝向、设备硬件及采样协议。这种设置依赖性使得学习可跨设备和数据集迁移的运动表征变得困难，也限制了可穿戴IMU在闭集识别之外的广泛应用。我们提出AnyMo，一种面向野外人体运动的几何感知、设置无关建模框架。AnyMo通过在密集体表位置上进行物理基础的IMU仿真，生成多样且合理的合成信号；从配对的合成安装视角与掩码部分观测中预训练图编码器；将多位置IMU数据分词为全身运动令牌，并与大语言模型对齐以实现运动-语言理解。我们在三个互补任务上评估AnyMo：跨14个未见下游数据集的零样本活动识别、跨模态检索及可穿戴IMU运动描述生成，其中在HAR任务上平均提升Accuracy/F1/R@2分别为11.7%/11.6%/22.6%；零样本IMU到文本及文本到IMU检索的MRR分别提升15.9%和28.6%；零样本描述生成的BERT-F1提升18.8%。这些结果支持AnyMo作为野外可穿戴运动理解的通用模型。项目页面：https://baiyuchen.com/project/AnyMo。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of inconsistent inertial sensor setups hindering cross-device motion modeling, AnyMo introduces a geometry-aware framework that simulates IMU signals across diverse body placements using physics-grounded synthesis. It pre-trains a graph encoder on synthetic data with masked observations, tokenizes multi-position IMU signals into full-body motion tokens, and aligns them with an LLM for motion-language understanding. Evaluated across zero-shot activity recognition, cross-modal retrieval, and motion captioning, AnyMo achieves significant gains: 11.7% accuracy, 11.6% F1, and 22.6% R@2 improvements on HAR, 15.9% and 28.6% MRR boosts in IMU-to-text and text-to-IMU retrieval, and 18.8% BERT-F1 gain in captioning, demonstrating its effectiveness as a generalist motion understanding model in real-world settings.
为解决惯性传感器配置差异导致的跨设备运动建模困难，AnyMo提出一种几何感知框架，通过物理驱动的密集体表IMU仿真生成多样且合理的合成信号。该框架先在合成数据上预训练图编码器，将多位置IMU信号编码为全身运动token，并与大语言模型对齐以实现运动-语言理解。实验表明，其在零样本活动识别中准确率提升11.7%，跨模态检索MRR分别提升15.9%和28.6%，零样本运动描述BERT-F1提升18.8%，验证了其在真实场景下可泛化的人体运动理解能力。
Insights Generator: Systematic Corpus-Level Trace Diagnostics for LLM Agents
Authors: Akshay Manglik, Apaar Shanker, Kaustubh Deshpande, Jason Qin, Yash Maurya, Veronica Chatrath, Vijay S. Kalmath, Levi Lentz, Yuan, Xue
First: 2026-05-20T16:13:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:51:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diagnosing failures in LLM agents remains largely manual. Practitioners inspect a small subset of execution traces, form ad-hoc hypotheses, and iterate. This process misses patterns that only emerge across trace populations and does not scale to production corpora where individual traces span tens of thousands of tokens. We formalize the problem of corpus-level trace diagnostics. Given a corpus of execution traces, the goal is to produce grounded natural-language insights that characterize systematic behavioral patterns across trace groups, each linked to supporting evidence. We present the Insights Generator (IG), a multi-agent system that answers diagnostic questions by proposing and testing hypotheses across the trace corpus to produce an evidence-backed insights report. We evaluate IG across qualitative and objective dimensions, spanning rubric-based report assessment and downstream performance improvements achieved by implementing IG insights. Human experts using IG reports improve scaffold performance by 30.4pp over the unmodified baseline scaffold, and coding agents leveraging IG-derived insights show consistent and stable gains. Across benchmarks, IG's scout-investigator architecture produces findings comparable in detection coverage to competing approaches, while domain experts rated IG reports as leading depth and evidence quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：洞察生成器：面向大语言模型代理的系统性语料级执行轨迹诊断
诊断大语言模型代理的故障目前仍主要依赖人工。从业者仅检查少量执行轨迹，形成临时假设并反复迭代，这一过程无法捕捉仅在轨迹群体中显现的模式，也无法扩展到生产语料中（单条轨迹可达数万token）。我们形式化了语料级轨迹诊断问题：给定一组执行轨迹，目标是生成基于证据的自然语言洞察，刻画各轨迹组中的系统性行为模式。我们提出了洞察生成器（IG），一个多智能体系统，通过在轨迹语料中提出并验证假设，生成证据支持的洞察报告。我们在定性与客观维度评估IG，涵盖基于评分标准的报告评估及实施IG洞察后下游性能提升。使用IG报告的人类专家，其框架性能较未修改基线提升30.4个百分点；利用IG洞察的编码代理则表现出稳定且持续的性能增益。在多个基准测试中，IG的侦察-调查架构在检测覆盖率上与竞品相当，而领域专家评价IG报告在深度与证据质量方面领先。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of manual LLM agent failure diagnosis, which overlooks cross-trace patterns and fails to scale, the paper introduces Insights Generator (IG), a multi-agent system that systematically analyzes large trace corpora to produce evidence-backed natural-language insights. IG proposes and tests hypotheses across trace groups to uncover systematic behavioral patterns, then generates diagnostic reports linked to supporting evidence. Experimental results show that human experts using IG reports improve scaffold performance by 30.4 points over baseline, while coding agents using IG insights achieve consistent gains. IG’s scout-investigator architecture matches or exceeds competing methods in detection coverage and is rated superior by domain experts in report depth and evidence quality.
为解决人工诊断LLM代理失败效率低、难以发现跨轨迹模式且无法扩展到大规模生产数据的问题，本文提出Insights Generator（IG），一个通过多智能体协作系统分析大规模执行轨迹、生成证据支持的自然语言洞察的框架。IG在轨迹群体间提出并验证假设，识别系统性行为模式，并生成关联证据的报告。评估显示，人类专家使用IG报告可使基础框架性能提升30.4个百分点，基于IG洞察的编码代理表现稳定提升；IG的侦察-调查架构在检测覆盖率上不逊于竞品，且专家评价其洞察深度与证据质量领先。
AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments
Authors: Sid-ali Temkit
First: 2026-05-21T16:51:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:51:04+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 14 figures, 6 tables. Single author. Code, data (75,898 deduplicated API responses), and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/chutapp/amel
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations. Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17). Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AMEL：累积消息效应对大语言模型判断的影响
大型语言模型常被用作自动化评估工具，用于审查代码、内容审核或评分输出，通常有大量项目通过同一对话。我们探讨先前对话历史的极性是否会影响后续判断，这一效应称为累积消息效应对大语言模型判断的影响（AMEL）。通过对4家供应商（OpenAI、Anthropic、Google及四个开源模型）的11个模型进行75,898次API调用，我们呈现了相同测试项目在孤立或跟随充满主要正面或负面评价的历史记录下的表现。模型倾向于向对话中占主导的极性偏移（d = -0.17，p < 10^-46）。该效应主要集中在模型基线时真正不确定的项目上（高熵项目d = -0.34，与基线确定性项目d = -0.15相比）。偏倚不随上下文长度增加而增长：5轮与50轮前序对话产生相同偏移（Spearman |r| < 0.01；OLS斜率p = 0.80）。且存在负面偏向不对称性：每项配对比较中，负面历史诱导的偏倚是正面历史的1.62倍（t = 13.46，p < 10^-39，n = 2,481）。扩展规模有助于但无法完全解决该问题（Anthropic：Haiku -0.22至Opus -0.17；OpenAI：Nano -0.34至GPT-5.2 -0.17）。三项后续研究缩小了机制范围。标记概率分布连续变化，而非阈值触发。负面偏向不对称性同时包含标记级和语义成分，尽管在当前样本量下归因平衡仍属探索性。位置无关紧要：在50轮历史中任意位置的五轮偏倚对话产生相同偏移。评估流程中最简单的解决方案是每项独立上下文；若必须批处理，平衡历史有助于缓解问题。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether prior conversation history biases LLM judgments, termed the accumulated message effect (AMEL). Using 75,898 API calls across 11 models from four providers, identical test items were presented either in isolation or after histories saturated with positive or negative evaluations. Results show models consistently shift toward the prevailing polarity in history (effect size d = -0.17, p < 10^-46), especially on uncertain items (d = -0.34), with no increase in bias with longer context. Negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive ones, and scaling models does not eliminate the effect. Mechanistic follow-ups reveal continuous token probability shifts, a negativity asymmetry with both token and semantic components, and position independence. The simplest mitigation is resetting context per item or balancing history when batching.
本研究探讨先前对话历史是否会影响大语言模型的判断，这一现象被称为‘累积消息效应’（AMEL）。通过75,898次API调用，覆盖11个来自四大供应商的模型，相同测试项在孤立呈现或跟随大量正面或负面评价历史后进行测试。结果表明，模型会持续向对话中主导极性倾斜（效应量d = -0.17，p < 10^-46），在基线不确定性高的项目上偏差更显著（d = -0.34），而非确定性项目（d = -0.15）。偏差不随上下文长度增加（5轮与50轮历史效果相似），且存在不对称性：负面历史诱导的偏差是正面历史的1.62倍。模型规模提升虽能缓解但无法根除该效应。后续分析表明，词元概率分布呈连续变化而非阈值切换，负面偏差的不对称性兼具词元与语义成分，且历史中位置不影响结果。最有效的缓解方法是在每个项目后重置上下文，或在必须批处理时平衡历史内容。
Pelican-Unify 1.0: A Unified Embodied Intelligence Model for Understanding, Reasoning, Imagination and Action
Authors: Yi Zhang, Yinda Chen, Che Liu, Zeyuan Ding, Jin Xu, Shilong Zou, Junwei Liao, Jiayu Hu, Xiancong Ren, Xiaopeng Zhang, Yechi Liu, Haoyuan Shi, Zecong Tang, Haosong Sun, Renwen Cui, Kuishu Wu, Wenhai Liu, Yang Xu, Yingji Zhang, Yidong Wang, Senkang Hu, Jinpeng Lu, Nga Teng Chan, Yechen Wu, Zeting Liu, Xianzhou Hou, Yong Dai, Jian Tang, Xiaozhu Ju
First: 2026-05-14T17:50:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:50:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Pelican-Unify 1.0, the first embodied foundation model trained according to the principle of unification. Pelican-Unify 1.0 uses a single VLM as a unified understanding module, mapping scenes, instructions, visual contexts, and action histories into a shared semantic space. The same VLM also serves as a unified reasoning module, autoregressively producing task-, action-, and future-oriented chains of thought in a single forward pass and projecting the final hidden state into a dense latent variable. A Unified Future Generator (UFG) then conditions on this latent variable and jointly generates future videos and future actions through two modality-specific output heads within the same denoising process. The language, video, and action losses are all backpropagated into the shared representation, enabling the model to jointly optimize understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action during training, rather than training three isolated expert systems. Experiments demonstrate that unification does not imply compromise. With a single checkpoint, Pelican-Unify 1.0 achieves strong performance across all three capabilities: 64.7 on eight VLM benchmarks, the best among comparable-scale models; 66.03 on WorldArena, ranking first; and 93.5 on RoboTwin, the second-best average among compared action methods. These results show that the unified paradigm succeeds in preserving specialist strength while bringing understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action into one model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：鹈鹕统一1.0：面向理解、推理、想象与行动的统一具身智能模型
我们提出了鹈鹕统一1.0，这是首个依据统一原则训练的具身基础模型。鹈鹕统一1.0使用单一视觉语言模型（VLM）作为统一理解模块，将场景、指令、视觉上下文和行动历史映射到共享语义空间。同一VLM同时作为统一推理模块，在单次前向传播中自回归生成任务导向、行动导向和未来导向的思维链，并将最终隐藏状态投影为稠密潜在变量。随后，统一未来生成器（UFG）基于该潜在变量，在同一去噪过程中通过两个模态特定输出头联合生成未来视频与未来行动。语言、视频与行动损失均反向传播至共享表示，使模型在训练中联合优化理解、推理、想象与行动，而非训练三个独立的专家系统。 实验表明，统一并不意味着妥协。单个检查点下，鹈鹕统一1.0在三大能力上均取得优异表现：在八个VLM基准测试中得分64.7，优于同类规模模型；在WorldArena中得分66.03，排名第一；在RoboTwin中得分93.5，为对比行动方法中的第二佳平均分。这些结果表明，统一范式成功保留了专家能力，同时将理解、推理、想象与行动整合于单一模型中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training separate models for understanding, reasoning, and action, the authors introduce Pelican-Unify 1.0, a unified embodied intelligence model that uses a single vision-language model (VLM) as a shared semantic and reasoning core. This VLM maps multimodal inputs into a latent space and generates reasoning chains autoregressively, projecting the final state into a dense variable that conditions a Unified Future Generator. The UFG then jointly produces future video and action outputs via modality-specific heads within a single denoising process, with all losses backpropagated into the shared representation. Experiments show that this unified approach achieves state-of-the-art or top-tier performance: 64.7 on VLM benchmarks, 66.03 on WorldArena, and 93.5 on RoboTwin, demonstrating that unification enhances performance without sacrificing specialization.
为解决传统方法中理解、推理、想象与行动需分别训练多个专家系统效率低下的问题，作者提出Pelican-Unify 1.0，该模型通过单一视觉语言模型作为统一语义与推理模块，将场景、指令、视觉上下文和动作历史映射至共享语义空间，并自回归生成任务导向、动作导向和未来导向的思维链，再通过统一未来生成器联合生成未来视频与动作。所有模态损失共同反向传播至共享表示，实现联合优化。实验表明，该统一架构在多个基准上表现优异：VLM基准得分64.7（同类模型最优）、WorldArena得分66.03（排名第一）、RoboTwin得分93.5（排名第二），证明统一范式既保留了专家能力又实现了多能力融合。
Abstraction for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Clarisse Wibault, Alexander Goldie, Antonio Villares, Maike Osborne, Jakob Foerster
First: 2026-05-21T16:50:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:50:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often exhibit significant redundancy due to symmetries and shared structure across state-goal pairs in real-world Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (GCRL). While hierarchical policies have been motivated for horizon reduction via temporal abstraction in offline GCRL, we demonstrate that hierarchy also enables absolute abstraction. By introducing relativised options as well as distinct representations for different levels of the hierarchy, we demonstrate how an agent can reuse experience across similar contexts of the state-space. Based on this framework, we introduce two simple algorithms for learning relativised options and abstracting from the absolute frame of reference. Our experiments show that such inductive biases significantly improve performance in offline GCRL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：离线目标条件强化学习中的抽象方法
马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）在现实世界的目标条件强化学习（GCRL）中常因状态-目标对之间的对称性和共享结构而表现出显著冗余。虽然分层策略已被提出用于通过时间抽象减少时间跨度，我们证明分层结构也可实现绝对抽象。通过引入相对化选项及不同层级的差异化表示，我们展示了智能体如何在状态空间相似上下文中复用经验。基于此框架，我们提出了两种简单算法，用于学习相对化选项并从绝对参考框架中抽象。实验表明，此类归纳偏置显著提升了离线GCRL的性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the redundancy in state-goal pairs within real-world goal-conditioned reinforcement learning, this work proposes using hierarchical policies to enable absolute abstraction rather than just temporal compression. It introduces relativised options and distinct representations at different hierarchy levels to allow agents to reuse experience across similar state contexts. Experimental results demonstrate that these inductive biases lead to significant performance improvements in offline goal-conditioned RL tasks.
为解决现实世界目标条件强化学习中状态-目标对的冗余问题，该研究提出利用分层策略实现绝对抽象而非仅时间压缩。通过引入相对化选项和不同层级的差异化表示，使智能体能在相似状态上下文中复用经验。实验结果表明，这些归纳偏置显著提升了离线目标条件强化学习任务的性能。
SpecBlock: Block-Iterative Speculative Decoding with Dynamic Tree Drafting
Authors: Weijie Shi, Qiang Xu, Fan Deng, Yaguang Wu, Jiarun Liu, Yehong Xu, Hao Chen, Jia Zhu, Jiajie Xu, Xiangjun Huang, Jian Yang, Xiaofang Zhou
First: 2026-05-08T04:59:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:49:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting a tree of candidate continuations and verifying it in one target forward. Existing drafters fall into two camps with opposite weaknesses. Autoregressive drafters such as EAGLE-3 preserve dependence along each draft path but call the drafter once per tree depth, making drafting a non-trivial share of per-iteration latency. Parallel drafters cut drafter calls by predicting multiple future positions in one forward, but each position is predicted without seeing the others, producing paths the verifier rejects. In this paper, we propose SpecBlock, a block-iterative drafter that combines path dependence with cheap drafting. Each drafter forward produces K dependent positions and we call this a block. The draft tree grows through repeated block expansions. Two mechanisms explicitly carry path dependence to keep later draft positions accurate. Within each block, a layer-wise shift carries the previous position's hidden state into every decoder layer. Across blocks, each new block can start from any position of the previous block, inheriting its hidden state to extend the path. To spend verifier budget where acceptance is likely, a co-trained rank head replaces the fixed top-k tree by allocating per-position branching during drafting. To avoid training the drafter on prefixes it never produces at inference, a valid-prefix mask drops the loss at later positions once an earlier one is wrong. Beyond static drafting, a cost-aware bandit at deployment uses free verifier feedback to update the drafter selectively, only when the expected throughput gain exceeds the update cost. Experiments show that SpecBlock improves mean speedup by 8-13% over EAGLE-3 at 44-52% of its drafting cost, and cost-aware adaptation extends this lead to 11-19%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpecBlock：基于动态树草稿的块迭代推测解码
推测解码通过草拟候选延续的树并一次性验证，加速大语言模型推理。现有草稿器分为两类，各有缺陷：自回归草稿器（如EAGLE-3）沿每条草稿路径保持依赖，但每层树深度调用一次草稿器，使草稿占迭代延迟显著部分；并行草稿器通过一次前向预测多个未来位置以减少调用，但各位置独立预测，导致验证器拒绝路径。本文提出SpecBlock，一种结合路径依赖与低成本草稿的块迭代草稿器。每次草稿前向生成K个依赖位置，称为一个块，树通过重复块扩展生长。两种机制显式传递路径依赖以保持后续草稿位置准确：块内层间偏移将前一位置隐藏状态传入所有解码层；块间新块可从上一区块任意位置开始，继承其隐藏状态以延续路径。为在高接受概率处分配验证器预算，联合训练的排序头替代固定top-k树，动态分配草稿时各位置分支。为避免训练草稿器在推理中从未生成的前缀，有效前缀掩码在早期位置错误时屏蔽后续位置损失。除静态草稿外，部署时成本感知多臂老虎机利用免费验证器反馈选择性更新草稿器，仅当预期吞吐量增益超过更新成本时更新。实验表明，SpecBlock在44-52%草稿成本下较EAGLE-3平均加速提升8-13%，成本感知自适应进一步扩大优势至11-19%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-offs between autoregressive and parallel drafters in speculative decoding, this paper introduces SpecBlock, a block-iterative drafter that combines path dependence with low-cost drafting. It generates K dependent positions per forward pass (a 'block'), expanding the draft tree iteratively while preserving context via layer-wise hidden state shifting within blocks and inherited states across blocks. A co-trained rank head dynamically allocates branching during drafting to prioritize likely acceptances, and a valid-prefix mask prevents training on invalid prefixes. At deployment, a cost-aware bandit updates the drafter only when expected throughput gain outweighs update cost. Experiments show SpecBlock achieves 8-13% higher mean speedup than EAGLE-3 at 44-52% of its drafting cost, with adaptive updates extending the lead to 11-19%.
为解决现有推测解码器在路径依赖性与效率间的权衡问题，SpecBlock提出一种块迭代式推测解码方法，每轮前向生成K个依赖位置（一个块），通过重复块扩展构建推测树，并在块内通过层间隐藏状态偏移、块间状态继承保持路径一致性。采用联合训练的排序头动态分配推测分支，优先生成高接受概率路径；有效前缀掩码避免在训练中学习推理时不会出现的前缀。部署时引入成本感知多臂老虎机，仅在预期吞吐提升超过更新成本时更新解码器。实验表明，SpecBlock在仅消耗EAGLE-3 44-52%推测开销下，平均加速比提升8-13%，成本感知自适应进一步扩大至11-19%。
TriSweep: A Four-Drone Swarm Framework for Electromagnetic Side-Channel Analysis
Authors: Eric Yocam, Varghese Vaidyan
First: 2026-05-21T16:47:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:47:44+00:00
Comments: Simulation framework + systems design for a four-drone swarm performing standoff electromagnetic side-channel analysis. No hardware fabricated yet
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel analysis traditionally assumes a stationary, close-proximity probe - a threat model that underestimates aerial adversaries. TriSweep is a simulation framework that designs and evaluates a four-drone swarm architecture for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25-1.5 m. Three spatially specialized collector drones - Anchor (full-spectrum), Mask Probe (mask-register loading leakage), and Cipher Probe (masked SubBytes output leakage) - feed a stationary Accumulator drone that performs coherent combining (+4.8 dB SNR gain) and second-order mask cancellation via a centered product of the two spatially separated leakage streams. Evaluated against three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets (ATmega8515 masked AES-128 and 50/100-sample desynchronized variants), the framework achieves a simulated key rank of 18 +/- 1.7 (five-seed) at 0.25 m on the primary masked dataset. Profiling-trace cross-correlation alignment reduces single-drone rank from 89 to 21 on the 100-sample-jitter variant, demonstrating compensation for drone hover vibration. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator converges to a loss of 0.454 (vs. random baseline 5.545) and improves rank on desynchronized datasets. No physical hardware has been fabricated; prototype construction is the planned next step.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TriSweep：一种用于电磁侧信道分析的四无人机编队框架
传统电磁（EM）侧信道分析假设存在静止且近距离的探测器，这一威胁模型低估了空中对手的能力。TriSweep 是一个仿真框架，设计并评估了一种四无人机编队架构，用于在0.25-1.5米远的自主远距离电磁侧信道分析嵌入式微控制器。三架空间特化的采集无人机——锚点无人机（全频谱）、掩码探针无人机（掩码寄存器加载泄漏）和密码探针无人机（掩码SubBytes输出泄漏）——将数据传输给静止的累加器无人机，后者通过相干叠加（+4.8 dB SNR增益）和两个空间分离泄漏流的中心积实现二阶掩码消除。在三个真实的ANSSI ASCAD数据集（ATmega8515掩码AES-128及50/100样本不同步变体）上评估，该框架在0.25米距离下对主掩码数据集的模拟密钥排名为18±1.7（五种子）。通过配置文件迹线互相关对齐，单无人机排名从89降至21（100样本抖动变体），展示了对无人机悬停振动的补偿能力。累加器中的双通道CNN收敛至0.454损失（对比随机基线5.545），并在不同步数据集上提升排名。尚未制造物理硬件；下一步计划是原型构建。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inadequacy of traditional EM side-channel analysis assumptions for aerial adversaries, TriSweep introduces a four-drone swarm framework for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers. The system employs three specialized drones—Anchor, Mask Probe, and Cipher Probe—to capture distinct leakage patterns, which are combined coherently by a stationary Accumulator drone to achieve a 4.8 dB SNR gain and second-order mask cancellation. Evaluated on three ANSSI ASCAD datasets, the framework achieved a simulated key rank of 18 ± 1.7 on the primary masked dataset at 0.25 m, with cross-correlation alignment reducing rank from 89 to 21 on a jittered variant, demonstrating vibration compensation. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator further improved performance, converging to a loss of 0.454 against a random baseline of 5.545 and enhancing rank on desynchronized datasets.
为应对传统静态电磁侧信道分析对空中攻击者估计不足的问题，TriSweep提出了一种四无人机编队框架，用于在0.25–1.5米距离外自主执行嵌入式微控制器的电磁侧信道分析。系统由三类专用无人机——锚点机、掩码探针机和密钥探针机——分别采集不同空间位置的泄漏信号，由静止的累加器无人机进行相干叠加（+4.8 dB信噪比增益）和二阶掩码抵消。在三个真实ANSSI ASCAD数据集上测试，框架在0.25米处对主掩码数据集实现18±1.7的模拟密钥排名，通过轨迹对齐将单机排名从89降至21，有效补偿无人机悬停振动。累加器中的双通道CNN收敛至0.454损失（随机基线为5.545），在非同步数据集上进一步提升密钥恢复性能。
Beyond the Org Chart: AI and the Transformation of Invisible Work
Authors: Stephanie Rosenthal, Shamsi Iqbal
First: 2026-05-21T16:46:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:46:44+00:00
Comments: 10 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An increasing number of news and research articles report that AI adoption is allowing professionals to blur and extend the boundaries of their corporate roles. With the goal of understanding how work processes might be changing in an AI-forward company, we interviewed 24 product-focused individuals at a large technology firm about how AI has impacted their own work, their work within their product team, and their professional interactions. Our conversations suggest that AI is not only changing formal role responsibilities and collaborations between those roles, but also changing informal cultural practices like professional mentoring that are key to helping professionals settle in their positions, stay engaged with their work, and grow their careers. Some of these changes are positive, such as smoother collaboration between peers, but other changes are more nuanced and put the typical career growth opportunities, like receiving feedback from professional networks and promoting leadership and mentorship, at risk. We propose steps that AI companies can take to make the invisible work more visible. Additionally, we propose efforts that individuals and leaders can take to support their colleagues through AI transformation while preserving healthy company cultures that support diverse thinking, collaboration, and informal interactions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越组织架构：人工智能与隐形工作的变革
越来越多的新闻和研究文章指出，人工智能的采用正使专业人士模糊并扩展其在公司中的角色边界。为理解在人工智能驱动的公司中工作流程可能如何变化，我们采访了某大型科技公司24位以产品为导向的员工，了解人工智能如何影响他们的个人工作、产品团队内的工作以及专业互动。我们的对话表明，人工智能不仅改变了正式的角色职责和角色间的协作，也改变了诸如专业指导等非正式文化实践——这些实践对帮助专业人士适应岗位、保持工作投入度和职业成长至关重要。部分变化是积极的，如同事间协作更顺畅；但其他变化更为复杂，可能削弱传统职业发展机会，例如从专业网络获得反馈、推动领导力与指导能力等。我们提出人工智能公司可采取的措施，以使隐形工作更显性化；同时，也提出个人和领导者可采取的行动，以在人工智能转型中支持同事，同时维护支持多元思维、协作与非正式互动的健康公司文化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing influence of AI on workplace dynamics, this study investigates how AI adoption reshapes both formal and informal work practices through interviews with 24 product-focused employees at a major tech firm. The researchers found that AI not only alters defined job roles and team collaborations but also disrupts culturally embedded practices like mentoring and peer feedback, which are vital for professional development and engagement. While some changes, such as improved peer collaboration, are beneficial, others—like diminished opportunities for mentorship and leadership recognition—pose risks to career growth and organizational culture. The paper concludes by proposing organizational strategies to make invisible work more visible and individual actions to sustain healthy, inclusive, and collaborative environments during AI-driven transformation.
鉴于越来越多的研究表明AI正在重塑超越正式职位描述的职业角色，本研究对一家大型科技公司中24名产品导向员工进行了访谈，探讨AI如何影响其工作流程、团队协作及非正式文化实践（如指导与反馈）。研究发现，AI不仅改变了正式职责与角色间协作，也冲击了职业发展与工作投入所依赖的非正式文化机制，如同行反馈与指导机会。虽然部分变化（如提升同事协作）具有积极意义，但其他变化可能削弱传统职业成长路径。作者建议企业采取措施使隐形工作更显性化，并提出个人与领导者可采取行动，在AI转型中维持健康、多元、协作的组织文化。
Tokenization with Split Trees
Authors: Craig W. Schmidt, Michael Krumdick, Adam Wiemerslage, Seth Ebner, Varshini Reddy, Yuval Pinter, Chris Tanner
First: 2026-05-21T16:46:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:46:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), a subword tokenization method that directly optimizes compression under a new recursive inference procedure. ToaST greedily splits each pretoken into a full binary tree using precomputed byte n-gram counts, independent of any vocabulary. Given a vocabulary, inference recursively descends each split tree and emits the first in-vocabulary node reached on each path. Vocabulary selection is formulated as an Integer Program (IP) that minimizes the total token count over all split trees under this inference procedure. The Linear Programming (LP) relaxation is near-integral in practice, yielding provably near-optimal vocabularies, with training time empirically scaling quadratically in the number of split trees. On English text, ToaST reduces token counts by more than 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at vocabulary sizes of 40,960 and above, reducing the number of inference tokens for models using this tokenizer, thus extending the effective context length. ToaST also uses common single-byte tokens less frequently than these baselines, leading to a substantial improvement in Renyi efficiency. In experiments training 1.5B parameter language models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%--7.6%, with significance for two of three, and scoring best on 13 of 22 individual tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于分裂树的分词方法
我们提出了基于分裂树的分词方法（ToaST），这是一种直接在新的递归推理过程中优化压缩的子词分词方法。ToaST 利用预计算的字节 n-gram 统计，贪婪地将每个预分词拆分为完整的二叉树，且不依赖任何词汇表。给定一个词汇表，推理过程会递归遍历每个分裂树，并在每条路径上输出第一个命中词汇表的节点。词汇表选择被建模为一个整数规划（IP）问题，旨在最小化在该推理过程下所有分裂树的总词数。其线性规划（LP）松弛在实践中接近整数解，从而得到可证明的近最优词汇表，且训练时间经验上随分裂树数量的平方增长。在英文文本上，当词汇表大小达到 40,960 及以上时，ToaST 相比 BPE、WordPiece 和 UnigramLM 分词数减少超过 11%，从而减少模型使用该分词器时的推理词数，延长有效上下文长度。此外，ToaST 比这些基线方法更少使用常见单字节词元，显著提升了 Renyi 效率。在训练 1.5B 参数语言模型的实验中，ToaST 达到最高的 CORE 分数，比基线高出 2.6% 至 7.6%，其中三项中有两项具有统计显著性，并在 22 项任务中的 13 项上表现最佳。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more efficient subword tokenization that directly optimizes compression, the authors propose Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), which constructs a full binary split tree for each pretoken using precomputed byte n-gram counts, independent of vocabulary. Inference recursively traverses each tree, emitting the first in-vocabulary node encountered on each path, while vocabulary selection is formulated as an Integer Program minimizing total token count under this procedure. The Linear Programming relaxation is near-integral, enabling near-optimal vocabularies with quadratic training scaling. Experiments show ToaST reduces token counts by over 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at 40,960+ vocabulary sizes, extends effective context length, reduces single-byte token usage, and improves Renyi efficiency. When training 1.5B parameter models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%–7.6% on 13 of 22 tasks.
为提升子词分词的压缩效率，ToaST提出一种基于拆分树的分词方法，通过预计算字节n-gram统计，为每个预词构建独立于词汇表的完全二叉树。推理时递归遍历每棵树，输出路径上首个命中词汇表的节点。词汇表选择建模为整数规划，最小化所有树的总词数，其线性规划松弛在实践中接近整数解，可获得近最优词汇表。实验表明，当词汇表规模达40,960以上时，ToaST比BPE、WordPiece和UnigramLM减少11%以上的词数，延长有效上下文长度，减少单字节词使用，提升Renyi效率。在训练1.5B参数模型时，ToaST获得最高CORE得分，较基线提升2.6%–7.6%，在22项任务中13项表现最佳。
Uncertainty-Aware Predictive Safety Filters for Probabilistic Neural Network Dynamics
Authors: Bernd Frauenknecht, Lukas Kesper, Daniel Mayfrank, Henrik Hose, Sebastian Trimpe
First: 2026-04-29T16:01:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:45:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predictive safety filters (PSFs) leverage model predictive control to enforce constraint satisfaction during deep reinforcement learning (RL) exploration, yet their reliance on first-principles models or Gaussian processes limits scalability and broader applicability. Meanwhile, model-based RL (MBRL) methods routinely employ probabilistic ensemble (PE) neural networks to capture complex, high-dimensional dynamics from data with minimal prior knowledge. However, existing attempts to integrate PEs into PSFs lack rigorous uncertainty quantification. We introduce the Uncertainty-Aware Predictive Safety Filter (UPSi), a PSF that provides rigorous safety predictions using PE dynamics models by formulating future outcomes as reachable sets. UPSi introduces an explicit certainty constraint that prevents model exploitation and integrates seamlessly into common MBRL frameworks. We evaluate UPSi within Dyna-style MBRL on standard safe RL benchmarks and report substantial improvements in exploration safety over prior neural network PSFs while maintaining performance on par with standard MBRL. UPSi bridges the gap between the scalability and generality of modern MBRL and the safety guarantees of predictive safety filters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于概率神经网络动力学的不确定性感知预测安全滤波器
预测安全滤波器（PSF）利用模型预测控制在深度强化学习探索过程中确保约束满足，但其依赖于物理原理模型或高斯过程，限制了可扩展性和广泛应用。同时，基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）方法通常采用概率集成（PE）神经网络，以在数据中捕捉复杂高维动力学并最小化先验知识。然而，现有将PE集成到PSF的尝试缺乏严谨的不确定性量化。我们提出不确定性感知预测安全滤波器（UPSi），通过将未来结果建模为可达集，利用PE动力学模型提供严谨的安全预测。UPSi引入显式置信度约束，防止模型被过度利用，并无缝集成到常见MBRL框架中。我们在Dyna式MBRL框架下评估UPSi，于标准安全强化学习基准上，显著优于现有神经网络PSF在探索安全性方面的表现，同时保持与标准MBRL相当的性能。UPSi弥合了现代MBRL的可扩展性与通用性与预测安全滤波器的安全保障之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and broadly applicable safety mechanisms in deep reinforcement learning, this work proposes UPSi, an uncertainty-aware predictive safety filter that integrates probabilistic ensemble neural networks into model predictive control frameworks. UPSi formulates future system states as reachable sets and enforces explicit certainty constraints to prevent unsafe model exploitation, enabling seamless compatibility with common model-based RL pipelines. Experimental results on standard safe RL benchmarks demonstrate that UPSi significantly improves exploration safety compared to prior neural network-based PSFs while preserving performance levels comparable to standard MBRL methods.
为提升深度强化学习中安全探索的可扩展性和通用性，本文提出UPSi，一种结合概率神经网络与严格不确定性量化预测安全滤波器。UPSi将未来系统状态建模为可达集，并引入显式置信约束以防止模型误用，兼容主流模型基强化学习框架。实验表明，UPSi在标准安全强化学习基准上显著提升探索安全性，同时保持与标准模型基方法相当的性能水平。
Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals
Authors: Shuo Yang, Jinda Lu, Chiyu Ma, Kexin Huang, Haoming Meng, Qihui Zhang, Yuyang Liu, Bolin Ding, Guoyin Wang, Li Yuan, Jingren Zhou
First: 2026-05-21T16:45:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:45:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：裁剪瓶颈：通过近边界信号的随机恢复稳定RLVR
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为扩展大语言模型推理能力的核心范式，但其优化常面临训练不稳定和收敛不佳的问题。通过对基于裁剪的GRPO类目标进行系统性剖析，我们发现硬裁剪引发的刚性裁剪决策是所研究RLVR设置中的关键实际瓶颈。具体而言，我们的分析表明，具有信息量的信号可能位于略高于裁剪阈值的近边界区域，而标准硬裁剪规则会丢弃这些信号。值得注意的是，一旦识别出该瓶颈，即使在边界处进行简单的随机扰动，也能恢复显著的性能增益。基于此发现，我们提出近边界随机救援（NSR），这是一种最小化、即插即用的修改方法，通过随机保留略超出边界的标记以恢复丢失的信号。虽然NSR通过随机采样可被解释为在期望意义上诱导隐式梯度衰减，但我们的消融实验表明，其随机、边界局部救援机制始终比确定性梯度衰减更有效。NSR在7B至30B不同规模模型及稠密与MoE架构上经大量实验验证，作为即插即用方案，显著提升了训练稳定性，并在DAPO和GSPO等强基线方法之上持续带来性能增益。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the training instability and suboptimal convergence in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), this work identifies hard clipping as a key bottleneck that discards informative signals near the clipping boundary. The authors propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. Experimental results across 7B to 30B models and both dense and MoE architectures show NSR significantly improves training stability and consistently outperforms strong baselines like DAPO and GSPO, with its stochastic boundary-local mechanism proving more effective than deterministic gradient decay.
为解决RLVR训练中的不稳定与收敛不佳问题，本文发现硬裁剪会丢弃边界附近有价值的信息，提出近边界随机救援（NSR）方法，通过随机保留略超阈值的token恢复信号。在7B至30B规模及密集/稀疏架构模型上，NSR显著提升训练稳定性，并持续优于DAPO和GSPO等基线方法。
Shallow ReLU$^s$ Networks in $L^p$-Type and Sobolev Spaces: Approximation and Path-Norm Controlled Generalization
Authors: Weizhao Li, Fanghui Liu, Lei Shi
First: 2026-05-18T14:27:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:40:00+00:00
Comments: 42 pages, 1 figure. Authors are listed in alphabetical order and contributed equally
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study approximation by shallow ReLU$^s$ networks, $σ_s(t)=\max{0,t}^s$, and the generalization behavior of such networks under $\ell_1$ path-norm control. For the $L^p$-type integral spaces $\widetilde{\mathcal{F}}_{p,τ_d,s}$, $1\le p\le2$, we establish approximation bounds for shallow networks using spherical harmonic analysis. In particular, when the parameter measure is the uniform measure $τ_d$ and $p<p^*=(2d+2)/(d+3)$, we obtain the rate $O(m^{-1/2-d(2-p)/(2d(2-p)+2p(2s+d+1))}\log^{3/2}m)$, which improves the corresponding random-feature rate. We also derive approximation rates for Sobolev spaces $W^{α,p}$ in the range $1\le p<2$ by embedding them into spectral Barron spaces. Finally, for nonparametric regression with sub-Gaussian noise, we prove minimax-optimal generalization bounds for path-norm-regularized shallow ReLU$^s$ networks over Barron and Sobolev spaces, with matching lower bounds up to logarithmic factors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：浅层ReLU^s网络在L^p型与Sobolev空间中的逼近与路径范数控制的泛化行为
我们研究了浅层ReLU^s网络（σ_s(t)=max{0,t}^s）在L^p型积分空间~F_{p,τ_d,s}（1≤p≤2）中的逼近能力，以及在ℓ_1路径范数控制下的泛化行为。利用球面调和分析，我们建立了浅层网络的逼近界。特别地，当参数测度为均匀测度τ_d且p<p^*=(2d+2)/(d+3)时，我们得到速率O(m^{-1/2-d(2-p)/(2d(2-p)+2p(2s+d+1))}log^{3/2}m)，优于相应的随机特征速率。此外，通过将Sobolev空间W^{α,p}（1≤p<2）嵌入谱Barron空间，我们推导了其逼近速率。最后，对于具有次高斯噪声的非参数回归问题，我们证明了路径范数正则化浅层ReLU^s网络在Barron和Sobolev空间上的最小最大最优泛化界，并给出了至多对数因子匹配的下界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding the approximation and generalization capabilities of shallow ReLU^s networks under path-norm regularization, the paper analyzes their performance in L^p-type and Sobolev spaces. Using spherical harmonic analysis, it derives approximation bounds for shallow networks in L^p spaces, achieving improved rates over random-feature methods when p < p* and uniform measure is used. It further embeds Sobolev spaces into spectral Barron spaces to obtain approximation rates for 1 ≤ p < 2. For nonparametric regression with sub-Gaussian noise, it proves minimax-optimal generalization bounds for path-norm-regularized networks in Barron and Sobolev spaces, with lower bounds matching up to logarithmic factors.
为理解浅层ReLU^s网络在路径范数约束下的逼近与泛化能力，本文研究其在L^p型和Sobolev空间中的表现。通过球面调和分析，推导出在均匀测度下L^p空间的逼近界，当p < p^*时，得到O(m^{-1/2-d(2-p)/(2d(2-p)+2p(2s+d+1))} log^{3/2} m)的收敛速率，优于随机特征方法。对Sobolev空间，通过嵌入谱Barron空间获得逼近率。在次高斯噪声下的非参数回归中，证明了路径范数正则化浅层网络在Barron与Sobolev空间中的最小最大最优泛化界，且下界在对数因子内匹配。
Cross-Domain Human Action Recognition from Multiview Motion and Textual Descriptions
Authors: Yannick Porto, Renato Martins, Thomas Chalumeau, Cedric Demonceaux
First: 2026-05-21T16:39:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:39:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICPR 2026. Code and trained models available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/OrientationAware-HAR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robustness to domain changes is a key capability for effective deployment of human action recognition systems in real-world scenarios, where action categories at inference can present important domain shifts or even unseen actions from training. In this context, improving the recognition capabilities of Zero-Shot Action Recognition models (ZSAR), without requiring strong annotation efforts, remains a central challenge. Most ZSAR approaches assume that actions are observed under geometric conditions similar to those seen during training. In practice, variations in human body orientation and camera viewpoint add a significant domain gap in ZSAR, substantially limiting generalization to novel action-motion combinations. In this context, this paper presents a novel orientation-aware action recognition approach with improved cross-domain capabilities. Our approach combines motion cues of multiple camera viewpoints and text descriptions of human actions in the training phase. We present a new orientation-aware motion encoding network to learn different motion features, and adapt a specific orientation-aware text prompt to match the corresponding features at inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently improves ZSAR performance across different recognition benchmarks, outperforming recent state-of-the-art zero-shot approaches on NTU-RGB+D, BABEL, NW-UCLA, and on two surveillance datasets. In addition, the learned representations exhibit strong transfer learning capabilities, yielding competitive performance on both cross-domain and same-domain recognition of seen actions. Code and trained models are available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/OrientationAware-HAR

Improving Viewpoint-Invariance and Temporal Consistency for Action Detection
Authors: Yannick Porto, Renato Martins, Thomas Chalumeau, Cedric Demonceaux
First: 2026-05-21T16:38:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:38:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICIP 2026. Code and trained models are available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/HydraView-TAD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Viewpoint change invariance and action temporal consistency are critical aspects for the effective deployment of human action detection of untrimmed videos. Existing appearance-based video detection methods often struggle with limited viewpoint diversity during training, while motion-based detection approaches frequently fail to model fine-grained temporal relationships across consecutive motion windows. This paper introduces a novel two-stage action detection approach designed to improve both view-invariance and global temporal coherence properties. In the first stage, we extract motion features from augmented virtual viewpoints, solely used at training. Then, the second stage introduces a new view-invariant, multi-scale temporal encoder based on selective state-space sequence modelling to aggregate information across viewpoints and time scales. Experiments on PKU-MMD and BABEL benchmarks demonstrate that this approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in all considered splits. Code and trained models are available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/HydraView-TAD
