Experiments on PKU-MMD and BABEL benchmarks show consistent performance gains over existing methods across all evaluation splits.
该论文旨在解决现有动作检测方法在处理视角变化和建模细粒度时序一致性方面的不足。其提出两阶段方法：第一阶段在训练时从增强的虚拟视角提取运动特征以提升视角不变性；第二阶段采用基于选择性状态空间建模的视角不变多尺度时序编码器，以捕捉跨视角和多尺度的时间依赖关系。在PKU-MMD和BABEL数据集上的实验表明，该方法在所有评估划分上均显著优于当前最先进方法。
Scout-Assisted Planning for Heterogeneous Robot Teams under Partially Known Environments
Authors: Hoang-Dung Bui, Abhish Khanal, Raihan Islam Arnob, Gregory J. Stein
First: 2026-05-21T16:36:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:36:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous robot teams navigating partially known environments face costly backtracking when ground robots encounter blocked roads that are only revealed upon physical traversal. We address this with Scout-Assisted Planning, a heterogeneous planning framework in which scouting Unmanned Aerial Vehicles proactively gather environmental information to improve Unmanned Ground Vehicle navigation. To focus scouting on the most consequential edges, we propose Information Gain-based Action Pruning, which scores candidate scouting actions by their expected impact on ground robot behavior. Since exact Information Gain-based Action Pruning computation is prohibitively expensive, we develop a Graph Neural Network based model that predicts information gain values directly from graph structure and belief state, reducing planning time to real-time levels without sacrificing solution quality. Experiments across three environment types show that SAP with Information Gain Action Pruning reduces ground robot travel cost by 31.9--37.7% over the Canadian Traveler Problem baseline, and outperforms proximity-based scouting guidance by an additional 8--14%, confirming that principled information-gain-guided scouting is both more effective and computationally feasible for real-world deployment
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于侦察机辅助的异构机器人团队在部分已知环境中的规划
自主机器人团队在部分已知环境中导航时，地面机器人遇到仅在物理穿越后才显现的阻塞道路，会导致昂贵的回溯。我们提出侦察机辅助规划（Scout-Assisted Planning），这是一种异构规划框架，其中侦察无人机主动收集环境信息以提升地面机器人导航效果。为聚焦于最具影响的边，我们提出基于信息增益的动作剪枝方法，通过评估候选侦察动作对地面机器人行为的预期影响进行评分。由于基于信息增益的动作剪枝计算成本过高，我们开发了一种基于图神经网络的模型，直接从图结构和信念状态预测信息增益值，将规划时间降至实时水平，且不牺牲解决方案质量。在三种环境类型中的实验表明，结合信息增益动作剪枝的SAP相比加拿大旅行者问题基线可降低地面机器人行驶成本31.9%至37.7%，并比基于邻近性的侦察引导方法进一步提升8%至14%，证实了基于信息增益的侦察策略在实际部署中既更有效又具备计算可行性。
Summary / 总结
To reduce costly backtracking in partially known environments, this work introduces Scout-Assisted Planning, a framework using aerial scouts to proactively gather environmental data for ground robots. It employs Information Gain-based Action Pruning to prioritize scouting actions based on their expected impact on ground navigation, and uses a Graph Neural Network to approximate these gains efficiently, enabling real-time planning without quality loss. Experiments across three environments show a 31.9–37.7% reduction in ground robot travel cost compared to the Canadian Traveler Problem baseline, and an additional 8–14% improvement over proximity-based scouting.
为降低部分已知环境中地面机器人因路径受阻而反复回溯的成本，该研究提出Scout-Assisted Planning框架，利用空中侦察无人机主动采集环境信息。通过基于信息增益的动作剪枝策略优先选择对地面机器人导航影响最大的侦察任务，并采用图神经网络高效近似信息增益值，实现实时规划。在三种环境中的实验表明，该方法相比加拿大旅行者问题基线可降低地面机器人行驶成本31.9–37.7%，相比基于距离的侦察策略进一步提升8–14%。
Posterior Collapse as Automatic Spectral Pruning
Authors: Johannes Hirn
First: 2026-05-21T16:36:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:36:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful. We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first. In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：后验坍缩作为自动谱剪枝
我们证明了β-VAE中的后验坍缩实现了自动谱剪枝。若某一潜在模式对重建的贡献低于由β设定的阈值，则该模式会坍缩。不同β值下的平衡解揭示了潜在模式从最不有用到最有利的逐步坍缩过程。 我们通过朗道稳定性分析将此推导为损失函数的后果。我们定义了一个与潜在变量重标度无关的序参量，用于排序活跃的潜在模式，其坍缩阈值可识别应优先检查的有效变量。 在线性高斯情况下，坍缩谱、效用谱和标准化PCA谱三者一致，每个坍缩遵循平均场定律。我们在WorldClim数据集上验证了这些预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding posterior collapse in β-VAEs as a form of automatic spectral pruning, the authors show that latent modes collapse when their reconstruction contribution falls below a β-dependent threshold, revealing a cascade of decoupling from least to most useful modes. Using a Landau stability analysis of the loss function, they derive a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active modes and identifies collapse thresholds for prioritizing effective variables. In the linear Gaussian setting, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum align, and each collapse follows a mean-field law, validated experimentally on the WorldClim dataset.
为理解β-VAE中后验坍缩作为自动谱剪枝现象，作者证明当潜在模式对重建的贡献低于β设定的阈值时，该模式即发生坍缩，从而揭示从最不有用到最有效模式逐步解耦的级联过程。通过损失函数的朗道稳定性分析，他们推导出一个与潜在变量缩放无关的序参量，用于排序活跃模式并识别应优先检查的有效变量。在线性高斯情形下，坍缩谱、效用谱与归一化PCA谱一致，且每个坍缩遵循平均场定律，实验结果在WorldClim数据集上得到验证。
U-CECE: A Universal Multi-Resolution Framework for Conceptual Counterfactual Explanations
Authors: Angeliki Dimitriou, Nikolaos Chaidos, Maria Lymperaiou, Giorgos Filandrianos, Giorgos Stamou
First: 2026-04-09T14:30:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:35:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI models grow more complex, explainability is essential for building trust, yet concept-based counterfactual methods still face a trade-off between expressivity and efficiency. Representing underlying concepts as atomic sets is fast but misses relational context, whereas full graph representations are more faithful but require solving the NP-hard Graph Edit Distance (GED) problem. We propose U-CECE, a unified, model-agnostic multi-resolution framework for conceptual counterfactual explanations that adapts to data regime and compute budget. U-CECE spans three levels of expressivity: atomic concepts for broad explanations, relational sets-of-sets for simple interactions, and structural graphs for full semantic structure. At the structural level, both a precision-oriented transductive mode based on supervised Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and a scalable inductive mode based on unsupervised graph autoencoders (GAEs) are supported. Experiments on the structurally divergent CUB and Visual Genome datasets characterize the efficiency-expressivity trade-off across levels, while human surveys and LVLM-based evaluation show that the retrieved structural counterfactuals are semantically equivalent to, and often preferred over, exact GED-based ground-truth explanations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：U-CECE：一种通用的多分辨率概念反事实解释框架
随着AI模型日益复杂，可解释性对于建立信任至关重要，但基于概念的反事实方法仍面临表达能力与效率之间的权衡。将底层概念表示为原子集合虽快速但忽略关系上下文，而完整图表示更忠实但需解决NP难的图编辑距离（GED）问题。我们提出U-CECE，一种统一、模型无关的多分辨率概念反事实解释框架，可根据数据环境和计算预算自适应调整。U-CECE涵盖三层表达能力：原子概念用于宽泛解释，关系集合的集合用于简单交互，结构图用于完整语义结构。在结构层面，支持基于监督图神经网络（GNNs）的精度导向的转导模式，以及基于无监督图自编码器（GAEs）的可扩展归纳模式。在结构差异显著的CUB和Visual Genome数据集上的实验刻画了各层级效率与表达能力的权衡，而人类调查和基于LVLM的评估表明，检索到的结构反事实解释在语义上等价于甚至优于基于精确GED的真值解释。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to balance expressivity and efficiency in explaining complex AI models, U-CECE introduces a multi-resolution framework that adapts to data and compute constraints. It offers three levels of conceptual representation: atomic sets for broad explanations, relational sets-of-sets for simple interactions, and structural graphs for full semantic fidelity. At the structural level, it supports both transductive GNN-based and inductive GAE-based methods. Experiments on CUB and Visual Genome demonstrate that U-CECE achieves efficient explanations across resolutions while human evaluations and LVLM assessments confirm that its structural counterfactuals are semantically equivalent and often preferred over exact GED-based ground truths.
为解决AI模型解释中表达能力与计算效率的权衡问题，U-CECE提出了一种多分辨率框架，可根据数据和计算资源自适应调整。该框架提供三个层次的概念表示：原子概念、关系集合和结构图，表达能力逐级增强但计算成本递增。支持基于GNN的转导式和基于GAE的归纳式方法处理结构化解释。在CUB与Visual Genome数据集上的实验表明，U-CECE在不同分辨率下均能生成高质量反事实，且人类评估与LVLM验证显示其结构化解释在语义上等效甚至优于精确的GED基准解释。
A strongly annotated passive acoustic dataset for tropical bird monitoring
Authors: Daniela Ruiz, Juan Sebastián Ulloa, Zhongqi Miao, Nicolás Betancourt, Maria Paula Toro-Gómez, Andrés Hernández, Bruno Demuro, Eliana Barona-Cortés, Angela Mendoza-Henao, Andrés Sierra-Ricaurte, Sebastián Pérez-Peña, Rahul Dodhia, Pablo Arbeláez, Juan M. Lavista Ferres
First: 2026-05-20T00:36:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:34:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Passive acoustic monitoring enables continuous, non-invasive biodiversity assessment across diverse ecosystems. The scale of these datasets has driven the adoption of machine learning, with supervised approaches showing strong performance. However, supervised methods require time-resolved annotated datasets, which remain scarce, especially in complex tropical soundscapes. We present PteroSet, a curated dataset of strongly annotated Neotropical bird vocalizations recorded in Puerto Asis (Putumayo) and Pivijay (Magdalena), Colombia, between 2023 and 2025. The dataset comprises 563 recordings (73.62 h) and 15,372 time-frequency annotations, including 6,702 events identified to the species level across 168 species. We release the annotations in a COCO-inspired JSON schema that unifies audio files, taxonomic categories, and labels for machine learning workflows. Beyond providing annotated data, PteroSet serves as a realistic benchmark that highlights key characteristics of tropical soundscapes, including acoustic co-occurrence and domain shift across recording sites. We provide a deep learning baseline for binary bird detection, demonstrating PteroSet's usability and the challenges it presents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于热带鸟类监测的强标注被动声学数据集
被动声学监测可实现对多样生态系统的持续、非侵入式生物多样性评估。这些数据集的规模推动了机器学习的应用，监督方法表现优异。然而，监督方法需要时间分辨的标注数据，而此类数据在复杂的热带声景中尤为稀缺。我们提出了PteroSet数据集，该数据集包含2023至2025年间在哥伦比亚Puerto Asis（Putumayo）和Pivijay（Magdalena）记录的中美洲鸟类鸣叫，共563段录音（73.62小时），包含15,372个时频标注，其中6,702个事件可识别至物种级别，涵盖168种鸟类。我们以COCO格式的JSON方案发布标注，统一音频文件、分类学类别和机器学习标签。PteroSet不仅提供标注数据，还作为现实基准，突出热带声景的关键特征，如声学共现和不同录音地点的领域偏移。我们提供了一个二元鸟类检测的深度学习基线，展示了PteroSet的实用性及其所面临的挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for annotated acoustic data to train machine learning models for tropical bird monitoring, this study introduces PteroSet, a strongly annotated dataset of Neotropical bird vocalizations collected in Colombia between 2023 and 2025. The dataset includes 563 audio recordings totaling 73.62 hours and 15,372 time-frequency annotations, with 6,702 events labeled to species level across 168 species. Annotations are structured in a COCO-inspired JSON format to support machine learning pipelines. Experimental results show that PteroSet enables a deep learning baseline for binary bird detection, while also revealing challenges such as acoustic co-occurrence and domain shift between recording sites, making it a realistic benchmark for tropical soundscape analysis.
为支持热带鸟类监测的机器学习模型训练，本研究构建了PteroSet数据集，包含2023至2025年间在哥伦比亚采集的563段音频（共73.62小时），标注了15,372个时频点，其中6,702个事件被标注至物种级别（共168种鸟类）。标注采用COCO风格JSON格式，便于机器学习流程使用。实验表明，该数据集可支撑二分类鸟类检测的深度学习基线模型，同时揭示了热带声景中的声学共现与站点间域偏移等挑战，使其成为评估热带声景建模性能的现实基准。
A Short and Unified Convergence Analysis of the SAG, SAGA, and IAG Algorithms
Authors: Feng Zhu, Robert W. Heath, Aritra Mitra
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-02-05T04:57:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:32:29+00:00
Comments: To appear at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic variance-reduced algorithms such as Stochastic Average Gradient (SAG) and SAGA, and their deterministic counterparts like the Incremental Aggregated Gradient (IAG) method, have been extensively studied in large-scale machine learning. Despite their popularity, existing analyses for these algorithms are disparate, relying on different proof techniques tailored to each method. Furthermore, the original proof of SAG is known to be notoriously involved, requiring computer-aided analysis. Focusing on finite-sum optimization with smooth and strongly convex objective functions, our main contribution is to develop a single unified convergence analysis that applies to all three algorithms: SAG, SAGA, and IAG. Our analysis features two key steps: (i) establishing a bound on delays due to stochastic sub-sampling using simple concentration tools, and (ii) carefully designing a novel Lyapunov function that accounts for such delays. The resulting proof is short and modular, providing the first high-probability bounds for SAG and SAGA that can be seamlessly extended to non-convex objectives and Markov sampling. As an immediate byproduct of our new analysis technique, we obtain the best known rates for the IAG algorithm, significantly improving upon prior bounds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAG、SAGA 和 IAG 算法的简洁统一收敛性分析
随机方差缩减算法（如随机平均梯度 SAG 和 SAGA）及其确定性对应方法（如增量聚合梯度 IAG）在大规模机器学习中已被广泛研究。尽管这些算法广受欢迎，但现有分析方法各异，依赖于针对每种方法的特定证明技术。此外，SAG 的原始证明以复杂著称，需借助计算机辅助分析。本文聚焦于光滑且强凸目标函数的有限和优化问题，主要贡献是提出一种统一的收敛性分析，适用于 SAG、SAGA 和 IAG 三种算法。该分析包含两个关键步骤：(i) 使用简单的集中不等式工具建立随机子采样导致延迟的上界；(ii) 精心设计一种新型李雅普诺夫函数，以考虑此类延迟。所得证明简洁且模块化，首次为 SAG 和 SAGA 提供了高概率界，且可无缝扩展至非凸目标和马尔可夫采样。作为新分析技术的直接副产品，我们获得了 IAG 算法目前最优的收敛速率，显著优于先前的界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented and complex existing analyses of SAG, SAGA, and IAG algorithms, this paper presents a unified convergence analysis applicable to all three methods under smooth and strongly convex finite-sum optimization. The method introduces two key innovations: bounding stochastic subsampling delays via concentration inequalities and constructing a novel Lyapunov function that incorporates these delays. The resulting proof is concise and modular, yielding the first high-probability convergence bounds for SAG and SAGA, and enabling seamless extension to non-convex and Markov sampling settings. Experimentally, the new analysis recovers the best-known rates for IAG, substantially improving upon prior theoretical guarantees.
为解决SAG、SAGA和IAG等随机方差缩减算法分析方法分散且复杂的问题，本文在光滑强凸有限和优化框架下，提出统一的收敛性分析框架。该方法通过集中不等式控制随机子采样延迟，并设计一种新型Lyapunov函数以纳入延迟影响。所获证明简洁模块化，首次为SAG和SAGA提供高概率收敛界，且可无缝扩展至非凸与马尔可夫采样场景。作为直接成果，该分析方法为IAG算法提供了当前最优收敛速率，显著优于先前理论界结果。
Optimus: A Robust Defense Framework for Mitigating Toxicity while Fine-Tuning Conversational AI
Authors: Aravind Cheruvu, Shravya Kanchi, Sifat Muhammad Abdullah, Nicholas Ka-Shing Kong, Daphne Yao, Murtuza Jadliwala, Bimal Viswanath
First: 2025-07-08T04:40:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:31:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACM CODASPY 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Customizing Large Language Models (LLMs) on untrusted datasets poses severe risks of injecting toxic behaviors. In this work, we introduce Optimus, a novel defense framework designed to mitigate fine-tuning harms while preserving conversational utility. Unlike existing defenses that rely heavily on precise toxicity detection or restrictive filtering, Optimus addresses the critical challenge of ensuring robust mitigation even when toxicity classifiers are imperfect or biased. Optimus integrates a training-free toxicity classification scheme that repurposes the safety alignment of commodity LLMs, and employs a dual-strategy alignment process combining synthetic "healing data" with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to efficiently steer models toward safety. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Optimus mitigates toxicity even when relying on extremely biased classifiers (with up to 85% degradation in Recall). Optimus outperforms the state-of-the-art defense StarDSS and exhibits strong resilience against adaptive adversarial and jailbreak attacks. Our source code and datasets are available at https://github.com/secml-lab-vt/Optimus
中文标题/摘要
标题：Optimus：一种在微调对话AI时缓解毒性行为的稳健防御框架
在不可信数据集上定制大型语言模型（LLMs）可能严重引入毒性行为。本文提出Optimus，一种旨在缓解微调危害同时保留对话实用性的新型防御框架。与现有依赖精确毒性检测或严格过滤的防御方法不同，Optimus解决了当毒性分类器不完美或存在偏见时仍能稳健缓解的关键挑战。Optimus整合了一种无需训练的毒性分类方案，重用商用LLMs的安全对齐能力，并采用合成“修复数据”与直接偏好优化（DPO）相结合的双策略对齐过程，高效引导模型向安全方向发展。大量评估表明，即使依赖极度偏见的分类器（召回率下降高达85%），Optimus仍能有效缓解毒性。Optimus优于当前最先进的防御方法StarDSS，并对自适应对抗攻击和越狱攻击表现出强鲁棒性。我们的源代码和数据集可在https://github.com/secml-lab-vt/Optimus获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of injecting toxic behaviors when fine-tuning LLMs on untrusted data, Optimus introduces a defense framework that mitigates toxicity without requiring precise toxicity classifiers, which are often biased or imperfect. It employs a training-free toxicity classification method derived from safety-aligned LLMs and combines synthetic healing data with Direct Preference Optimization to align models toward safe responses. Experimental results show Optimus effectively reduces toxicity even when using severely degraded classifiers (up to 85% recall loss), outperforming state-of-the-art defense StarDSS and resisting adaptive adversarial and jailbreak attacks.
为应对在未受信任数据上微调大语言模型时可能引入毒性行为的风险，Optimus 提出了一种无需依赖精确毒性检测器的防御框架。该框架基于商品化 LLM 的安全对齐构建无训练毒性分类机制，并结合合成修复数据与直接偏好优化（DPO）引导模型向安全方向演化。实验表明，即使在使用严重偏倚的分类器（召回率下降高达85%）的情况下，Optimus 仍能有效降低毒性，并优于当前最优方法 StarDSS，在对抗性攻击和越狱攻击中表现出强鲁棒性。
Ray-Aware Pointer Memory with Adaptive Updates for Streaming 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Feifei Li, Qi Song, Chi Zhang, Rui Huang
First: 2026-05-07T06:42:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:29:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dense 3D reconstruction from continuous image streams requires both accurate geometric aggregation and stable long-term memory management. Recent feed-forward reconstruction frameworks integrate observations through persistent memory representations, yet most rely primarily on appearance-based similarity when updating memory. Such appearance-driven integration often leads to redundant accumulation of observations and unstable geometry when viewpoint changes occur. In this work, we propose a ray-aware pointer memory for streaming 3D reconstruction that explicitly models both spatial location and viewing direction within a unified memory representation. Each memory pointer stores its 3D position, associated ray direction, and feature embedding, allowing the system to reason jointly about geometric proximity and viewpoint consistency. Based on this representation, we introduce an adaptive pointer update strategy that replaces traditional fusion-based memory compression with a retain-or-replace mechanism. Instead of averaging nearby observations, the system selectively retains informative pointers while discarding redundant ones, preserving distinctive geometric structures while maintaining bounded memory growth. Furthermore, the joint reasoning over spatial distance and ray-direction discrepancy enables the system to distinguish between local redundancy, novel observations, and potential loop revisits in a unified manner. When loop candidates are detected, pose refinement is triggered to enforce global geometric consistency across the reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed ray-aware memory design significantly improves long-term reconstruction stability and camera pose accuracy while maintaining efficient streaming inference. Our approach provides a principled framework for scalable and drift-resistant online 3D reconstruction from image streams.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向流式3D重建的射线感知指针内存与自适应更新
从连续图像流中进行密集3D重建需要精确的几何聚合与稳定的长期记忆管理。近期的前馈重建框架通过持久化记忆表示整合观测，但多数仅依赖外观相似性更新记忆。此类外观驱动的整合常导致观测冗余累积，且在视角变化时几何结构不稳定。本文提出一种射线感知指针内存，统一建模空间位置与观察方向。每个内存指针存储3D位置、关联射线方向及特征嵌入，使系统可联合推理几何邻近性与视角一致性。基于此表示，我们引入自适应指针更新策略，以保留或替换机制替代传统融合式记忆压缩。系统不再平均邻近观测，而是选择性保留信息性指针并丢弃冗余指针，既保留独特几何结构，又控制内存增长。此外，联合空间距离与射线方向差异推理，使系统统一区分局部冗余、新观测与潜在闭环重访。检测到闭环候选时，触发位姿优化以强制重建全局几何一致性。大量实验表明，所提射线感知内存设计显著提升长期重建稳定性与相机位姿精度，同时保持高效流式推理。本方法为从图像流中实现可扩展且抗漂移的在线3D重建提供原理性框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of 3D reconstruction from continuous image streams due to appearance-based memory updates, this work introduces a ray-aware pointer memory that jointly encodes 3D position and ray direction for each memory entry, enabling geometric and viewpoint-aware reasoning. The method replaces traditional fusion-based memory compression with an adaptive retain-or-replace mechanism that selectively preserves informative pointers while discarding redundant ones, maintaining bounded memory growth and preserving geometric structure. Experimental results show improved long-term stability and camera pose accuracy compared to baseline methods, with the ability to detect loop candidates and trigger pose refinement for global consistency.
为解决流式图像序列重建中因外观驱动记忆更新导致的几何不稳定问题，本文提出一种射线感知的指针记忆机制，每个指针同时编码3D位置与视线方向，支持联合空间距离与视角一致性判断。系统采用自适应更新策略，按需保留或替换指针以避免冗余积累，保持几何结构完整性并控制内存增长。实验表明，该方法显著提升长期重建稳定性与相机位姿精度，同时具备有效的闭环检测能力。
The efficiency-gain illusion: People underestimate the rate of AI use and overestimate its benefits on simple tasks
Authors: Sunny Yu, Myra Cheng, Ahmad Jabbar, Ilia Sucholutsky, Katherine M. Collins, Dan Jurafsky, Robert D. Hawkins
First: 2026-05-21T16:28:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:28:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
People are increasingly turning to AI assistance for simple tasks, e.g., arithmetic, spell-check, and answering simple questions. But does AI assistance actually save users time and effort? We investigate people's propensity to use AI for cognitively simple tasks and assess whether their reliance is well-calibrated. Across three pre-registered user studies (N = 2691), we find that people frequently choose to use AI even when doing so is inefficient (i.e. provides no meaningful time or effort savings). We identify systematic miscalibration at two levels: (1) a self-estimate miscalibration where people on average believe that they are using AI less than they actually are, and (2) efficiency-gain illusions where people overestimate how much time and effort savings AI use affords. We also identify a session-level carryover effect where a participant's prior AI use leads to further AI adoption and entrenches their miscalibration about time savings. Our results shed light on the mechanisms and biases underlying people's choice of whether to use AI as well as the risk of an overreliance feedback loop.
中文标题/摘要
标题：效率增益的错觉：人们低估了AI的使用率，高估了其在简单任务中的益处
人们越来越多地寻求AI协助完成简单任务，例如算术、拼写检查和回答简单问题。但AI协助是否真的节省用户的时间和精力？我们研究了人们在认知简单任务中使用AI的倾向，并评估其依赖程度是否准确。在三项预注册用户研究（N=2691）中，我们发现人们经常选择使用AI，即使这样做并不高效（即未能带来有意义的时间或精力节省）。我们识别出两个层面的系统性误校准：（1）自我估计误校准，即人们平均认为自己使用AI的频率低于实际频率；（2）效率增益错觉，即人们高估了AI使用所能带来的时间和精力节省。我们还发现了一个会话级的延续效应：参与者先前的AI使用会进一步促进AI采纳，并巩固其对时间节省的误判。我们的研究揭示了人们选择是否使用AI背后的机制和偏见，以及过度依赖的反馈循环风险。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing public use of AI for simple tasks like arithmetic or spell-check, this study investigates whether users accurately perceive AI’s efficiency gains. Through three pre-registered experiments with 2,691 participants, researchers found that users frequently chose AI even when it offered no real time or effort savings, revealing two key miscalibrations: users underestimated their own AI usage and overestimated the efficiency gains AI provided. Additionally, prior AI use in a session led to increased future adoption, reinforcing these misperceptions and creating a feedback loop that risks overreliance.
鉴于公众日益频繁地使用AI完成简单任务（如算术或拼写检查），本研究旨在考察用户是否准确感知AI的效率优势。通过对2691名参与者进行三项预注册实验，研究发现用户常选择使用AI，即使它并未带来实际的时间或精力节省，揭示了两种认知偏差：用户低估自身AI使用频率，同时高估AI带来的效率收益。此外，用户在前一阶段使用AI会促使其后续更频繁使用AI，从而强化效率幻觉并形成依赖反馈循环。
ChronoVAE-HOPE: Beyond Attention -- A Next-Generation VAE Foundation Model for Specialized Time Series Classification
Authors: José Alberto Rodríguez, Luis Balderas, Miguel Lastra, Antonio Arauzo-Azofra, José M. Benítez
First: 2026-05-21T16:26:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:26:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have become a new component of the state-of-the-art in general time series forecasting. However, adapting them to specialized classification tasks remains constrained by two interconnected challenges: the quadratic cost of standard attention mechanisms and the inability to disentangle the structural components underlying time series variability. This technical report introduces ChronoVAE-HOPE, a next-generation TSFM that reconciles massive generalization with structured latent representation for time series classification. The core of the proposal is a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) framework built upon the HOPE Block, which replaces quadratic attention with a dual-memory system: Titans modules for dynamic short-term retention and a Continuum Memory System (CMS) for the abstraction of long-term historical context. A key architectural novelty is the disentangled latent space, which factorizes representations into independent trend and seasonal components via dedicated encoder heads and separate decoder pathways. ChronoVAE-HOPE undergoes self-supervised pre-training on the Monash archive, combining a Masked Time Series Modeling (MTSM) auxiliary objective with a disentangled VAE reconstruction loss. The pre-trained encoder is subsequently frozen and used to generate fixed-length embeddings for downstream classification on the UCR benchmark datasets. Empirical results demonstrate strong performance across diverse temporal domains, particularly in settings characterized by strict causal structure. ChronoVAE-HOPE establishes a robust and interpretable framework for the adaptation of foundation models to time series classification through structured generative representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ChronoVAE-HOPE：超越注意力机制——面向专用时间序列分类的下一代VAE基础模型
时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）已成为通用时间序列预测领域的最新组成部分。然而，将其适配至专用分类任务仍受限于两个相互关联的挑战：标准注意力机制的二次方成本，以及无法解耦时间序列变异性背后的结构成分。本技术报告介绍了ChronoVAE-HOPE，一种下一代TSFM，它在实现大规模泛化的同时，为时间序列分类提供结构化的潜在表示。该方案的核心是一个基于HOPE模块的变分自编码器（VAE）框架，用双记忆系统替代二次方注意力机制：Titan模块用于动态短期记忆保留，连续记忆系统（CMS）用于抽象长期历史上下文。关键架构创新是解耦的潜在空间，通过专用编码器头和独立解码路径，将表示分解为独立的趋势和季节性成分。ChronoVAE-HOPE在Monash数据集上进行自监督预训练，结合掩码时间序列建模（MTSM）辅助目标与解耦VAE重建损失。预训练编码器随后被冻结，用于在UCR基准数据集上生成固定长度嵌入以支持下游分类。实证结果表明，该模型在多种时序领域表现优异，尤其在具有严格因果结构的场景中表现突出。ChronoVAE-HOPE建立了一种稳健且可解释的框架，通过结构化生成表示将基础模型适配至时间序列分类任务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of attention mechanisms and the lack of structural disentanglement in time series classification, ChronoVAE-HOPE introduces a VAE-based foundation model using the HOPE Block to replace quadratic attention with a dual-memory system for efficient short- and long-term retention. Its key innovation lies in a disentangled latent space that separates trend and seasonal components via dedicated encoder heads and decoder pathways. Pre-trained on the Monash archive using Masked Time Series Modeling and VAE reconstruction, the model generates fixed-length embeddings for downstream classification on UCR datasets, achieving strong performance across diverse temporal domains, especially under strict causal conditions.
为解决时间序列分类中注意力机制效率低和结构不可分离的问题，ChronoVAE-HOPE提出一种基于VAE的下一代基础模型，采用HOPE模块以双记忆系统替代二次注意力，实现短时动态保留与长时历史抽象。其架构通过独立编码器头与解码路径分离趋势与季节性成分，构建可解耦的潜在空间。模型在Monash数据集上通过掩码建模与VAE重构损失自监督预训练，生成固定长度嵌入用于UCR基准分类任务，在多种时序领域表现优异，尤其在需严格因果结构的任务中效果突出。
UniSD: Towards a Unified Self-Distillation Framework for Large Language Models
Authors: Yiqiao Jin, Yiyang Wang, Lucheng Fu, Yijia Xiao, Yinyi Luo, Haoxin Liu, B. Aditya Prakash, Josiah Hester, Jindong Wang, Srijan Kumar
First: 2026-05-07T17:22:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:25:55+00:00
Comments: Website: https://unifiedsd.github.io/ Code: https://github.com/Ahren09/UniSD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Self-distillation (SD) offers a promising path for adapting large language models (LLMs) without relying on stronger external teachers. However, SD in autoregressive LLMs remains challenging because self-generated trajectories are free-form, correctness is task-dependent, and plausible rationales can still provide unstable or unreliable supervision. Existing methods mainly examine isolated design choices, leaving their effectiveness, roles, and interactions unclear. In this paper, we propose UniSD, a unified framework to systematically study self-distillation. UniSD integrates complementary mechanisms that address supervision reliability, representation alignment, and training stability, including multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping. Across six benchmarks and six models from three model families, UniSD reveals when self-distillation improves over static imitation, which components drive the gains, and how these components interact across tasks. Guided by these insights, we construct UniSDfull, an integrated pipeline that combines complementary components and achieves the strongest overall performance, improving over the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points. Extensive evaluation highlights self-distillation as a practical and steerable approach for efficient LLM adaptation without stronger external teachers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniSD：面向大语言模型的统一自蒸馏框架
自蒸馏（SD）为无需依赖更强外部教师的大语言模型（LLMs）适配提供了有前景的路径。然而，在自回归LLMs中，SD仍具挑战性，因为自生成轨迹自由形式、正确性依赖任务，且合理推理仍可能提供不稳定或不可靠的监督。现有方法主要考察孤立的设计选择，其有效性、角色及交互关系尚不明确。本文提出UniSD，一个统一框架以系统研究自蒸馏。UniSD整合互补机制，解决监督可靠性、表征对齐与训练稳定性问题，包括多教师一致性、EMA教师稳定化、token级对比学习、特征匹配与发散裁剪。在六项基准与三类模型家族的六种模型上，UniSD揭示了自蒸馏何时优于静态模仿、哪些组件驱动性能提升、以及这些组件在不同任务中的交互方式。基于这些洞见，我们构建UniSDfull，一个整合互补组件的流水线，实现最强整体性能，相较基线模型提升+5.4分，相较最强基线提升+2.8分。广泛评估表明，自蒸馏是无需更强外部教师的高效LLM适配实用且可控方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt large language models without external teachers, this paper introduces UniSD, a unified self-distillation framework that systematically addresses key challenges in autoregressive LLMs, including unreliable self-generated supervision and training instability. UniSD integrates multiple complementary mechanisms—multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping—to enhance supervision reliability, representation alignment, and training stability. Evaluated across six benchmarks and six models from three families, UniSD identifies when self-distillation outperforms static imitation, which components drive performance gains, and how they interact across tasks. The resulting UniSDfull pipeline, combining all effective components, achieves the best overall performance, improving the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points, demonstrating self-distillation as a practical and steerable adaptation method.
为实现无需外部教师的大语言模型高效适配，本文提出UniSD统一自蒸馏框架，系统整合多教师一致性、EMA教师稳定化、词元级对比学习、特征匹配与发散裁剪等机制，以解决监督信号不可靠、表征对齐偏差及训练不稳定等问题。在六个基准和三类模型的六种模型上评估，UniSD揭示了自蒸馏优于静态模仿的场景、各组件的增益来源及其跨任务交互关系。基于这些发现构建的UniSDfull集成方案，综合所有有效组件，相较基线模型提升5.4分，相较最强基线提升2.8分，验证了自蒸馏作为实用且可控的无外部教师适配方法的有效性。
Orchard: An Open-Source Agentic Modeling Framework
Authors: Baolin Peng, Wenlin Yao, Qianhui Wu, Hao Cheng, Xiao Yu, Rui Yang, Tao Ge, Alessandro Sordoni, Xingdi Yuan, Yelong Shen, Pengcheng He, Tong Zhang, Zhou Yu, Jianfeng Gao
First: 2026-05-14T16:35:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:25:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic modeling aims to transform LLMs into autonomous agents capable of solving complex tasks through planning, reasoning, tool use, and multi-turn interaction with environments. Despite major investment, open research remains constrained by infrastructure and training gaps. Many high-performing systems rely on proprietary codebases, models, or services, while most open-source frameworks focus on orchestration and evaluation rather than scalable agent training. We present Orchard, an open-source framework for scalable agentic modeling. At its core is Orchard Env, a lightweight environment service providing reusable primitives for sandbox lifecycle management across task domains, agent harnesses, and pipeline stages. On top of Orchard Env, we build three agentic modeling recipes. Orchard-SWE targets coding agents. We distill 107K trajectories from MiniMax-M2.5 and Qwen3.5-397B, introduce credit-assignment SFT to learn from productive segments of unresolved trajectories, and apply Balanced Adaptive Rollout for RL. Starting from Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking, Orchard-SWE achieves 64.3% on SWE-bench Verified after SFT and 67.5% after SFT+RL, setting a new state of the art among open-source models of comparable size. Orchard-GUI trains a 4B vision-language computer-use agent using only 0.4K distilled trajectories and 2.2K open-ended tasks. It achieves 74.1%, 67.0%, and 64.0% success rates on WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and DeepShop, respectively, making it the strongest open-source model while remaining competitive with proprietary systems. Orchard-Claw targets personal assistant agents. Trained with only 0.2K synthetic tasks, it achieves 59.6% pass@3 on Claw-Eval and 73.9% when paired with a stronger ZeroClaw harness. Collectively, these results show that a lightweight, open, harness-agnostic environment layer enables reusable agentic data, training recipes, and evaluations across domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Orchard：一个开源的代理建模框架
代理建模旨在将大语言模型转化为能够通过规划、推理、工具使用和与环境的多轮交互来解决复杂任务的自主代理。尽管投入巨大，开源研究仍受限于基础设施和训练方面的差距。许多高性能系统依赖专有代码库、模型或服务，而大多数开源框架侧重于编排和评估，而非可扩展的代理训练。我们提出了Orchard，一个用于可扩展代理建模的开源框架。其核心是Orchard Env，一个轻量级环境服务，提供跨任务领域、代理框架和流水线阶段的可复用沙箱生命周期管理原语。在Orchard Env之上，我们构建了三个代理建模方案：Orchard-SWE针对编码代理，从MiniMax-M2.5和Qwen3.5-397B中蒸馏10.7万个轨迹，引入信用分配监督微调以学习未解决轨迹中的有效片段，并应用平衡自适应展开进行强化学习。从Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking开始，Orchard-SWE在SWE-bench Verified上SFT后达到64.3%，SFT+RL后达到67.5%，在同等规模的开源模型中创下新纪录。Orchard-GUI仅用0.4K蒸馏轨迹和2.2K开放式任务训练了一个4B视觉-语言计算机使用代理，在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web和DeepShop上分别取得74.1%、67.0%和64.0%的成功率，成为最强的开源模型，同时与专有系统保持竞争力。Orchard-Claw针对个人助理代理，仅用0.2K合成任务训练，其在Claw-Eval上达到59.6%的pass@3，在与更强的ZeroClaw框架配合时达到73.9%。总体而言，这些结果表明，一个轻量、开源、框架无关的环境层可实现跨领域的可复用代理数据、训练方案和评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of open-source infrastructure for scalable agentic modeling, the authors introduce Orchard, an open-source framework enabling training of autonomous agents through planning, tool use, and multi-turn interaction. At its core is Orchard Env, a lightweight environment service managing sandbox lifecycles across domains and training stages. Three agent-specific recipes are built on top: Orchard-SWE trains a coding agent using distilled trajectories and credit-assignment SFT plus RL, achieving 67.5% on SWE-bench Verified; Orchard-GUI trains a vision-language agent with minimal data, reaching 74.1% on WebVoyager; and Orchard-Claw trains a personal assistant agent with synthetic tasks, scoring 59.6% on Claw-Eval. These results demonstrate that a lightweight, open, harness-agnostic environment enables reusable training pipelines and competitive performance across domains.
为解决开源领域缺乏可扩展的自主代理建模基础设施的问题，作者提出了Orchard开源框架，支持通过规划、推理和工具使用训练自主代理。其核心是Orchard Env，一个轻量级环境服务，用于跨领域管理沙箱生命周期。框架构建了三个代理专用训练方案：Orchard-SWE基于蒸馏轨迹与信用分配SFT+强化学习，使Qwen3-30B模型在SWE-bench上达到67.5%；Orchard-GUI仅用少量数据训练视觉语言代理，在WebVoyager上达到74.1%；Orchard-Claw仅用合成任务训练个人助理代理，在Claw-Eval上达到59.6%。结果表明，轻量、开放、与代理框架无关的环境层可支持跨领域可复用的代理数据、训练方案与评估。
Hyperparameter Transfer with Mixture-of-Expert Layers
Authors: Tianze Jiang, Blake Bordelon, Cengiz Pehlevan, Boris Hanin
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-28T03:02:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:25:08+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers have emerged as an important tool in scaling up modern neural networks by decoupling total trainable parameters from activated parameters in the forward pass for each token. However, sparse MoEs add complexity to training due to (i) new trainable parameters (router weights) that, like all other parameter groups, require hyperparameter (HP) tuning; (ii) new architecture scale dimensions (number of and size of experts) that must be chosen and potentially taken large. To make HP selection cheap and reliable, we propose a new parameterization for transformer models with MoE layers when scaling model width, depth, number of experts, and expert (hidden) size. Our parameterization is justified by a novel dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) analysis. When varying different model dimensions trained at a fixed token budget, we find empirically that our parameterization enables reliable HP transfer across models from 51M to over 2B total parameters. We further take HPs identified from sweeping small models on a short token horizon to train larger models on longer horizons and report performant model behaviors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用专家混合层进行超参数迁移
专家混合（MoE）层已成为通过将前向传递中激活的参数与总可训练参数解耦来扩展现代神经网络的重要工具。然而，稀疏MoE增加了训练复杂性：(i) 新的可训练参数（路由权重），与其他参数组一样，需要超参数（HP）调优；(ii) 新的架构规模维度（专家数量和大小），必须选择并可能取较大值。为使超参数选择廉价且可靠，我们提出了一种在扩展模型宽度、深度、专家数量和专家（隐藏）大小时，用于Transformer模型的新型参数化方法。该参数化方法由新颖的动力学平均场理论（DMFT）分析所支持。在固定token预算下，通过改变不同模型维度进行实验，我们发现该参数化方法能够在51M至超过2B总参数的模型间实现可靠的超参数迁移。我们进一步利用在短token时间范围内对小型模型进行超参数扫描所确定的超参数，训练在长token时间范围内的大型模型，并报告了其表现优异的模型行为。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complexity of hyperparameter tuning in sparse Mixture-of-Experts layers, this work proposes a novel parameterization for transformer models that scales with model width, depth, expert count, and expert size. The method is grounded in a dynamical mean-field theory analysis and enables reliable hyperparameter transfer across models ranging from 51M to over 2B parameters. Experimental results show that hyperparameters optimized on small models with short token budgets can effectively train larger models on longer horizons without performance degradation.
为解决稀疏Mixture-of-Experts层训练中超参数调优复杂的问题，本文提出一种基于动力学平均场理论的新参数化方法，支持模型宽度、深度、专家数量及专家隐藏层大小的扩展。实验表明，该方法可在51M至20亿参数规模的模型间实现可靠的超参数迁移，且在短序列预算下优化的超参数可有效应用于长序列大模型，保持高性能无需重新调优。
Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence
Authors: Sean Wu, Pan Lu, Yupeng Chen, Jonathan Bragg, Yutaro Yamada, Peter Clark, David Clifton, Philip Torr, James Zou, Junchi Yu
First: 2026-05-21T16:23:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:23:36+00:00
Comments: 73 pages, 13 figures, 29 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用人工智能预测科学进步
人工智能（AI）正日益融入科学发现，但其能否预测科学进步仍不明确。为研究此问题，我们引入了一个基于时间的评估框架，用于在受控知识约束下预测科学进步。我们提出了CUSP（截止条件下的未见科学进步），这是一个跨学科、事件级基准，通过可行性评估、机制推理、生成性解决方案设计和时间预测来评估AI系统中的科学预测能力。在4760个科学事件中，我们观察到当前前沿模型存在系统性且领域依赖的局限性。尽管模型能从竞争性候选方案中识别出合理的研究方向，但无法可靠预测科学进展是否实现，且系统性地误判其发生时间。不同领域间表现差异显著，AI进展的时间预测比生物学、化学和物理学的进展更可预测。模型表现对事件是否发生在训练截止前或后基本无敏感性，表明这些局限性不能仅由训练数据中的知识暴露解释。在受控信息访问条件下，额外的截止前知识可提升表现，但无法弥补与全信息设置之间的差距，且对高引用进展的差距更为明显。模型还表现出系统性过度自信和强烈响应偏差，表明其不确定性估计不可靠。综上，当前AI系统作为科学进步预测工具尚显不足。获取先前知识并不能转化为可靠的预测能力，而表现更多受益于事后信息而非前瞻预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing role of AI in scientific discovery, this study investigates whether AI can reliably forecast scientific progress. It introduces CUSP, a benchmark evaluating AI systems across four dimensions—feasibility, reasoning, solution design, and timing—under controlled knowledge constraints. Across 4,760 scientific events, the study finds that while models can identify plausible research directions, they fail to reliably predict whether advances will materialize or when they will occur, with performance varying significantly by domain (e.g., AI is more predictable than biology or physics). Performance does not improve substantially with access to pre-cutoff knowledge, and models exhibit overconfidence and response biases, suggesting unreliable uncertainty estimates. Overall, current AI systems are inadequate as forecasting tools for scientific progress.
鉴于人工智能在科学发现中的日益重要性，本研究旨在探究AI是否能可靠预测科学进展。通过构建一个基于时间的评估框架和CUSP基准测试（涵盖可行性、机制推理、方案生成与时间预测四个维度），研究人员分析了4760个科学事件。结果表明，尽管模型能识别出合理的研究方向，但无法可靠预测科学突破是否实现或何时发生，且不同学科表现差异显著——AI进展更易预测，而生物学、化学和物理学则较难。模型表现与事件是否发生在训练截止前或后关系不大，说明其局限性超出训练数据知识范围。即使提供截止前额外知识，性能提升有限，尤其在高引用突破上差距更明显。模型还表现出过度自信和响应偏差，削弱了其作为预测工具的可靠性。
Conceptualizing Embeddings: Sparse Disentanglement for Vision-Language Models
Authors: Piotr Kubaty, Patryk Marszałek, Łukasz Struski, Adam Wróbel, Jacek Tabor, Marek Śmieja
First: 2026-05-21T16:23:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:23:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models learn powerful multimodal embeddings, yet their internal semantics remain opaque. While sparse autoencoders (SAEs) can extract interpretable features, they rely on expanding the representation dimension, which compromises the original geometry and introduces redundancy. We introduce CEDAR (Conceptual Embedding Disentanglement via Adaptive Rotation), a post-hoc method that reveals the compositional structure of pretrained embeddings without increasing dimensionality. By learning an invertible transformation with a top-$k$ sparsity bottleneck, CEDAR concentrates semantic information into axis-aligned disentangled coordinates. In CLIP-like architecture, individual coordinates can be interpreted with textual concepts, while for generative models such as BLIP, they can be decoded into natural language descriptions. Experiments demonstrate that CEDAR achieves a competitive reconstruction-sparsity trade-off while producing explanations that are more interpretable and better aligned with human perception. Our results suggest that the apparent entanglement in vision-language representations can be resolved through a suitable change of basis, eliminating the need for overcomplete expansions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：嵌入的概念化：视觉-语言模型的稀疏解耦
视觉-语言模型学习强大的多模态嵌入，但其内部语义仍不透明。虽然稀疏自动编码器（SAEs）可提取可解释特征，但它们依赖于扩展表示维度，这会破坏原始几何结构并引入冗余。我们提出CEDAR（通过自适应旋转实现概念嵌入解耦），一种后处理方法，可在不增加维度的情况下揭示预训练嵌入的组合结构。通过学习一个具有top-k稀疏瓶颈的可逆变换，CEDAR将语义信息集中到轴对齐的解耦坐标中。在CLIP类架构中，单个坐标可与文本概念对应；对于BLIP等生成模型，可解码为自然语言描述。实验表明，CEDAR在重建与稀疏性之间取得良好平衡，生成的解释更易理解且更符合人类感知。我们的结果表明，视觉-语言表示中的表观纠缠可通过合适的基变换解决，无需过度扩展。
Summary / 总结
Vision-language models generate powerful multimodal embeddings but lack interpretable internal semantics. To address this, the authors propose CEDAR, a post-hoc method that disentangles these embeddings via an invertible transformation with a top-k sparsity constraint, preserving original dimensionality while aligning features with textual concepts or natural language. Experiments show CEDAR achieves competitive reconstruction quality while significantly improving interpretability and human alignment without requiring overcomplete representations.
为了解释视觉-语言模型中不可见的语义结构，同时不增加维度，作者提出了CEDAR方法，通过可逆变换与top-k稀疏约束对嵌入空间进行旋转，使语义维度沿正交轴对齐，从而实现可解释的分解——在CLIP类模型中对应文本概念，在BLIP等生成模型中可解码为自然语言描述。实验表明，CEDAR在重建精度与稀疏性之间取得良好平衡，其解释结果比现有方法更符合人类感知。
Towards Open World Sound Event Detection
Authors: P. H. Hai, L. T. Minh, L. H. Son
Venue: Signal Processing, Article 110707, 2026
First: 2026-05-05T16:23:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:23:09+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 3 figures. Accepted to Signal Processing (Elsevier)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sound Event Detection (SED) plays a vital role in audio understanding, with applications in surveillance, smart cities, healthcare, and multimedia indexing. However, conventional SED systems operate under a closed-world assumption, limiting their effectiveness in real-world environments where novel acoustic events frequently emerge. Inspired by the success of open-world learning in computer vision, we introduce the Open-World Sound Event Detection (OW-SED) paradigm, where models must detect known events, identify unseen ones, and incrementally learn from them. To tackle the unique challenges of OW-SED, such as overlapping and ambiguous events, we propose a 1D Deformable architecture that leverages deformable attention to adaptively focus on salient temporal regions. Furthermore, we design a novel Open-World Deformable Sound Event Detection Transformer (WOOT) framework incorporating feature disentanglement to separate class-specific and class-agnostic representations, together with a one-to-many matching strategy and a diversity loss to enhance representation diversity. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves marginally superior performance compared to existing leading techniques in closed-world settings and significantly improves over existing baselines in open-world scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向开放世界的声事件检测
声事件检测（SED）在音频理解中扮演关键角色，应用于监控、智慧城市、医疗和多媒体索引等领域。然而，传统SED系统基于封闭世界假设，难以应对现实环境中频繁出现的新声学事件。受计算机视觉中开放世界学习成功启发，我们提出开放世界声事件检测（OW-SED）范式，要求模型检测已知事件、识别未知事件并从中增量学习。为应对OW-SED的特殊挑战（如事件重叠与模糊性），我们提出一种1D可变形架构，利用可变形注意力自适应聚焦显著时域区域。此外，我们设计了新型开放世界可变形声事件检测Transformer（WOOT）框架，结合特征解耦分离类特定与类无关表征，辅以一对多匹配策略和多样性损失以增强表征多样性。实验表明，我们的方法在封闭世界场景中略优于现有领先方法，在开放世界场景中显著超越现有基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of closed-world sound event detection in real-world environments where novel acoustic events frequently appear, this work introduces Open-World Sound Event Detection (OW-SED), inspired by open-world learning in computer vision. The method employs a 1D Deformable architecture with deformable attention to focus on salient temporal regions and proposes the WOOT framework, which includes feature disentanglement, one-to-many matching, and a diversity loss to improve representation separation and generalization. Experiments show that the model performs marginally better than state-of-the-art closed-world methods and significantly outperforms existing baselines in open-world settings.
为应对传统闭世界声事件检测在现实场景中面对新声事件时效果受限的问题，本文提出开放世界声事件检测（OW-SED）范式，借鉴视觉领域开放世界学习思路。所提出的WOOT框架采用1D可变形架构，利用可变形注意力聚焦关键时域区域，并通过特征解耦分离类特定与类无关表征，结合一对多匹配策略与多样性损失提升表征鲁棒性。实验表明，WOOT在开放世界场景下显著优于现有基线方法，在闭世界场景下也取得微幅性能提升。
HumanSplatHMR: Closing the Loop Between Human Mesh Recovery and Gaussian Splatting Avatar
Authors: Yeheng Zong, Pou-Chun Kung, Yike Pan, Seth Isaacson, Yizhou Chen, Ram Vasudevan, Katherine A. Skinner
First: 2026-05-04T16:26:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:22:38+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://scottyehengz.github.io/HumanSplat/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Accurately recovering human pose and appearance from video is an essential component of scene reconstruction, with applications to motion capture, motion prediction, virtual reality, and digital twinning. Despite significant interest in building realistic human avatars from video, this paper demonstrates that existing methods do not accurately recover the 3D geometry of humans. ViT-based approaches are not consistently reliable and can overfit to 2D views, while NeRF- and Gaussian Splatting-based avatars treat pose and appearance separately, limiting rendering generalization to new poses. To resolve these shortcomings, this paper proposes HumanSplatHMR, a joint optimization framework that refines 3D human poses while simultaneously learning a high-fidelity avatar for novel-view and novel-pose synthesis. Our key insight is to close the loop between geometric pose estimation and differentiable rendering. Unlike prior human avatar methods that rely on accurate human pose obtained through motion capture systems or offline refinement, which are impractical in in-the-wild scenarios, our approach uses only human mesh estimates from a state-of-the-art human pose estimator to better reflect real-world conditions. Therefore, instead of using the human pose only as a deformation prior, HumanSplatHMR backpropagates photometric, segmentation, and depth losses through a differentiable renderer to the pose parameters and global position. This coupling refines the global 3D pose over time, improving accuracy and alignment while producing better renderings from novel views. Experiments show consistent improvements over pose recovery baselines that omit image-level refinement and avatar baselines that decouple pose estimation from avatar reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HumanSplatHMR：闭合人体网格恢复与高斯点渲染人像之间的循环
从视频中准确恢复人体姿态与外观是场景重建的关键组成部分，应用于动作捕捉、动作预测、虚拟现实和数字孪生等领域。尽管已有大量研究致力于从视频构建逼真的人体虚拟形象，本文证明现有方法无法准确恢复人体的3D几何结构。基于ViT的方法不够稳定且易对2D视角过拟合，而基于NeRF和高斯点渲染的人像方法将姿态与外观分离，限制了对新姿态的渲染泛化能力。为解决这些问题，本文提出HumanSplatHMR，一种联合优化框架，同时优化3D人体姿态并学习高保真虚拟形象，以支持新视角和新姿态合成。我们的核心思想是闭合几何姿态估计与可微渲染之间的循环。与依赖运动捕捉系统或离线优化的现有方法不同，我们的方法仅使用当前最先进的姿态估计器输出的人体网格，更贴近真实场景。因此，HumanSplatHMR不仅将人体姿态作为形变先验，还通过可微渲染器将光度、分割和深度损失反向传播至姿态参数和全局位置，这种耦合机制随时间优化全局3D姿态，提升精度与对齐效果，同时生成更优的新视角渲染结果。实验表明，该方法在忽略图像级优化的姿态恢复基线和分离姿态估计与虚拟形象重建的虚拟形象基线上均取得持续改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate 3D human geometry recovery from video for applications like motion capture and virtual reality, this paper addresses limitations in existing methods that either overfit to 2D views or decouple pose and appearance. HumanSplatHMR introduces a joint optimization framework that simultaneously refines 3D human poses and learns a high-fidelity avatar, closing the loop between pose estimation and differentiable rendering. Instead of relying on motion capture or offline refinement, it uses only mesh estimates from a pose estimator and backpropagates photometric, segmentation, and depth losses to refine pose parameters over time. Experimental results show consistent improvements over baselines that lack image-level refinement or separate pose estimation from avatar reconstruction.
为解决从视频中准确恢复人体3D几何结构以支持动作捕捉和虚拟现实等应用的问题，本文针对现有方法易过拟合2D视角或割裂姿态与外观重建的缺陷，提出HumanSplatHMR联合优化框架。该方法通过在姿态估计与可微渲染间建立闭环，仅依赖姿态估计器输出的网格估计，将光度、分割与深度损失反向传播至姿态参数与全局位置，实现动态姿态优化。实验表明，相比忽略图像级优化或割裂姿态与重建的基线方法，该框架显著提升姿态精度与新视角渲染质量。
RAGCap-Bench: Benchmarking Capabilities of LLMs in Agentic Retrieval Augmented Generation Systems
Authors: Jingru Lin, Chen Zhang, Stephen Y. Liu, Haizhou Li
First: 2025-10-15T04:13:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:22:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mitigates key limitations of Large Language Models (LLMs)-such as factual errors, outdated knowledge, and hallucinations-by dynamically retrieving external information. Recent work extends this paradigm through agentic RAG systems, where LLMs act as agents to iteratively plan, retrieve, and reason over complex queries. However, these systems still struggle with challenging multi-hop questions, and their intermediate reasoning capabilities remain underexplored. To address this, we propose RAGCap-Bench, a capability-oriented benchmark for fine-grained evaluation of intermediate tasks in agentic RAG workflows. We analyze outputs from state-of-the-art systems to identify common tasks and the core capabilities required for their execution, then construct a taxonomy of typical LLM errors to design targeted evaluation questions. Experiments show that "slow-thinking" models with stronger RAGCap performance achieve better end-to-end results, underscoring the benchmark's validity and the importance of enhancing these intermediate capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAGCap-Bench：评估代理式检索增强生成系统中大语言模型能力的基准
检索增强生成（RAG）通过动态检索外部信息缓解了大语言模型（LLM）的关键局限，如事实错误、知识过时和幻觉。近期研究通过代理式RAG系统扩展了这一范式，其中LLM作为代理迭代规划、检索并推理复杂查询。然而，这些系统在处理具有挑战性的多跳问题时仍存在困难，其中间推理能力尚未充分探索。为此，我们提出了RAGCap-Bench，一个面向能力的基准，用于细粒度评估代理式RAG工作流中的中间任务。我们分析了当前最先进系统的输出，识别出常见任务及其执行所需的核心能力，并构建了典型LLM错误的分类体系，以设计针对性的评估问题。实验表明，具备更强RAG能力的“慢思考”模型在端到端结果上表现更优，凸显了该基准的有效性及提升中间能力的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate intermediate reasoning capabilities in agentic RAG systems, this work introduces RAGCap-Bench, a benchmark designed to assess fine-grained skills such as planning, retrieval, and multi-hop reasoning. The method involves analyzing outputs from state-of-the-art systems to derive a taxonomy of core tasks and common LLM errors, then constructing targeted evaluation questions. Experimental results demonstrate that models exhibiting slower, more deliberate reasoning—referred to as 'slow-thinking'—achieve superior end-to-end performance, validating the benchmark's utility and highlighting the critical role of intermediate capabilities in agentic RAG.
为评估代理式RAG系统中的中间推理能力，本研究提出RAGCap-Bench基准，用于细粒度评估规划、检索与多跳推理等核心能力。方法通过分析当前顶尖系统输出，归纳典型任务与常见LLM错误，进而设计针对性评测题。实验表明，表现出“慢思考”特征的模型在中间任务上表现更优，最终端结果也更佳，验证了该基准的有效性，并强调提升中间能力的重要性。
